### 2026-07-03 — Faction payroll starved payable lower ranks: an unaffordable high-rank stipend `break`s the loop instead of skipping — *drop faction-payroll-break-continue*
Sanctioned follow-up to the `faction-armory-save-character` bug-hunt (which deferred this as the best remaining bounded, in-lane target). `engine/organizations.py::faction_payroll_tick` pays faction members `ORDER BY rank_level DESC` (`db.get_org_members`: highest rank / biggest stipend first). The treasury-depletion guard `if treasury - org_paid < stipend:` did `break` — so the moment ONE high-rank member's stipend outran the remaining treasury, **every remaining lower-rank member was cut off**, even though their smaller stipends the treasury still easily covered. Concrete repro: a faction with treasury 120, a rank-5 member (stipend 500) and a rank-1 member (stipend 50) — the rank-5 member trips the guard, `break` fires, and the rank-1 member's 50cr stipend (which the 120cr treasury trivially covers) is never paid. **Fix:** `break` → `continue`. Skip the member you can't afford and keep paying the ones you can, in the existing rank-descending order. The `treasury - org_paid < stipend` guard still bounds `org_paid` to the treasury on every iteration, so the change **never overdraws** — total disbursement stays ≤ treasury; it only stops starving affordable lower ranks (balance-conservative — it pays *more* members, but never more credits than the treasury already holds). The funnels are already correct and untouched: the stipend faucet routes through `adjust_credits(..., "org_stipend")` and the treasury sink through `adjust_org_treasury(org_id, -org_paid)`; the PG2.PL.A insurance-debt intercept path is unchanged. Live/reachable: `faction_payroll_tick` is registered on the tick scheduler (`server/game_server.py:553`, `interval=86400` ≈ one game-day) via `server/tick_handlers_economy.py`. No schema change, no new faucet/sink, era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched. New `tests/test_faction_payroll_break_continue_2026_07_03.py` (4): the core regression (an affordable low-rank member IS paid despite an unaffordable higher rank — 0 pre-fix, `break`); never-overdraws (three ranks 500/100/50 vs treasury 120 → only the rank-3 100cr stipend packs in, the 50cr tail no longer fits, total 100 ≤ 120, treasury debited exactly 100); no-regression (treasury 1000 pays every member in full, 550 total); and a source guard that the depletion branch is a `continue`, not the old starving `break`. Targeted gate green: new file (4) + `test_pg2_pl_post_launch` (12, the existing stipend/intercept coverage — no regression) + hygiene + foundation smoke.

### 2026-07-03 — Housing data-loss fix: the 2026-07-02 lot-occupancy fix was incomplete (evict/foreclosure still leaked lots); `sell_shopfront` could destroy the wrong shop — *drop housing-teardown-completeness*
A break-it pass on the 2026-07-02 housing-hardening drop's occupancy fix found it was incomplete, plus a second, independent bug. **Bug #1:** `engine.housing.checkout_room`'s lot-occupancy decrement only fired for `housing_type == "rented_room"` — but `checkout_room(force=True)` is also the shared teardown primitive for `@housing evict` (`parser/housing_commands.py::AdminHousingCommand`) and `tick_housing_rent`'s rent-default foreclosure, BOTH of which call it directly on an owned `private_residence`, never through `sell_home`. So `@housing evict` (or a rent-default repossession) tore an owned Tier-3 home down but never freed its lot — a permanent lot-slot leak, 4 admin commands from a leaked lot. **Fix — centralized, not duplicated:** `checkout_room`'s decrement now fires for every housing type it tears down (rented_room, faction_quarters, private_residence), gated only on whether `get_lot_by_room` finds a backing lot row — `faction_quarters` has none, so it's a natural no-op there, and `private_residence`'s `entry_room_id` is the same lot-room id `purchase_home` recorded at purchase time. The duplicate decrement `sell_home` added in the 2026-07-02 drop (which ran AFTER `sell_home`'s own `checkout_room(force=True)` call) is REMOVED, since `checkout_room` now owns it — leaving both would have double-decremented every sold home. `sell_shopfront` and `sell_hq` do their own independent teardowns (never route through `checkout_room`) and were already each single-decrementing; unchanged. **Bug #2 (independent):** `sell_shopfront` resolved its target via `get_housing(db, char_id)` ("most recent home by id DESC") instead of `resolve_active_home(db, char)` (the home the player is physically standing in) — but the confirmation prompt one line earlier in `_cmd_sell` (`parser/housing_commands.py`) used `resolve_active_home` to show the price. A 2-shopfront owner (`MAX_TIER4_PER_CHAR=2`, different planets) standing in shop A, seeing A's price, and confirming would have shop B destroyed instead. Fixed by resolving via `resolve_active_home`, exactly like `sell_home` already does. No schema change, no new faucet/sink (funnels unchanged), era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched. New `tests/test_housing_teardown_completeness_2026_07_03.py` (5): evict and rent-default-foreclosure each pinned to decrement `current_homes` exactly once (two-owner-per-lot setup so a leak — stuck at 2 — and a double-decrement — clamped to 0 — are both distinguishable from the correct single decrement to 1); `sell_home` pinned to decrement exactly once (guards the double-count regression from removing its old decrement); a plain `rented_room` checkout regression guard (still decrements once, unchanged); and the 2-shopfront-owner scenario proving the shop stood-in is the one destroyed and the other survives (reversed pre-fix). Targeted gate green: new file (5) + `test_housing_hardening_2026_07_02` (14) + the housing/lots-provider/admin cluster (170 passed, 7 skipped, 28 pre-existing deselected — unrelated marker filtering) + `test_t321_umbrella_admin_audit` + `test_t321_admin_command_access_invariant` (21), no regressions.

### 2026-07-03 — Hygiene batch: mapgen accidental-commit red, loader-overlap warning, help dup keys, dead spa locator, colorblind wound-crit, GCW residue guard — *drop hygiene-batch-f6*
Six independent Fable F6-minor cleanups, each verified against HEAD before touching (no phantom claims), landed together as low-risk. **(1) mapgen red — found the REAL root cause, not just the symptom:** `tools/mapgen/term_boundaries.json` was committed 8 days ago with populated `terms` (cantina/starship) despite its own `_note` saying it must be empty until a live batch runs. The obvious fix (`terms` → `{}`) went green immediately — then the FULL suite gate re-dirtied it right back to the exact same populated content, proving the "accidental commit" was never a one-off: `tests/test_mapgen_style_ref_experiment.py::TestStyleRefExperimentOffline` redirects `paths.SEEDS_DIR`/`BATCHES_DIR`/`MAPS_DIR` into a tempdir (its own docstring claims "fully isolated") but never redirected `paths.TERM_BOUNDARIES_FILE` — so `style_ref_experiment.run_experiment()`'s `BatchOrchestrator` (constructed with no `boundaries_path=`, unlike `overnight_runner.py`'s sibling call) falls through to the git-tracked file, and its mock run detects "cantina"/"starship" as on-theme terms in the real `mos_eisley` paint brief and writes them straight into the tracked seed on every run of this test (and therefore every full-suite run, `run_all_tests.bat` included). **Real fix:** redirected `P.TERM_BOUNDARIES_FILE` alongside the other three path constants in that test's `setUp`/`tearDown` (production `cli.py`/`style_ref_experiment.py` intentionally default to the tracked file for real interactive runs — only the TEST's isolation claim was false); `terms` reset to `{}` again, `_note`/`_schema` preserved; verified by re-running the offending test class and diffing the tracked file byte-for-byte (unchanged). `tests/test_mapgen_pipeline_offline.py::TestBoundarySeedFile::test_committed_seed_is_valid_and_empty` now green and STAYS green. **(2) F6.1 loader-overlap warning:** `engine/npc_loader.py::load_npcs_from_yaml` warned "no 'npcs' key" every boot for `npcs_drop_mob_grind_coruscant_underworld.yaml`, which is correctly `wilderness_npcs:`-keyed and loaded by its own pass (`load_wilderness_npcs`) but is *also* listed under `content_refs.npcs` in era.yaml (off-limits for this drop) — the loader now recognizes sibling-schema top-level keys (`wilderness_npcs`/`jedi_village_npcs`) and skips them silently (`log.debug`), while a genuinely broken file (neither `npcs:` nor a sibling key) still warns. **(3) F6.2 help dup keys:** `anomalies`/`force`/`salvage` collided across `data/help/commands/*.md` (live-command reference, alphabetically first in `os.walk`) and `data/help/topics/*.md` (general rules/lore) — the topics doc silently won every boot by accident of file-walk order, which was actively WRONG for two of the three (`+help anomalies` showed the *space* deepscan topic instead of the live ground `anomalies` command's own doc; `+help salvage`'s topic claimed "Technical attribute check" when the live command runs a Salvage `perform_skill_check`). Fix: the command docs now own the exact command keys (`anomalies`/`force`/`salvage`); the topic docs moved to distinct keys (`space_anomalies`/`the_force`/`space_salvage`, matching their own existing aliases) with zero content loss. Only alias edit: dropped `anom` from the space-anomalies topic (it's the ground command's own alias, was being silently reclaimed by the topic on every boot). No other `see_also` fields touched. **(4) F6.3 dead spa locator:** `tests/spa/test_m3_tokens.py::test_parity_with_engine_wound_level_enum`'s regex looked for `class WoundLevel(IntEnum):` immediately followed by the enum members, but the live class carries a one-line docstring first — the regex never matched, so the test silently skipped on every box forever. Regex now tolerates an optional docstring line; the test executes and passes (confirmed via `-v`: PASSED, not SKIPPED). **(5) F6.5 UI micro:** `.here-wound-hurt`/`.here-wound-crit` shared `--warn` (crit differed only by bold) — added `--warn-crit` (`#ff4ad1`, distinct hue) to both themes (pad/cockpit) so a colorblind setup can still tell "hurt" from "critical" NPC wound severity in the HERE panel. **(6) F6.4 GCW display-string residue:** `engine/territory.py::_GUARD_TEMPLATES['empire'/'rebel']`, `engine/contest.py::_REGION_ANCHOR_TEMPLATES['empire'/'rebel']`, and the `flavor` dict inside `territory.get_zone_influence_line()` are the last engine-level GCW player-facing strings — deliberately NOT stripped (the rewicker migration may still reference the code paths) per the drop brief. Added a guard instead: the sole producer of any live DB `org_code` is `engine.organizations.seed_organizations()`, which reads `code:` off `data/worlds/<era>/organizations.yaml`'s `factions`/`guilds` lists — Clone Wars (the only production era; GCW's world directory is deleted, `era_state._DEFAULT_ERA = "clone_wars"`) never assigns `empire`/`rebel` as a code, so the residue dicts can never actually fire for a real player. New `tests/test_hygiene_batch_2026_07_03.py` (21): mapgen metadata preservation + a root-cause regression guard that runs the previously-offending `TestStyleRefExperimentOffline` class and diffs the tracked seed byte-for-byte (must be unchanged); loader silent-skip for both sibling schemas + still-warns-on-genuine-breakage + the real offending file; help-loader zero duplicate-key warnings + command-docs-own-the-key + topics-moved + all three alias resolutions (`anom`→ground command, `deepscan`→space topic, `useforce`→force topic); the fixed WoundLevel locator regex (independent re-derivation) + a live subprocess run confirming the target test is no longer skipped; both wound-severity CSS rules; and the GCW-residue guard (live org roster has no empire/rebel code, `seed_organizations`' file/field assumptions anchored against drift, default era is clone_wars, and a corpus-wide scan of every CW world YAML for a stray `empire`/`rebel` org/faction code). No schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched, zero YAML deletions. **Full-suite gate:** 16297 passed, 9 failed (990s under confirmed heavy multi-session box contention — 3+ other sessions' pytest/mapgen processes observed running concurrently throughout, incl. another session mid-run on the identical `test_smoke_chain_walkthrough.py -k republic_soldier` test independently). Triaged every failure individually (isolated re-run + `git stash`-clean-HEAD re-run, none touch any file this drop modified): the mapgen seed red was THIS drop's own root-cause discovery (fixed above, confirmed green post-fix, and confirmed it STAYS green by re-running the fixed test); `test_cities_phase4b::test_dock_sell_in_city_credits_city` is the standing accepted baseline red. Of the other 7: **3 are a real, reproducible, pre-existing issue** — `test_smoke_chain_walkthrough`'s republic_soldier/separatist_commando/smuggler cases all fail consistently (4/4 for republic_soldier) both on this branch and on a clean-stashed HEAD; root cause is a genuine ~6s async-settle timing budget the walker polls for `talk_to_npc`'s chain-hook completion (`tests/smoke/scenarios/chain_walkthrough.py::_drive_talk_to_npc`) — a different mechanism than the previously-documented combat-RNG flake, worth a fresh look but out of this drop's scope. **The remaining 3** (`test_smoke_ground_combat::test_g1_attack_starts_combat`, `test_smoke_pc_bounty_session2::test_bty_5_bh_claim_and_release`, `test_f6a6_boot_ordering::test_correct_ordering_picks_up_era_yaml`) all PASSED cleanly in isolation post-run — transient box-contention flakes from that specific run, not reproducible.

### 2026-07-03 — Fix the `smuggler` (and `republic_soldier`/`separatist_commando`) onboarding-chain talk_to_npc walkthrough flake — *drop smuggler-chain-step3-fix*
Root-caused the reported "smuggler soft-locked at step 3" smoke failure — it reproduced deterministically on THIS box but the root cause is **not Reggi-Voss- or smuggler-specific**: `republic_soldier` (steps 1 & 4) and `separatist_commando` (step 4) fail identically in isolation with the same corpus/engine mechanism, which rules out a data or NPC-dialogue-interception bug. Traced the real `talk` command path end to end with targeted instrumentation (reverted before landing): `TalkCommand.execute` → `_generate_and_display` → `_post_talk_hooks` → `engine.chain_events.on_talk_to_npc` all fire and match correctly — the chain step DOES advance, just not within the smoke walkthrough's polling window. `tests/smoke/scenarios/chain_walkthrough.py::_drive_talk_to_npc` polled for a flat ~6s (30 × 0.2s) after issuing `talk <npc>`, assuming (per its own docstring) "no real Ollama backend" in the smoke harness. That assumption is false: the harness boots the real `GameServer`, which wires a real `AIManager` against `http://localhost:11434` — on any dev box where Ollama is actually running (part of the normal stack per CLAUDE.md), `_generate_and_display`'s dialogue call is a genuine network round-trip, bounded in production to `NPC_DIALOGUE_TIMEOUT_S` (12.0s, `parser/npc_commands.py`) via `asyncio.wait_for`. Confirmed directly: walking all 7 chains together, the same smuggler dialogue call completed in 3.05s (Ollama already warm from an earlier chain's talk step); walking `smuggler` or `republic_soldier` alone (first-ever NPC dialogue call in the process → cold model load) exceeded the test's 6s poll window every time — a genuine race between the test's impatience and the model's real, production-legal worst-case latency, not an engine bug. **Fix (test-only, no engine/data change):** `_drive_talk_to_npc` now polls out to `NPC_DIALOGUE_TIMEOUT_S + 8.0s` (imported from `parser.npc_commands`, deadline-based via `time.monotonic()`, mirroring `tests/harness.py::_drain_text`'s style) instead of a flat 6s — a bound production itself guarantees the post-talk hook has been reached by, regardless of whether Ollama is up, down, warm, or cold. No production code touched, so normal LLM NPC dialogue for non-chain NPCs and every other chain's talk_to_npc step are unaffected by construction. Targeted gate green: `smuggler`, `republic_soldier`, `separatist_commando` each pass in isolation (previously all three failed identically in isolation — a pre-existing flake this drop surfaces was wider than reported, not narrower); full `test_smoke_chain_walkthrough.py` (7/7) green; `test_f8c2b_chain_events.py` + `test_chain_corpus_reachability_invariant.py` + `test_drop_f8c2a_chain_anchors.py` (78) green; `test_generalized_questline_phantom_tonnage.py` (36, unaffected — drives `on_talk_to_npc` directly, never exercises this scenario helper) green. No schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched. Files: `tests/smoke/scenarios/chain_walkthrough.py`.

### 2026-07-03 — Phantom `db.update_character` API crashed the faction armory + destroyed stolen home trophies — *drop faction-armory-save-character*
Bug-hunt fix (Opus quality loop; 2 read-only code-reviewers on the fresh, non-contended `organizations`/`territory` clusters → adjudicated skeptically). Four runtime sites called `db.update_character(char_id, inventory=...)` — a method that **does not exist** on `db.database.Database` (there is no `__getattr__`; the real writer is `save_character`, and `inventory` is an allowlisted writable column). Every call raised `AttributeError` at runtime. Reachable, live, and test-invisible (no test exercised these functions):
- `engine/territory.py` `armory_deposit_item` / `armory_withdraw_item` / `armory_withdraw_resources` (1308/1357/1436) — the `faction armory deposit|withdraw [<res> <qty>]` commands. These became live-reachable when the region-ownership gate was un-stubbed (QA 2026-06-21, `parser/faction_commands._cmd_armory`), so `faction armory deposit/withdraw` has crashed for every player since; deposit also popped the item from the in-memory inventory before the crash (session-state corruption).
- `engine/housing.py` `attempt_theft` (3460) — the home-theft **success** branch removes the trophy from the home and **commits** that removal (3450) before the crash, so a successful theft **destroyed** the item (gone from the home, never credited to the thief).
Fix: all four sites now call `db.save_character(char_id, inventory=...)` — the established idiom used across `engine/buildings.py` and `engine/communal_objective_runtime.py`. Also dropped a now-redundant `await db.commit()` in `attempt_theft` (`save_character` commits internally). Behavior-preserving beyond making the features actually work — no schema, no faucet/sink, no funnel touched (these are inventory writes, not credit movement), era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched. New `tests/test_faction_armory_save_character_2026_07_03.py` (5: behavioral deposit/withdraw-item/withdraw-resources round-trips against a real in-memory DB — each raised AttributeError pre-fix — plus an API-existence guard [Database has `save_character`, not `update_character`] and a source guard pinning all touched files). Targeted gate green (184 across the new test + cities/faction/territory/housing suites + hygiene + foundation smoke). **Deferred, NOT fixed here (single-theme, and balance/design-adjacent — noted for a future fire or Brian):** in `organizations.py`, the payroll loop's `break`-on-unaffordable-stipend starves payable lower-rank members (should be `continue`); the cross-faction rep penalty `int(delta * -0.5)` truncates a `delta=1` gain to a 0 penalty; the faction-switch cooldown is bypassable via `leave`→`join`; and the spec-faction promotion-equipment gate suppresses rank≥2 equipment. Also latent: the armory deposit path assumes a dict-shaped inventory and would raise on the `'[]'` list default (only reachable for an empty inventory, which can't deposit anyway; the canonical fix, when a fire takes it, is `engine.items.coerce_inventory`).

### 2026-07-03 — CRITICAL launch-blocker: the staged-cult scenario WIN paid ZERO reward; the relic grant destroyed inventory — *drop staged-cult-reward-payout-fix*
A break-it pass proved a real staged-cult WIN (Hollow Sun / Ember Court / Ashen Hand / Iron Veil / Drowned Choir — all 5 cults are now staged site scenarios) paid nothing: no rep, no title, no 1000cr capstone, no relic. **Bug #1 (root cause):** the site-clear path `engine.communal_objective_runtime.on_scenario_progress` (~L460-540) advanced a staged cult's stage cursor on a resolved anomaly but never wrote `contribs[cid]["points"]` — only the legacy `record_strike` (menace-path cults) wrote points, and `rally strike` is unconditionally redirected away to the site once a stage anomaly is armed (`parser/communal_commands.py::_redirect_strike_to_site`), so a REAL 3-stage clear reached `_distribute_rewards` with only the `_stage` bookkeeping key in `contributions_json` → `total = sum(points) <= 0` → the whole payout short-circuited before rep/title/capstone/relic ever ran. **Fix:** the anomaly substrate already stamps its resolver on every real resolution path (`WildernessAnomaly.resolved_by`, set at `engine/wilderness_anomalies.py:3847` skill / `:4619` `:4802` combat) — `on_scenario_progress` now reads `anom.resolved_by` at the point a stage's anomaly is confirmed cleared and credits that character `CO.stage_clear_points()` (new constant `STAGE_CLEAR_POINTS = 100` + tunable accessor `communal.stage_clear_points`, mirroring the `win_capstone_credits()` pattern; share-relative only — never needs to match strike-path point magnitudes), persisted in the SAME existing `contributions_json` UPDATE. A missing/invalid `resolved_by` (an aged-out re-arm, or an unattributed clear) never blocks the stage advance — it simply earns no points. **Admin-path parity:** `@director cult win` (`force_resolve`) used to force a win with zero real contributors and pay nothing, confusing anyone verifying the feature; `force_resolve` gained an optional `credit_char_id` kwarg (in-memory credit only, not persisted to the row) and `parser/director_commands.py::_cult`'s `win`/`resolve` branch now passes the invoking admin's `ctx.session.character` id, so the admin command visibly pays out. **Bug #2:** `_grant_capstone_item` (~L745) hand-rolled its inventory parse (`if not isinstance(inv, dict): inv = {}`), silently discarding a bare-LIST inventory — the DB column's schema default `'[]'`, the shape a fresh character carries — and replacing it with `{"items": [relic]}`, destroying every other item. Fixed by swapping to the canonical `engine.items.coerce_inventory` (the same fix already applied to `engine/vendor_droids.py`'s stock/unstock paths for the identical ambiguity), which normalizes both shapes without ever dropping existing items. No schema change, no new faucet (reuses `communal_win_capstone`), era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched. New `tests/test_staged_cult_reward_payout_2026_07_03.py` (3) drives the REAL resolver signal instead of the existing tests' hand-injected `contribs["cid"]={"points":50}` (exactly why they missed bug #1): a full 3-stage Hollow Sun clear pins points increasing after every stage (fails hard pre-fix with `KeyError` — verified against pre-fix code) and the win paying rep + the `communal_win_capstone` credit faucet + the relic; a bare-list-inventory pin proves the relic grant preserves pre-existing items; a two-contributor split proves the bigger contributor is the top contributor (gets the relic) and both cross the title-share threshold. Targeted gate green: new file (3) + `test_drowned_choir_staged_scenario_2026_07_02` + `test_iron_veil_staged_scenario_2026_07_02` + `test_drop4b_communal_cult` + `test_events_playable_scenarios_2026_06_24` + `test_events_more_scenarios_2026_06_24` + `test_session38` + `test_guide_26_cult_uprising_reverify` + `test_guide_26_reward_tier_accuracy_2026_07_02` + `test_fragment_prune_contract` + `test_fork_event_staged_2026_06_23` + `test_guide_27_administration` + `test_qa_batch2_tail_2026_06_23` + `test_situation_digest` (296 total), no regressions. NOTE: `@director cult win` with zero real contributors AND no identifiable admin character still intentionally pays nothing — verify the full reward chain via real staged-site play (or the admin path with a logged-in character). Full suite gate: 16285 passed, 8 failed — all accounted for (5 accepted baseline reds + 3 unrelated SPA/JSDOM subprocess tests confirmed as parallel-contention flakes, 12/12 green in isolation).

### 2026-07-03 — The 36th accessible questline: "The Phantom Tonnage" — a new civilian big-ship VENUE (Ardent Span liner, Kuat) unlocks the first combat-aboard-a-capital-ship arc — *drop generalized-questline-phantom-tonnage*
Resolves `QUEST.t3_24_36th_arc_skill_pool_exhausted` (design_calls_pending_brian → design_calls_resolved_recent) via **Fork B** (Brian: "you pick" the concept). Every clean attribute skill pool the T3.24 lane could reach was spent at 35 arcs; this arc's freshness is a new SHAPE, not a new skill. **The seam reality:** there is NO capital-ship-as-combatant seam — `ALLOWED_COMPLETION_TYPES` (`engine/tutorial_chains.py:59`) has no `ship_destroyed`/`space_combat_won`, space combat emits no chain event, and the wilderness-anomaly multi-phase "wave" machinery is parallel and unbridged to the chain step engine. So the arc realizes "big-ship combat" honestly as **person-scale ground combat fought ABOARD a capital-class liner**, via the one real chain combat seam — `combat_won` against a statically-authored NPC tagged `ai_config.chain_enemy_template`. The capital-ship identity comes from the **VENUE**, not a ship-vs-ship mechanic: 4 new hand-built rooms — `kuat_liner_gangway` → `kuat_liner_gundeck` → `kuat_liner_manifest_core` → `kuat_liner_bridge` (linear, reciprocal exits) — in a new zone `kuat_bulk_liner`, appended additively to `data/worlds/clone_wars/planets/kuat.yaml` (ids 335-338) and `zones.yaml`. Boarded via ONE additive `gangway:` exit on the existing anchor room 322 (`kuat_supply_station`, zone `kdy_orbital_ring` — an orbital station, not a golden-snapshot-pinned exterior surface room) + a reciprocal `out:` back. **The arc** (`kuat_phantom_tonnage`, mirrors `kuat_papered_refit`'s shape): (1) `talk_to_npc` Suli Verrin (independent bonded-tonnage auditor, giver, `kuat_supply_station`); (2) `skill_check_passed` `sensors`@11 (`kuat_liner_gangway`: scan the true loaded mass against the declared tonnage); (3) `combat_won` `phantom_tonnage_gundeck_boss` enemy_count 1 (`kuat_liner_gundeck`: Hakko Vurm, Weequay deck-boss); (4) `skill_check_passed` `computer programming`@13 (`kuat_liner_manifest_core`: slice the doctored manifest core); (5) `talk_to_npc` Renk Talo (non-hostile stand-down enforcer captain, `kuat_liner_bridge`). **Critical phantom-consumer discipline:** because step 3 is a REAL `combat_won` chain step, Hakko Vurm's `ai_config` carries `chain_enemy_template: phantom_tonnage_gundeck_boss` — the OPPOSITE of the staged-cult bosses, which omit the tag because they have no chain step — so `_collect_defeated_chain_templates` (`parser/combat_commands.py`) actually credits the kill via `on_combat_won`. **Winnability:** Hakko Vurm's stat block is **cloned verbatim** from the proven-in-band `papered_refit_enforcer` (Vodran Sekk) — identical attributes/skills/weapon — so the foil-winnability guard passes by construction; `enemy_count: 1` (no untested multi-foil wave, conservative per Brian's standing balance preference). **Rewards copied VERBATIM from `kuat_papered_refit`** (the freelance-tier canonical `test_questline_reward_tier_consistency` enforces): 300cr + independent:5 graduation, per-step rep 2/2/3/3/2, a single `credits: 150` on the combat step, `cp_reward: 3` achievement (`phantom_tonnage_cleared`) — arc total 450cr + 17 independent rep, identical to all 35 peers. New NPC file `data/worlds/clone_wars/npcs_drop_generalized_questline_phantom_tonnage.yaml` (3 NPCs: Suli Verrin, Hakko Vurm, Renk Talo — all non-canon, era-clean, WEG R&E D6), wired into `era.yaml` `content_refs.npcs`. Era-clean: the Ardent Span is an explicitly CIVILIAN merchant liner (no Republic-Navy framing), the racket a generic civilian syndicate/contraband front, KSF the only on-stage authority — no Imperial/Empire/Rebel/TIE strings, no canonical Clone Wars figures. **Logged, non-blocking future fork:** a TRUE ship-vs-ship capital-combat step (fly the liner, trade turbolaser fire, disable it) has no seam and is a separate deliberate future design+build (a new `ALLOWED_COMPLETION_TYPES` value + a space-combat→chain bridge) — this arc does not need it and does not wait on it. All world-YAML edits purely additive (zero deleted lines, verified via `git diff --numstat`). New `tests/test_generalized_questline_phantom_tonnage.py` (36: shape, full walkthrough via the production dispatcher, wrong-skill/wrong-enemy-template non-advance guards, achievement registration, reward-band-matches-papered-refit-exactly, reachability, venue connectivity + the additive anchor-exit, NPC placement incl. the `chain_enemy_template` critical guard, era-cleanness). Targeted gate green: 92 passed (new file + `test_questline_reward_tier_consistency` + `test_questline_skill_difficulty_winnability` + `test_questline_foil_winnability_band` + `test_chain_corpus_reachability_invariant` + `test_questline_directory`) + `tools/verify_tutorial_chains.py` schema pass (only the pre-existing stale "expected 9 chains" assertion fails, unrelated — now 50) + foundation smoke boot green. No schema, no new faucet/sink, `force_sensitive` untouched.

### 2026-07-03 — Local NPC-dialogue model is now `OLLAMA_MODEL`-driven; default Mistral 7B → Qwen3.5-9B — *drop ollama-model-env-driven* (Fable review)
Per Fable's 2026-07-03 provider review: Mistral 7B is a Sep-2023 model, and the "Mistral 7B only, RTX 3070 8GB" architecture invariant dated from when it was the best 8GB fit — no longer true (current 7–9B models beat it at the same ~5GB Q4 footprint). `ai/providers.py`: the three `AIConfig` model fields (`default_model` / `tier1_model` / `tier2_model`) and the `OllamaProvider` param default now read the **`OLLAMA_MODEL`** env var (default **`qwen3.5:9b`**), with an optional **`OLLAMA_TIER2_MODEL`** to point the premium story-NPC tier at a bigger tag — so a model swap is a one-line env change with zero code edit (the provider abstraction was built for exactly this). Architecture-invariant lines updated: CLAUDE.md stack line + `docs/design/director_ai_design_v1.md` (the "Mistral 7B only / No 24B" hard constraint is **lifted**; guidance is now "a 7–9B Q4 tag fits the 3070, skip 14B+"). New `tests/test_ollama_model_env_driven_2026_07_03.py` (5: default retires mistral, `OLLAMA_MODEL` drives all tiers, `OLLAMA_TIER2_MODEL` overrides only tier2, blank-env fallback, bare-provider default); 87 AI-provider tests green (the two `mistral` mentions in tests are self-contained stubs). Config only — no NPC-behavior logic changed. OPERATOR NOTE: the default matches `qwen3.5:9b`; if `ollama list` shows a different tag, set `OLLAMA_MODEL` to it. (A sub-1B tag is a quality downgrade from Mistral 7B for in-character text — use ~7–9B.)

### 2026-07-03 — Weapon-experiment fumble no longer inflicts an inverted, escalating quality penalty — *drop experiment-fumble-quality-loss*
Fixes a genuine, reachable correctness bug in the weapon-experimentation path (`ExperimentCommand`, `parser/crafting_commands.py` + `engine/crafting.py`) surfaced by a read-only code-reviewer hunt. In WEG D6 a **fumble** (Wild Die = 1) is independent of success: `perform_skill_check` sets `fumble = roll.complication` while `success = roll.total >= difficulty` and `margin = roll.total - difficulty` (`engine/skill_checks.py:305-336`), and `roll_d6_pool` applies the full complication penalty (Wild Die → 0 **and** the highest normal die removed, `engine/dice.py:262-266`). So once a crafter's pool is large enough to still clear the difficulty after that penalty, **`fumble is True` while `margin >= 0`** — a fumble-with-net-success, realistic for a skilled crafter. The dispatch routes *every* fumble to `_handle_fumble` (`crafting_commands.py:511`), `resolve_experiment_failure` returns `"quality_loss"` for any `margin > -5`, and the handler computed `loss = abs(result.margin) * 2` — feeding the **positive** success margin in as if it were a failure magnitude. The penalty was therefore **inverted**: a *stronger* roll on a fumble destroyed *more* quality (margin +8 → 16 points lost; +2 → 4 lost), scaling with how well the crafter rolled. **Fix (minimal, balance-conservative):** the quality-loss magnitude is single-sourced through a new pure helper `engine.crafting.experiment_quality_loss(margin) = abs(min(margin, 0)) * 2`, which clamps the basis at 0 so the loss tracks the **failure** margin only. Genuine failures (`margin < 0`) are byte-identical to before; a fumble-with-success now costs **0** quality — routing is unchanged (no new weapon destruction), and the handler emits an honest "the modification doesn't take, but the weapon is unharmed" line instead of a false "degrades quality" message when nothing is lost. Both experiment quality-loss sites (the fumble path and the non-fumble `_handle_regular_failure` path, which only ever sees negative margins) now go through the helper. No schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched. New `tests/test_experiment_fumble_quality_loss_2026_07_03.py` (5): failure-margin losses unchanged, fumble+success costs 0, the loss curve is monotonically non-increasing across margins −12..+12 (a better roll can never cost more — the anti-inversion invariant), the former `abs(+8)*2 = 16` value is gone, and `resolve_experiment_failure` confirms `"quality_loss"` is the live outcome for a fumble+success. Targeted gate green (126: new + craft-quality-tunables + craft-telemetry + craft-armor-soak + guide-07 + hygiene + smoke-foundation), no regressions.

### 2026-07-03 — Multi-phase combat anomalies chain wave→wave without a re-`attack` (every staged-cult combat stage) — *drop anomaly-wave-reengage*
Closes the last deferred events-lane polish item (`HANDOFF_events_playable_fix_tune_2026-07-02` §"Deferred quick follow-ups" #2). Every tier≥2 combat wilderness anomaly — including **all 10 staged-cult combat stages** (Hollow Sun / Ember Court / Ashen Hand / Iron Veil / Drowned Choir, each a 2-wave assault + a 2-wave boss) — used to **stutter between waves**: clearing wave 1 fired the kill hook (`award_combat_anomaly_reward` → `_advance_to_next_phase`), which spawns wave 2 into the anchor room and records it on `anomaly.spawned_npc_ids` — but those fresh hostiles were never added to the active `CombatInstance`, so `_combat_finished` saw no live hostiles, **ended the fight**, and the player had to type `attack` again to engage the next wave. **Fix (no new engine system; mirrors the existing `_check_city_guard_engagement` mid-combat NPC-injection precedent):** the kill-hook path now reports the just-spawned wave back to the combat layer through a caller-owned sink. `engine/wilderness_anomalies.py::award_combat_anomaly_reward` gains an optional `wave_reengage_sink: Optional[list] = None` kwarg — on a successful phase ADVANCE it extends the sink with the new `spawned_npc_ids` (a `skill_gate` advance leaves that list empty → no-op; left `None` by the engine-only test callers, fully backward-compatible; the sole production caller is the parser hook). `parser/combat_commands.py::_apply_combat_wear` threads a local sink into that call and **returns** it; both resolution paths (`_try_auto_resolve` + the admin `resolve`) capture the return and, **before** the `_combat_finished` check, call the new `_reengage_anomaly_wave(combat, ctx, npc_ids)` helper, which loads each fresh NPC (`get_npc` → `build_npc_character`), `add_combatant`s it as an active `is_npc` hostile, and registers its behavior in `_npc_behaviors` — so the next `resolve_round` re-rolls initiative for the arrivals and the encounter chains wave→wave in one continuous fight. Fully fail-soft: any error logs at warning and combat proceeds exactly as before (the player can still re-`attack` — the pre-fix behavior); the injection is idempotent (skips ids already in the fight) and scoped precisely to the advanced anomaly's wave (no room re-scan, no wilderness-tile ambiguity). Behaviour-preserving on every non-anomaly and single-phase path (`_wave_ids` is empty → the re-engage no-ops). No schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched. New `tests/test_anomaly_wave_reengage_2026_07_03.py` (6): drives the REAL `_apply_combat_wear` loop + a REAL `CombatInstance` on a representative tier-2 anomaly (`hutt_smuggling_convoy`, 2 thugs → 3 hostiles) — a cleared wave returns exactly the fresh, distinct wave-1 ids; a mid-phase kill and a final-boss clear report NO wave (no phantom chain); the reported ids inject into a live combat as active hostiles that flip `_combat_finished` False (PC-vs-NPC fight continues); injection is idempotent; empty input is a safe no-op. Targeted gate green: new file (6) + `test_anomaly_defeat_clears_on_incap` + `test_syn7a/b/8_wilderness_anomalies` + `test_t3_23_party_skill_gate_phase1` + `test_cities_phase7c_combat` + `test_combat_dead_hooks_reachable` + `test_drowned_choir/iron_veil_staged_scenario` + `test_guide_26_cult_uprising_reverify` + `test_combat_mechanics` (305), no regressions.
### 2026-07-03 — Fix NULL zone_id on Dune Sea wilderness landmarks (incl. the Hollow Sun site) + a static guard against recurrence — *drop wilderness-zone-integrity*
Root cause: `engine/wilderness_writer.py::_lookup_zone_id_by_name` resolves a wilderness region's zone by exact `SELECT id FROM zones WHERE name = ?` (then a LIKE fallback). A zone's DB `name` is set by `engine/world_writer.py::_write_zones` L140 as `zone.raw.get("name", slug)` — no zone in `data/worlds/clone_wars/zones.yaml` declares an explicit `name:` (they use `name_match:` instead), so every zone's effective DB name is just its YAML slug key. `data/worlds/clone_wars/wilderness/dune_sea.yaml` set `region.zone: jundland_wastes`, a slug that doesn't exist anywhere in zones.yaml (real Tatooine slugs are `tatooine_jundland` and `tatooine_dune_sea`) — this was already flagged as an open finding in the 2026-07-02 guides-publish drop ("dune_sea.yaml has a zone-name mismatch"). The lookup silently returned NULL, so every Dune Sea landmark room — including the Hollow Sun staged-cult site — landed with a NULL `zone_id` and inherited default security instead of the intended lawless Tatooine-outback posture. Cross-checked all 6 wilderness regions that declare a `region.zone` (coruscant_underworld, ey_akh, kuat_sabotaged_yards, nar_shaddaa_drowned_sublevels, tatooine_jundland, dune_sea): dune_sea was the only one broken. **Fix:** one-line value edit — `region.zone` now reads `tatooine_dune_sea` (the region's own zone, matching the pattern every other wilderness region already follows; confirmed `security: lawless` in zones.yaml, consistent with the region's `default_security: lawless`). Net-zero-line YAML edit (comment-preserving, additive-safe). **Guard:** new `tests/test_wilderness_zone_resolves_2026_07_03.py` mirrors the writer's effective-name resolution EXACTLY (no LIKE-fallback laxity) against every `data/worlds/clone_wars/wilderness/*.yaml` that declares a `region.zone`, so a future region naming a nonexistent zone slug fails the suite instead of silently NULLing at build time; plus a focused dune_sea pin and a runtime test that writes the real Dune Sea region against an in-memory DB seeded with a `tatooine_dune_sea` zone, asserting every landmark room gets that zone's real (non-NULL) `zone_id`. Flipped one pre-existing pin on the old broken value: `tests/test_wilderness_drop2.py::TestSentinelAndRegistryWrite::test_registry_row_for_dune_sea` asserted `zone_slug == "jundland_wastes"` (the raw YAML value cached verbatim in the `wilderness_regions` registry row) — now asserts the fixed, resolvable `"tatooine_dune_sea"`. No schema change, no engine code touched, era-clean. Targeted gate green: new file (6) + `test_drowned_choir_staged_scenario_2026_07_02` (25) + `test_world_writer` (29) = 60, plus `test_wilderness_drop2` + `test_dune_sea_minimal` (80 combined), no regressions. Full-suite gate (16225 passed, 7 failed under confirmed heavy multi-session box contention — all 7 verified as either the 2 sanctioned baseline reds or isolation-passing flakes, zero new failures).

### 2026-07-03 — Player-facing stale-snapshot sinks (smuggling fine, sabacc loss, sleep-theft) hardened to charge the LIVE balance — *drop stale-snapshot-sink-credit-integrity*
Extends the credit-integrity sweep from the automated tick sinks (`tick-sink-credit-integrity`) to three PLAYER-FACING sinks carrying the same "delta computed from a cached session balance, applied with the default `allow_negative=True`" class. When a character's cached `credits` snapshot goes stale — an out-of-band weekly-debt / housing-rent tick, or an earlier sleep-theft, drained the real balance since the session loaded the character (an online player's `ctx.session.character` is NOT refreshed by those DB-side ticks) — the computed delta overdrew the LIVE column negative. New shared funnel helper **`db.debit_capped(char_id, cost, source)`**: charges up to `cost` against the live balance via `adjust_credits(..., allow_negative=False)`, and on a refused overdraw takes exactly the real remaining balance (floors at 0, never negative) — routing every movement through the funnel/ledger. **(A) `parser/smuggling_commands.py`** — the customs FINE in both `check_patrol_on_launch` (~455) and `check_patrol_on_arrival` (~559) computed `adjust_credits(cid, max(0, credits-fine) - credits, "smuggling_fine")` off the session snapshot; both now call `debit_capped(char_id, fine, "smuggling_fine")` and display the real post-balance. **(B) `parser/sabacc_commands.py`** — a LOSS/TIE/FUMBLE debited `adjust_credits(cid, new_credits - credits, "sabacc")` where `new_credits = max(0, credits - bet)` (overdraws when the live balance is below the snapshot); split into a plain faucet on win (`net_win`) and `debit_capped(cid, bet, "sabacc")` on a loss, with the session cache + `Balance:` line now reflecting the authoritative return (None-safe fallback to the computed value for test doubles). **(C) `engine/sleeping.py::attempt_pickpocket`** — the theft read the victim's balance from the (stale, possibly-offline) cached dict and, worse, was a non-atomic two-leg transfer: if crediting the thief faulted after debiting the victim, credits were DESTROYED (victim charged, thief gained nothing, no refund). Now reads the victim's LIVE balance via `get_character`, caps the take at what they actually have, debits with `allow_negative=False`, and refunds the victim (`theft_loss_refund`) if crediting the thief fails — a true atomic transfer that never mints or destroys credits. All changes are behaviour-preserving on the funded happy path and only alter the broke/racing/fault path. No schema, no NEW faucet/sink tag (reuses `smuggling_fine`/`sabacc`/`theft_loss`/`theft_gain`; adds a `theft_loss_refund` compensation tag), era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched. New `tests/test_qa_stale_snapshot_sink_credit_integrity_2026_07_03.py` (10): behavioural coverage of the real `debit_capped` (covered charge; overdraw floors to the live balance never negative; broke charges nothing; zero-cost no-op; signed-cost magnitude; every movement funnel-routed) + structural drift-guards pinning the helper's funnel-routing and all three call sites (both smuggling fines via `debit_capped`, sabacc loss via `debit_capped` + win faucet, theft reads live balance + atomic guard + refund leg). Targeted gate green: new file (10) + `test_sabacc_telemetry` (its `_FakeDB` given a `debit_capped` double) + `test_smuggling_reward_tunables` + `test_contraband_scan_checkpoint` + `test_ledger_chokepoint_complete` + `test_drop1b3_ledger_migration_complete` + `test_guide_23_sabacc_entertainer` + `test_qa_tick_sink_credit_integrity` + hygiene + smoke-foundation (107) + smuggling/social smoke scenarios (18).
### 2026-07-03 — Post-review cleanup: Iron Veil reward-claim wording corrected; F4 wilderness-overview boot warning re-verified as a real design fork, NOT shipped — *drop post-review-cleanup*
Two independent, fully-specified fixes from an adversarial review; both re-verified against HEAD before touching anything (no-phantom-claims discipline). **Fix 2 (shipped):** the 2026-07-02 Iron Veil drop's CHANGELOG/comment claim that its reward bands were "copied byte-for-byte/EXACTLY from ember_court" is false for 2 of its 3 templates — `iron_veil_cell_assault` and `iron_veil_veilmaster` swap `composite`→`energy` in `success_reward.resources` (credits + influence DO match; only the skill stage `iron_veil_power_relay` is fully identical). The reward VALUES are fine and intentional (energy-themed for a shipyard/blackout cult at equal quantity+value) — this is a documentation-accuracy fix only, no reward data changed. Reworded the `engine/wilderness_anomalies.py` ~L2506 comment block and this CHANGELOG's 2026-07-02 Iron Veil entry to claim only what's true: credit + influence bands match Ember Court's same-tier templates (pinned by an equality test), resources are re-themed to energy at equal quantity+value and are NOT byte-for-byte identical (except the skill stage). Strengthened `tests/test_iron_veil_staged_scenario_2026_07_02.py::test_reward_bands_match_ember_court_same_tier_templates` to assert the skill stage's `success_reward`/`fail_reward` are fully equal to `ember_court_relay_slice`'s (not just credits), while still not asserting full resource equality on the combat/boss templates (intentionally different) — so the prose can't drift back to the false claim. The Drowned Choir "byte-for-byte from ashen_hand" claim is untouched (independently verified accurate). **Fix 1 (NOT shipped — re-confirmed design fork, logged `MAP.fable_f4_wilderness_overview_no_areageometry_reconfirmed` in `design_calls_pending_brian`):** the brief asked for a loader-side fallback in `engine/area_loader.py::load_area_geometry` — try `maps/<slug>_overview.yaml` when the bare `maps/<slug>.yaml` is absent — to fix the every-boot `[area_loader] registry: skipping coruscant_underworld / tatooine_dune_sea: AreaGeometry YAML not found` warning, on the stated premise that the two `_overview.yaml` files "are real AreaGeometry YAML the loader already understands." Implemented the fallback exactly as specified and verified it against both files: both raise `AreaGeometryLoadError: missing required field 'schema_version'` instead of "not found" — still a load failure, still skipped, the boot outcome is unchanged. Root cause: `maps/coruscant_underworld_overview.yaml` / `maps/tatooine_dune_sea_overview.yaml` are GENERATED Tier-1b wilderness-overview projections (`tools/gen_wilderness_overview.py`) — `area_key`/`bounds`/`terrain_zones`/`routes`/`landmarks`, no `schema_version`/`districts`/`rooms`/`exits` — an incompatible schema from a room-based AreaGeometry, serving a different already-wired live consumer (`static/spa/m3_wilderness_overview_data.js` via `m3_tier_wilderness_body.js::resolveRegion`, the Tier-1b wilderness zoomed-out map), not the room-based datapad `AreaGeometryRegistry`. This exactly reconfirms `TD.FABLE_F4_wilderness_overview_no_areageometry` (tech_debt, already logged 2026-07-03 by the `fable-review-incorporation` drop, which had already corrected Fable's original one-line-fix misdiagnosis) — an independent re-derivation, no new information. `engine/area_loader.py` reverted to byte-identical HEAD after the scratch verification (a half-fix with a misleading rationale would itself be a phantom claim); no boot-parity guard test added (it would pin an outcome that depends on a still-open design choice: (a) teach the loader/discovery to silently skip overview-schema files — closes the log noise, ships no room-based geometry for these two regions — or (b) author full room-based AreaGeometry for both from scratch — real content-authoring scope, not a loader fix). No schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean; files touched: `engine/wilderness_anomalies.py` (comment only), `tests/test_iron_veil_staged_scenario_2026_07_02.py`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `TODO.json`.

### 2026-07-03 — New `lane-partition` skill: carve a backlog into conflict-free parallel lanes — *drop skill-lane-partition*
Brian's call: build the partitioner that would have pre-empted this session's two coordination failures — the F5a events-lane overlap between the two sessions, and the ~5 `CHANGELOG.md`/`TODO.json` union-resolves. Tooling/meta only; no code, no schema.

### 2026-07-03 — P0: chain `combat_won` credit lost to a straight-to-DEAD finishing blow (all 40 chains) — *drop chain-combat-won-dead-snapshot* (Fable review F1)
Fable's review found the "flaky" `test_chain_walks_to_graduation[republic_soldier]` smoke (~2/6 pass — long mis-filed as a combat-RNG flake) was actually a real **P0 soft-lock**. `resolve_round()`'s `_cleanup()` pops any combatant that reached **DEAD** from `combat.combatants` the same round it falls; the F.8.c.2.b chain-completion block in `parser/combat_commands.py` collected defeated foils by iterating the **live (post-pop) dict**, so a foil finished with a straight-to-DEAD blow was invisible → `on_combat_won` never fired → chain credit silently lost. Blast radius: every `combat_won` step (7 onboarding chains + all 33 accessible-questline foils); on a **single-foil questline step it's a HARD soft-lock** (foil despawned dead, step unfireable). This is the DEAD-side twin of the 2026-07-02 `anomaly-defeat-clear` fix, and the 4th consumer bitten by the `_cleanup` pop — `_apply_combat_wear` / `_award_mob_grind_rewards` / `_award_early_combat_cp` were already snapshot-fed; the chain block was the odd one out. **Fix (matches the siblings; `engine/combat.py` untouched):** collection extracted to a pure helper `_collect_defeated_chain_templates(db, npc_combatants)` fed the pre-resolution snapshot `_pre_npcs`; defeat predicate unchanged (`can_act_now()` False — Incap+ AND the stun-KO capture the Bounty Hunter chain relies on). New `tests/test_chain_combat_won_dead_snapshot_2026_07_03.py` (10: the DEAD-foil regression, both-droids→count-2, ladder/robustness, + a source-read wiring canary so a live-dict revert fails deterministically). The flaky smoke went 2/6 → **9/9 consecutive** post-fix. Re-derived at HEAD per Fable's reference-only contract (the companion zip was not applied). No schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean.

### 2026-07-02 — Housing-engine hardening: lot-slot leak on sale, checkout-destroys-owned-home, stale single-home rental block, cross-tier lot IDs, stale discount text — *drop housing-engine-hardening*
Five housing defects the parallel session that closed out housing flagged (3 precise, 2 vague-but-locatable). **Bug 1 (`engine/housing.py::sell_home`):** missing the `housing_lots.current_homes` decrement its siblings `sell_shopfront`/`sell_hq` both do — every sold Tier-3 home permanently consumed a lot slot, eventually starving Tier-3 housing everywhere. Fixed by adding the identical `UPDATE housing_lots SET current_homes = MAX(0, current_homes - 1) WHERE room_id = ?` after a successful teardown. **Bug 2 (`engine/housing.py::checkout_room`):** treated an owned `private_residence` like a rental, refunding the always-0 `deposit` field and destroying the home for nothing with no confirmation. Resolved per Brian's pre-decided fix: `checkout_room` now takes a keyword-only `force: bool = False` and REFUSES an owned home ("Use 'housing sell' instead") unless `force=True`. Because `sell_home`'s own teardown, the `tick_housing_rent` rent-default foreclosure, and `@housing evict` all legitimately need to tear an owned home down, each now passes `force=True`; only the bare player-facing `housing checkout` command is refused. `parser/housing_commands.py::_cmd_checkout` also skips its storage-count teaser for an owned home so the player isn't told items "will be returned on checkout" immediately before the refusal. **Bug 3 (`engine/housing.py::rent_room`):** still carried the pre-multi-home hard single-home block ("already have a place to stay... checkout first"), which — pre-Bug-2-fix — funneled a 2nd-home rental attempt into the 0cr destruction path. Replaced with the same `MAX_TIER3_TOTAL` total-home cap `purchase_home` already uses (counts ALL housing records, not just tier 3, since this is the overall "own up to 4 homes" limit `housing list` documents). **Bug 4 (found during investigation, `rent_room`/`purchase_home`/`purchase_hq`):** `housing_lots` is one flat table shared by every tier with no type column; only `purchase_shopfront` validated a lot's `room_id` against its own tier's provider corpus. The other three purchase/rent paths accepted ANY lot_id, including one belonging to a different tier (e.g. renting a Tier-3 lot's ID would silently consume ITS slot for a Tier-1 room). Fixed with an exclusion-based check — reject only a `room_id` AFFIRMATIVELY known to belong to a *different* tier — so every existing ad hoc/test-fabricated lot (not registered in any era's provider corpus) is unaffected. **Bug 5 (`engine/housing.py::get_tier3_listing_lines`):** a stale `"(-50% rent)"`/`"(-25% rent)"` display hint for lawless/contested Tier-3 lots, mirroring Tier 4's real `_TIER4_SECURITY_DISCOUNT` mechanic — except `purchase_home` has never applied any security-based discount to Tier-3 `weekly_rent` (always the flat `TIER3_TYPES[...]["weekly_rent"]`). The claim was never backed by a real mechanic; removed rather than inventing a new pricing mechanic in a bug-fix drop. New `tests/test_housing_hardening_2026_07_02.py` (14): lot-slot freed + rebuyable after sale; checkout refuses an owned home (home survives) and `force=True` still tears it down for internal callers; a plain rental checkout is unaffected; renting a 2nd home under the cap succeeds and the 5th is refused with the new cap message (not the stale single-home message); `rent_room`/`purchase_home`/`purchase_hq` each refuse a real cross-tier lot_id sourced from `housing_lots_provider`, with a structural + functional regression guard proving ad hoc lots still work; the Tier-3 listing carries no discount string (source-level + a lawless-lot rendered-output check). Targeted gate green: new file (14) + the full housing/lots-provider/telemetry/HQ-handler cluster (`test_qa_housing_*`, `test_fork_housing_multihome_2026_06_23`, `test_f5a*`, `test_f5b*`, `test_t319_housing_telemetry`, `test_t2def_dynamic_hq_handler`, `test_home_prestige`, admin/umbrella-audit, chain-corpus-reachability, housing smoke scenario) = 173+34+36+9+5+74 all green, no regressions. No schema, no new faucet/sink (Bug 1's decrement mirrors an existing funnel-adjacent occupancy counter, not a credit path), era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched.

### 2026-07-03 — Weekly-tick credit sinks (Hutt debt + housing rent) hardened to debit-first with the atomic overdraw guard — *drop tick-sink-credit-integrity*
Closes the last two instances of the "stale pre-check + no `allow_negative` guard" credit-integrity bug class. The 2026-06-21 QA sweep (`test_qa_housing_credit_integrity_2026_06_21`) fixed the **player-initiated** housing paths (`rent_room`/`purchase_home`/`purchase_shopfront`) to debit-first with `allow_negative=False`, but left the two **automated weekly-tick sinks** carrying the same class. **(A) `engine/debt.py::process_all_debts`** persisted the principal reduction via `save_character` *before* debiting credits, and debited (`adjust_credits(..., "debt_payment")`) with the default `allow_negative=True` against a STALE per-tick balance snapshot (the bulk `SELECT id, credits, attributes` at the top of the loop). Two real bugs: a debit failure between the two writes handed out **free debt forgiveness** (principal already dropped, credits never taken — swallowed by the per-char `except`), and a concurrent spend between the snapshot and the debit drove the balance **negative**. Fixed: debit FIRST with `allow_negative=False` (a refused overdraw returns `None` → treated as a missed payment, exactly like insufficient funds; a debit fault is caught and also treated as missed), and only on a successful debit is the principal reduction persisted — with a compensating `debt_payment_refund` if that persist then fails, so a debtor is never charged without progress (mirrors the `engine/dens.py` sink-compensation pattern). **(B) `engine/housing.py::tick_housing_rent`** debited weekly rent with the default `allow_negative=True` against a per-row balance read; a concurrent drain drove rent negative while it still "collected". Fixed: `allow_negative=False`; a refused overdraw (`None`) now falls to the existing overdue branch instead of overdrawing. Both changes are behaviour-preserving on the happy path (funds present → same debit, same principal/rent updates) and only alter the broke/racing path. No schema, no new faucet/sink, era-clean; only the two tick functions touched (the player-initiated paths and telemetry emitters are untouched). New `tests/test_qa_tick_sink_credit_integrity_2026_07_03.py` (8): deterministic stub-DB coverage — debt happy-path debits-before-persist and reduces principal with the atomic flag; overdraw-refused and debit-fault both leave the principal untouched and record a missed payment (no free forgiveness); a principal-persist failure refunds the debit (never charged net); housing rent overdraw-refused marks overdue with no negative, happy-path collects and advances `rent_paid_until`; plus source guards pinning the debit-before-persist ordering and `allow_negative=False` on both ticks. Targeted gate green: the new file + `test_qa_housing_credit_integrity_2026_06_21` + `test_t319_debt_telemetry` + `test_t319_housing_telemetry` (60).

Generalizes the events-as-playable-scenarios pattern to the **Iron Veil** (Kuat shipyards) — landing alongside the sibling **Drowned Choir** (Nar Shaddaa) drop, together completing the conversion: all 5 cults (Hollow Sun / Ember Court / Ashen Hand / Iron Veil / Drowned Choir) now run as 3-stage site scenarios, and **none** remain on the legacy menace-counter path, with NO new engine code. **Bespoke venue:** a new `data/worlds/clone_wars/wilderness/kuat_sabotaged_yards.yaml` region (4 landmarks: The Blackout Gantry / The Dark Cargo Bay / The Dead Relay Hub / The Veil Den) with its own `kuat_sabotaged_yards` zone, reachable via a new on-foot edge room (`kuat_sabotaged_yards_edge`) off Kuat Drive Yards — Ring Warehouse District (additive-only edit to `planets/kuat.yaml` + `zones.yaml`). **Stages** (mirror Ember Court): Stage 1 combat `iron_veil_cell_assault` (break the saboteur cells) → Stage 2 skill `iron_veil_power_relay` (primary `technical` / secondary `computer_programming` + `alt_skills` `[persuasion,con,bargain]` — splice the relays or talk down the dock crews) → Stage 3 boss `iron_veil_veilmaster`. Reward bands **copied byte-for-byte** from the ember_court templates (balance-neutral; pinned by an equality test). `engine/staged_event.py` gains `IRON_VEIL_STAGES` + `STAGED_CULTS`/`STAGED_CULT_REGION` entries; `engine/wilderness_anomalies.py` +3 templates; `era.yaml` +2 additive `content_refs`; 4 ambient NPCs (3 hostile, 1 non-hostile dock-tech flavor) in a new `npcs_drop_iron_veil_scenario.yaml`. Guide_26 §3 is rewritten (jointly with the Drowned Choir drop) to teach that every cult uprising is now a staged operation — the old "galaxy-wide menace cult" bullet is retired. The boss deliberately **omits** `chain_enemy_template` (a staged-cult boss has no consumer for it — a dead field; omission pinned, anti-phantom). New `tests/test_iron_veil_staged_scenario_2026_07_02.py` (19): staged-descriptor contracts, reward-band equality, region-load + reachability + no-pinned-room-deletion, full orchestrator walk (arm → clear 3 stages → win → rep). Pre-existing "N staged cults" pins (`test_fork_event_staged` / `test_guide_26_cult_uprising_reverify` / `test_anomaly_social_alt_skills` / `test_world_writer`) reconciled to the new 5-cult, 0-menace reality (room-manifest count 292→293 for the new edge room). Era-clean, WEG R&E D6, funnel-routed, `force_sensitive` derived, world-YAML additive-only.

### 2026-07-02 — Two parser-dispatch defects from a break-it QA pass: incapacitated PCs could still act; an unguarded NL-combat call could kill a session — *drop parser-dispatch-hardening*
Two defects in `parser/commands.py`, both additive/defensive. **(A) Incapacitated/mortally-wounded PCs could act outside combat.** `CommandParser._execute`'s dead-state intercept only gated `wound_level >= WoundLevel.DEAD` (6) (a `DEAD_ALLOWED` allow-list); there was no matching gate for `INCAPACITATED` (4) or `MORTALLY_WOUNDED` (5), even though `combat.declare_action` already refuses those with "You can't act in your current condition." — so an unconscious/dying-but-alive character could freely walk to new rooms and chat. Fixed by adding a second intercept one rung down the wound ladder (`INCAPACITATED <= wound_level < DEAD`), gated by a new conservative `CommandParser.INCAPACITATED_ALLOWED` set: `DEAD_ALLOWED`'s passive/status commands (look/l, help/+help/?/commands/+commands, who/+who, quit/@quit/logout, respawn — harmless here, `RespawnCommand` self-guards on `wound_level < DEAD` with "You're not dead!") plus OOC chatter (`ooc`/`+ooc`). Movement and active IC commands (attack, say, emote, etc.) are blocked with the same message `combat.declare_action` uses. DEAD keeps its own gate/message unchanged (checked first, so DEAD never falls through to the incapacitated message). **(B) Unguarded NL-combat dispatch call.** In `parse_and_dispatch`, the call to `try_nl_combat_action(...)` was the only dispatch call in that function not wrapped in try/except; an AI-layer fault (a DB hiccup in `SceneContext.build`, an NPC despawning mid-combat) would propagate out of the coroutine and up through `server/game_server.py`'s `_game_loop` (`await self.parser.parse_and_dispatch(...)` there also has no surrounding guard), silently killing that session's game-loop task — the player would get zero output forever. Now wrapped in try/except mirroring the function's other guarded dispatch surfaces: logs via `log.exception` (no bare `except: pass`) and sends the player the same generic "An error occurred processing your command..." line used elsewhere in the file. New `tests/test_parser_dispatch_hardening_2026_07_02.py` (10): (A) INCAPACITATED and MORTALLY_WOUNDED each refuse a movement command and an active IC command but still allow `look`; DEAD still refuses with its own "You are DEAD" message (not the incapacitated one) and still allows `respawn`; a HEALTHY character is unaffected (regression pin) — via a lightweight `CommandParser._execute` harness mirroring `test_command_telemetry.py`. (B) a live-harness (`tests/harness.py`) regression: `parser.combat_commands.try_nl_combat_action` monkeypatched to raise, fed through a real booted session — asserts the clean error line is returned AND the session's actual `_game_loop` task survives and keeps dispatching a follow-up command (the pre-fix repro: the task silently dies and the session goes permanently dark). No schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean; only `parser/commands.py` touched — `combat_commands.py`/space/housing/content files untouched. Targeted gate green: new file (10) + `test_command_telemetry`/`test_t321_admin_command_access_invariant`/`test_qa_highs_batch2_2026_06_20`/`test_qa_postship_fixes`/`test_combat_dead_hooks_reachable`/`test_qa_sweep2_bacta_death_2026_06_22` (55) + the full `test_command_*`/palette/telemetry-rollup/npe/pm2 cluster (184), all green.
