### 2026-07-02 — Richer space-anomaly engagement + untrained-roll fix — *drop space-anomaly-richer-flavor*
Brian 2026-07-02 ("I don't want a simple system — make it more engaging"): `course anomaly <id>` resolved every non-derelict type with one flat roll, and — a real bug — cache/mynock/pirates passed a governing-ATTRIBUTE name (technical/mechanical) where a skill SLUG was expected, so `_get_skill_pool` couldn't find the skill and the check silently rolled untrained dice. `parser/space_commands.py`: `_ANOMALY_ENGAGE` now carries a per-type `mechanic` and every skill is a real `data/skills.yaml` slug; `_engage_anomaly` branches on it. **distress** = a Perception ambush read (`search`, Easy 10). **cache** = a two-step gate — hold the approach (`space transports`, Easy 10) THEN crack the seal (`security`, Moderate 15), both must pass (solo rolls both; a crewed pilot+engineer can split them). **mynock** = an Easy piloting detach (`space transports`, 8) whose FAILURE now knocks a working ship system to `damaged` (new `_damage_random_system`, mirrors the hazard-table path; repair via `+ship/repair`/spacedock). **imperial (Republic dead drop)** = a Slicing decode (`computer programming/repair`, Difficult 20). **pirates** = a `starship gunnery` skirmish (Moderate 15) whose victory DROPS A SALVAGEABLE WRECK (`add_wreck_anomaly`) — the reward is the salvage per the readout, so pirates no longer pays a flat faucet. Also normalized the engage path off a per-call `SkillRegistry().load_file()` onto the cached `get_cached_skill_registry()`. The full multi-hostile INTERACTIVE combat (pirates + an actual era-clean patrol spawn on imperial-failure) is a genuine scope+feel fork — **deferred and logged as `SPACE.anomaly_combat_live_tick_vs_skirmish`** (registering the dormant space-combat AI tick, and combat feel, are Brian's call). `data/guides/Guide_24_Encounters_Hazards.md` §4 gains an "Engaging an anomaly" subsection + a `course anomaly <id>` command-table row; its phantom-guard flipped (`course anomaly` must now be present; `investigate <anomaly_id>` stays out as a wilderness verb). New `tests/test_space_anomaly_richer_resolution_2026_07_02.py` (8: mechanic shape, the slug-resolves-to-a-trained-pool guard that catches the attribute-vs-slug bug, `_damage_random_system` flip + all-damaged no-op, faucet payout range + crit ceiling); `tests/test_fork_space_anomaly_engage_2026_06_23.py` tightened (mechanic-aware spec + attribute-name-is-not-a-slug guard). Targeted 31 green. NOTE (deferred to the guides-accuracy pass): Guide_24's hazard section still says hazard debuffs have "no cure path" — stale since the 2026-06-23 cure (drink verb + 1200s decay).

### 2026-07-02 — Comlink is planet-scoped + 4 design calls resolved — *drop decisions-comlink-hazard-anomaly*
Resolves `COMM.comlink_not_planet_scoped` (Brian 2026-07-02, Option A): `comlink` now rides the planetary comm net instead of broadcasting galaxy-wide. `server/channels.py broadcast_comlink` takes `db`/`sender_planet` and filters recipients to the sender's planet — the sender always hears their own echo, and an off-planet sender (deep space / ship in transit, `sender_planet=None`) reaches only themselves (comlink is the ground channel; space uses the cockpit `comm`). `db=None` degrades to the legacy unfiltered broadcast so existing harnesses stay green. `parser/channel_commands.py ComlinkCommand` derives the sender's planet via `engine.housing._planet_for_room` and passes it through. Fixed a latent blind spot in that shared helper: the six non-prefixed Coruscant districts (senate_district, jedi_temple, monumental_district, commercial_district, southern_underground, entertainment_district) and Kuat's `kdy_orbital_ring` resolved to `None` (lumped in with deep space) — added `_NONPREFIXED_ZONE_PLANET` (sourced from each planet YAML's `planet:` declaration) so Coruscant no longer fragments; additive (only affects zones that previously returned None), which also closes the same gap for housing's "rent from here". `data/guides/Guide_21_Channels_Mail_News.md` reverted from galaxy-wide to planet-wide framing (13 touchpoints, v1.1→v1.2). Two guide-authoritative guards inverted (`test_comlink_is_planet_scoped`, `test_guide_comlink_is_planet_wide`); new `tests/test_fork_comlink_planet_scoped_2026_07_02.py` (4: same-planet-only delivery, Coruscant districts share a planet, off-planet sender echoes only, db=None legacy). Targeted suite 49 green. ALSO logged Brian's other 2026-07-02 design-call resolutions (moved pending→resolved_recent, pending 11→7): `EVENT.communal_rework_staged_scenarios`=PRE-LAUNCH (implementation lane = the parallel main session, its declared next-focus); `ENV.hazard_debuff_no_cure_path`=verified ALREADY CURED 2026-06-23 (drink verb + 1200s decay + double-scale fix; stale-OPEN closed); `SPACE.anomaly_engagement_mostly_unwired`=verified ALREADY BUILT 2026-06-23 (a49f3e0), with a Brian-directed richer-flavor follow-up rework landing as a separate drop.

### 2026-07-02 — Launch-critical: per-droid lock closes two vendor-droid credit-integrity races (item duplication + minted credits) — *drop vendor-droid-concurrency-lock*
A break-it QA pass (real per-session asyncio game loops + `asyncio.gather` via `tests/harness.py`) found two DETERMINISTIC corruption races in `engine/vendor_droids.py`. Root cause: `sell_to_droid`, `buy_from_droid`, and `collect_escrow` each mutate a vendor droid's shared JSON `data` blob (stock quantities, buy-order `qty_filled`/`escrow_deposited`, `escrow_credits`) via a read → await(credits/inventory mutation) → write-back sequence with NO per-object lock. **#1** two concurrent `sell <resource> to <shop>` against a buy order funded for exactly 1 unit both succeeded and both got paid — 1000cr paid against a 500cr escrow (credits minted). **#2** two concurrent `buy <item> from <shop>` against a qty=1 stock slot both succeeded — the item duplicated while escrow/`total_sales` reflected only one sale. **Fix (additive, no schema change):** a module-level per-droid `asyncio.Lock` registry (`_droid_locks` + `_get_droid_lock(droid_id)`) now wraps the critical section — from the droid `data` read through the final `db.update_object` write-back — of all three functions, so a waiting task re-reads the POST-write state (stock/escrow already consumed) instead of the stale pre-write snapshot. Serializes ops on the SAME droid only; different droids never contend. All existing single-request behavior + the `adjust_credits` funnel calls unchanged. Also added `reset_droid_locks()` + wired it into `tests/harness.py`'s per-class boot (same class of gotcha as the existing `world_events._manager`/`reset_bounty_board()` test-isolation resets — a Lock left bound to a previous class-scoped harness's now-closed event loop collided with a same-numbered droid in a fresh temp DB, surfaced by the new concurrency tests themselves). New `tests/test_vendor_droid_concurrency_2026_07_02.py` (3): both named races reproduced end-to-end through the real player-facing command path (`buy ... from <shop>` / `sell ... to <shop>`) on two independent sessions fired via `asyncio.gather`, asserting exactly one side wins, the other is cleanly refused, payout never exceeds escrow, stock never goes negative, and `qty_filled`/`escrow_credits`/`total_sales` stay internally consistent; plus a bonus direct-call test pinning the same fix on `collect_escrow`. Existing vendor/shop/economy suites green (103 + 9 smoke EE + 57 housing/economy-validation, no regressions). No schema, no faucet/sink change, era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched.

### 2026-07-02 — Drowned Choir becomes a playable staged scenario (Nar Shaddaa) — 4th of 5 cults converted — *drop drowned-choir-scenario*
Generalizes the events-as-playable-scenarios pattern to the **Drowned Choir** (Nar Shaddaa undercity) — the 4th cult (after Hollow Sun / Ember Court / Ashen Hand) to move from the legacy menace-counter to a 3-stage site scenario, mirroring the shipped cults with NO new engine code. **Bespoke venue:** a new `data/worlds/clone_wars/wilderness/nar_shaddaa_drowned_sublevels.yaml` region (4 landmarks: Choir's Ingress / Runoff Warrens / Patrons' Cistern / Drowned Sanctum) reusing the `nar_shaddaa_undercity` zone, reachable via a `down`/`climb` edge off the existing `undercity_depths` room (**zero edits** to `nar_shaddaa.yaml` — pinned by a test). **Stages** (mirror Ashen Hand): Stage 1 combat `drowned_choir_runoff_assault` (raid the flooded sub-levels) → Stage 2 skill `drowned_choir_patron_exposure` (primary `security` / secondary `investigation` + `alt_skills` `[persuasion,con,bargain]` — expose the patrons/turn the desperate) → Stage 3 boss `drowned_choir_choirmaster`. Reward bands **copied byte-for-byte** from the ashen_hand templates (balance-neutral; pinned by an equality test). `engine/staged_event.py` gains `DROWNED_CHOIR_STAGES` + `STAGED_CULTS`/`STAGED_CULT_REGION` entries; `engine/wilderness_anomalies.py` +3 templates; `era.yaml` +2 additive `content_refs`; 3 ambient hostiles in a new `npcs_drop_drowned_choir_scenario.yaml`. Guide_26 §3 moves the Drowned Choir from the menace bullet to the staged bullet. The boss deliberately **omits** `chain_enemy_template` (a staged-cult boss has no consumer for it — a dead field; omission pinned, anti-phantom). New `tests/test_drowned_choir_staged_scenario_2026_07_02.py` (25): staged-descriptor contracts, reward-band equality, region-load + reachability + no-planet-edit, full orchestrator walk (arm → clear 3 stages → win → rep). Pre-existing "3 staged cults" pins (`test_fork_event_staged` / `test_guide_26_cult_uprising_reverify` / `test_anomaly_social_alt_skills`) reconciled to the new 4-cult reality. Era-clean, WEG R&E D6, funnel-routed, `force_sensitive` derived, world-YAML additive-only.

### 2026-07-02 — Guide_26 §3 cult-rout reward corrected to the engine's real three tiers (guides accuracy pass) — *drop guide26-reward-tier-accuracy* (Opus loop)
GUIDES authoritative-accuracy lane. The `hollow-sun-tuning` win-capstone (b00bc4c) added a **1000cr credit bounty + a relic** to the cult-rout win and updated Guide_26 §3 to say so — but the rewritten reward paragraph read that *"everyone who did a meaningful share of the fight also collects a credit bounty,"* implying the bounty reaches a **broader** set than the commemorative status flag. Verified against HEAD it does not: in `engine/communal_objective_runtime._distribute_rewards` the capstone credit fires only inside `if capstone > 0 and CO.earns_title(pts, total, won=True)` — the **same** `>= title_share_threshold` (≥10% share) predicate that gates the status flag — so the flag and the bounty go to the *identical* set (the fight-carriers), while only Republic **rep** is universal (every scorer gets `REP_FLOOR`=3 → `REP_MAX`=15 by share) and only the single **top** contributor gets the relic. The guide's phrasing under-taught the ≥10%-share bar and risked a small contributor expecting 1000cr they will not receive. **Fix (docs-only, engine READ-ONLY, era-clean, no schema/faucet/sink):** §3's reward paragraph now teaches the reward in **three explicit tiers** — universal rep (show-up), the status-flag-**and**-bounty pair to "the same people … the ones who did the heavy lifting, not everyone who threw a single strike," and the top-contributor relic. New `tests/test_guide_26_reward_tier_accuracy_2026_07_02.py` (5) pins the corrected contract from BOTH sides: the engine (a 5%-share contributor earns rep but NOT `earns_title`; a 60%-share one earns both; the capstone block is gated on `earns_title`) and the prose (the over-broad phrasing is gone; the flag+bounty are tied to the "same people"; rep band 3/15 + relic retained) — so an engine change making the capstone universal, or a guide re-drift, fails loudly here. Existing `test_guide_26_cult_uprising_reverify.py` (credit bounty / relic / 3 / 15 assertions) stays green.

### 2026-07-02 — Advertised social route now actually rolls: `alt_skills` on the 3 staged-cult skill stages — *drop anomaly-social-alt-skills* (Opus loop)
Closes the deferred follow-up named at the end of the `hollow-sun-tuning` entry (and HANDOFF_events_playable_fix_tune_2026-07-02 §"Deferred quick follow-ups" #1). The three staged-cult **skill** stages advertised a social route in BOTH their `long_desc` and the `staged_event` stage `skills[]` pool — Hollow Sun "turn the moisture farms (persuasion / con)", Ember Court "rally the conscripted hive-laborers (persuasion / con)", Ashen Hand "turn the level-dwellers (persuasion / con / bargain)" — but `_resolve_anomaly_skill` only ever rolled `primary_skill`/`secondary_skill` via `_pick_better_skill` (two skills). A face who was TOLD they could resolve the stage got rolled on a slicer skill they had 0D in (their best of `security`/`computer_programming`), silently failing the advertised path. **Fix (additive, opt-in per template, backward-compatible):** each of the 3 `resolution:"skill"` scenario templates gains an `alt_skills` list — `hollow_sun_cistern_slice`/`ember_court_relay_slice` → `[persuasion, con, bargain]`; `ashen_hand_informant_turn` (primary `persuasion`, secondary `investigation`) → `[con, bargain, security]` — chosen to make each template's ROLLABLE set (primary ∪ secondary ∪ alt) a superset of the staged_event stage's advertised `skills[]`. `_resolve_anomaly_skill` now builds `[primary, secondary, *alt_skills]` and picks the first the character has trained via the already-tested `_pick_best_of_skills` (role substitution — NOT dice min-maxing; a char trained in the primary still rolls it). With no `alt_skills` present the candidate list is `[primary, secondary]`, which `_pick_best_of_skills` resolves byte-identically to the old `_pick_better_skill` (pinned by a test) — every other skill anomaly (Dune-Sea etc.) is untouched. All skills roll against the same `TIER1_RESOLUTION_DC`, so this is balance-neutral (it enables a path, it does not lower a difficulty). Schema comment block documents the optional field; `_pick_better_skill` is retained for its 4 direct unit tests. New `tests/test_anomaly_social_alt_skills_2026_07_02.py` (8): a persuasion-only face resolves Hollow Sun via `persuasion`; a slicer still rolls `security` (primary wins); an ashen-hand security-only char resolves via the alt; an untrained char falls back to primary; the helper back-compat equivalence; and a **cross-surface invariant** — every skill each staged skill stage advertises IS rollable by the template it maps to (guards future drift/renames). No schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean, `force_sensitive` untouched, `engine/combat.py` not touched. Targeted gate green (new + full `test_syn7a_wilderness_anomalies` = 73 pass).

### 2026-07-02 — Hollow Sun playable-scenario tuning: ~6h staged menace clock + a real win capstone (Brian's calls) — *drop hollow-sun-tuning*
Post-validation tuning of the now-completable Cult of the Hollow Sun (and the staged-event system), on Brian's two decisions from the live break-it findings. **(1) ~6h staged menace window.** A STAGED cult's menace is a ONE-WAY FAILURE TIMER — staged strikes never push it down (`communal_objective_runtime.record_strike`), so the window is purely `(MENACE_MAX-MENACE_START)/rate`. At the legacy 0.35/min that auto-lost in **~3.1h** while the 48h `DEADLINE_HOURS` never bound (dead code for staged). Brian chose a "one session" **~6h** window → new `STAGED_MENACE_PER_MINUTE = 0.18` (tunable `communal.staged_menace_per_minute`) + a `staged_menace_per_minute()` accessor; `advance_menace()` gains an optional `per_minute` arg (default = the legacy `menace_per_minute()`), and the escalation tick (`advance_and_resolve`) passes the staged rate ONLY when `is_staged(cult.key)`. **The legacy strike-path cults are untouched** (they keep 0.35/min, where players push menace DOWN). **(2) A real win capstone.** The headline rout paid **0 credits** (Republic rep + a title flag only), and each stage's anomaly loot ran LOWER than a standalone anomaly — so a rare 3-stage win never felt worth it. Brian chose a "bigger" capstone: new `WIN_CAPSTONE_CREDITS = 1000` (tunable `communal.win_capstone_credits`) paid through the metered `adjust_credits` faucet to **every title-earning contributor** (≥10% share), plus a **one-off invented cult relic** (era-clean; `_CAPSTONE_LOOT` per cult, top contributor only) into inventory. Bounded + rare (one uprising per ~6h) — the general economy is the sink, exactly as for the existing anomaly / questline reward faucets (a Brian-sanctioned reward faucet). **(3) Boss difficulty:** Brian confirmed the Stage-3 group-finale as-is — no change. New `tests/test_hollow_sun_tuning_2026_07_02.py` (8): staged rate < legacy + ~6h window + `advance_menace` default/clamp/negative-guard; capstone amount + title-earner payout + top-contributor relic + zero-capstone disable. Existing communal/events suite green (85 pass incl. the playable-scenario walk). No schema; funnel-routed (`adjust_credits`); era-clean; `force_sensitive` untouched. **DEFERRED to a small follow-up:** wiring the advertised persuasion/con "turn the farmers" fallback into `hollow_sun_cistern_slice` (needs an `alt_skills` add to the shared anomaly skill resolver), and the choppy wave→wave re-engage polish.

### 2026-07-02 — Combat anomalies clear on DEFEAT, not only DEATH — the Hollow Sun (and every combat anomaly) were unwinnable — *drop anomaly-defeat-clear*
A live break-it playthrough of the **Cult of the Hollow Sun playable scenario** found its combat stages could **not** be completed through the documented `attack` loop. Combat ends the instant the last hostile can no longer act (`CombatInstance.is_over` → `active_combatants` → `can_act_now`, i.e. `>= INCAPACITATED(4)`), and the post-round room/combat cleanup vanishes the downed NPC at that same threshold — but the anomaly kill-hook in `parser/combat_commands.py::_apply_combat_wear` only fired the clear/phase-advance at `>= DEAD(6)`. Since a finishing blow rarely one-shots straight to DEAD, the last zealot was incapacitated, removed from the room, and the anomaly's `spawned_npc_ids` never emptied — the stage stuck at 0/N forever and `attack <name>` returned "you don't see them here" (reproduced 6/8 kills across two characters). Because Stage 1 (wave) and Stage 3 (boss) route through the generic `resolution:"combat"` path, **all three staged cults (Hollow Sun / Ember Court / Ashen Hand) and every other combat wilderness anomaly** were affected identically. **Fix (one-line gate):** the anomaly clear hook now fires at `>= INCAPACITATED` — defeat = can't-act — aligning with the game's own victory semantics (the bounty *capture* chain and combat achievements already treat incapacitation as a win). The bounty / WoW.3a kill hooks intentionally **stay** DEAD-gated (capture and not-yet-killed carry distinct meaning there); only the anomaly clear — whose SOLE completion path is this hook — moves to defeat. Root cause verified against `engine/combat.py::_cleanup` (removes only `WoundLevel.DEAD` from combat, so an incapacitated NPC remains iterable by `_apply_combat_wear`), confirming the single-point fix. New `tests/test_anomaly_defeat_clears_on_incap_2026_07_02.py` (5) drives the **real** `_apply_combat_wear` parser loop — the seam the existing tier-2 tests never exercised (they call `award_combat_anomaly_reward` directly): INCAPACITATED / MORTALLY_WOUNDED / DEAD each advance the stage; STUNNED / HEALTHY correctly do **not** (guards against over-lowering the gate). Full existing tier-2 engine suite green — no regression. No schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean.

### 2026-07-02 — Guide_16 §12/§16 reconciled to the fun15 reflex commands (guides re-verify) — *drop guide16-reflex-commands-reverify* (Opus loop)
GUIDES re-verify lane. The fun15 drop (`9a3f083`) turned the words a newcomer reflexively types — `goals` / `situation` / `list` — into first-class TEXT-STATUS commands (and `e0dbcfb`/fun13 made `inventory`/`inv`/`i` a real `+inv` alias, `presence` a `+who` alias). That shipped AFTER Guide_16's last pass, leaving a **test-invisible guide drift**: §12 "What If You Get Stuck" still framed `inventory`/`situation`/`list` as *"a reflexive word a newcomer reaches for"* that the game **doesn't recognize** and answers with *"I didn't catch that."* — actively misinforming the exact newcomer the section is written for (those words now RESOLVE and answer you). Verified all six reflex forms resolve against the live registry (`goals`/`situation`/`list`/`inventory`/`inv`/`i`/`presence`). Reconciled Guide_16 (docs-only, engine READ-ONLY, era-clean, no schema/faucet/sink): **§12 intro** now splits into two safety nets — the reflex words are *real commands that answer you* (each prints the same data the web sidebars show, as plain text for telnet+web), and the *"I didn't catch that"* recovery is reserved for genuinely-unrecognized input (a typo `atatck` / a plain-English question); **§12 recovery path #1** teaches the reflex status commands (`goals`/`situation`/`list`/`inventory`/`presence`) as no-exact-syntax status tools; **§16 quick-ref** gains rows for each. Also fixed the now-stale `TestNLConfusionRedirectTeaching` (its comment + one assertion still claimed those reflex words were *"inputs the recovery catches"*), and added `TestReflexStatusCommandTeaching` (6): the guide teaches them as real commands, the stale "unrecognized" framing stays dead, each producer resolves against the registry, and Goals/Situation still read the sidebar data (so a revert fails loudly). 96 guide-16 tests green (was 90).

### 2026-07-01 — Zone-keyed ambient audio system (off by default, silent until CC0 loops added) — *drop fun16-sound-system*
Resolves the build half of `UX.sound_atmosphere` (Brian: "source CC0 loops, embed" — I built the plug-and-play system; the CC0 loop files are Brian's to source, since I can't download/generate audio). Pure client (no server change — `zone_type` is already on the HUD/room payload). `static/client.html`: a `ZONE_AUDIO` map (`zone_type`→track: cantina/spaceport/market/deep-space/city; unmapped→silence) + an ambient manager that CROSSFADES the looped track when the room's zone changes (hooked in `handleHudUpdate` + `handleSpaceState` where `data.zone_type` is already read) at a low ambient volume. FULLY FAIL-SAFE: load wrapped in try/catch + `.play()` promise `.catch()`-swallowed, so a missing file (404) or a browser autoplay block is SILENT — never a console error or crash (this is the shipping state today: no audio files present). OFF by default (`localStorage sw_ambient_enabled`, absent=off; `sw_ambient_volume` default 0.5), toggled by a new `SOUND` button (mirrors the Drop 8 sheet toggles); autoplay-policy-correct — audio only starts on the user's toggle gesture, never on page load. `static/audio/README.md` documents the expected files (one CC0 .ogg per track basename) + the zone map so the loops are drop-in. New `tests/spa/test_fun16_ambient_audio.py` (20: map + manager present, toggle defaults OFF, load path guarded); full spa suite 983 green, client-wireup unbroken. This completes all four of Brian's 2026-07-01 forks (fun13 inventory aliases, fun14 vibroaxe band, fun15 text commands, fun16 sound; sheet-delta=keep-per-view, shop_verb=deferred to the command-syntax-rework).

### 2026-07-01 — Reflex words become real commands: `goals` / `situation` / `list` / `presence` — *drop fun15-text-status*
Brian's decision (2026-07-01) on the fun13 follow-up: the reflex words a new player types that were web PANELS with no backing command (`goals`, `situation`, `presence`) or resolved to the wrong thing (`list`) become real TEXT-STATUS commands, working for telnet AND web, reusing existing data (no new producers). Four commands in `parser/builtin_commands.py`: **`goals`** prints the active questline + accepted mission + claimed bounty as text (the same data the GOALS sidebar uses via `get_questline_status` + the mission/bounty boards — the fun11 accepted-mission logic, no visibility filter); **`situation`** (no `sit` alias — `sit` stays `join`; the alias was dropped for prefix-collision safety, this entry's original "(alias `sit`)" corrected 2026-07-03 per Fable F3) prints the active world-events via `get_world_event_manager().get_status()` ("The sector is quiet right now." when none); **`list`** prints the in-room `ai_config.vendor` NPC's buyable stock — the exact `vendor_stocked` catalog `buy <item>` sells, so list and buy agree (helpful no-vendor line otherwise); **`presence`** is registered as an alias of `+who`. All PLAYER-level, fail-safe, era-clean; no faucet/sink/schema. Collision-checked (none in the command-syntax-rework's DELETED_EXACT_FORMS; `list` only existed as a `trade list` subcommand arg). New `tests/test_fun15_text_status.py` (7). This completes the "reflex words don't dead-end" thread: fun12 made them helpful, fun13 aliased the inventory family, fun15 makes the panel/vendor reflexes actually work.

### 2026-07-01 — Winnability band admits melee foils: the vibroaxe is accepted, main re-greened — *drop fun14-vibroaxe-band*
Resolves the design-call `BAL.condemned_hull_melee_foil_band` (Brian: "accept the vibroaxe + widen the band"), which the OpusLoop had turned into a hard gate-red: `test_questline_foil_winnability_band` (loop-added) was entirely blaster-centric and rejected The Condemned Hull's melee foil **Bohrus Kang** (Houk enforcer, vibroaxe) — reading his deliberately-low blaster (2D+2) as his to-hit (below the floor + blew the cluster spread) and barring his melee weapon. Fix (TEST-only, no foil nerf — Bohrus is already in-band on his melee stats: melee_combat 4D ≤ 5D ceiling, STR 3D+1 ≤ 4D ceiling, brawling 4D+1): the guard is now weapon-type-aware — a melee-weapon foil is judged on its `melee_combat` to-hit (new `_primary_to_hit` helper), its weapon admitted via a new `ALLOWED_MELEE` set (vibroaxe + common melee), and the to-hit cluster is computed on the effective attack skill. The melee-foil winnability model was documented as a "ranged advantage" (blaster + kite + dodge while the foil must close) — **CORRECTED 2026-07-03 per Fable F2: that kite premise does NOT exist in the engine.** Ground combat opens at SHORT range round 1, there is no closing cost or advance/retreat verb, and dodge defends *ranged* only (melee is opposed vs melee/brawling parry a blaster build hasn't raised). The **stat-band acceptance still stands** (Bohrus is in-band on his melee stats), but it rests on the stat band, not a kite that can't happen; on his numbers a landed vibroaxe swing mean-margins to Incapacitated, so whether The Condemned Hull is genuinely fresh-char-winnable is a **telemetry question** (`@balance chains` start→complete funnel vs its 34 siblings; if it lags, the parked lever is a `blaster_pistol` foil one-liner — NOT vibroblade, same Incap-per-hit; a real melee-foil *class* would need a range-band verb, separate design). The vibroaxe is NOT gated by a no-one-shot bound (that would reject the vibroaxe the call accepts). All 10 winnability tests green. NOTE: also caught+fixed a separate loop-red this cluster — a field-kit wound-ladder canary whose over-broad regex false-matched scattered tokens (drop 569246d).

### 2026-07-01 — G5: live NPC wound state in the web HERE panel — *drop g5-npc-wound-here-panel* (Opus loop)
T3.17/T3.18 **G5 server-half + client**: surface each live hostile NPC's wound state in the web client's HERE panel (`room_contents`), so a player can see **at a glance which mob is nearly down** in a multi-enemy fight — the combat feed narrates the blow-by-blow; this is the persistent readout. The lane's enumerated standing quality work (guides authoritative passes, command-syntax rework, telemetry read-side integrity, T3.21 LOW/MED tail) is **verified exhausted + guarded at HEAD** this fire, and the primary T3.24 lane is **paused** pending Brian's `QUEST.t3_24_36th_arc_skill_pool_exhausted` design call — so this fire took the last bounded, in-lane, invariant-clean feature item. **Producer** (`server/session.py`): new pure `_npc_wound_map(combat)` READ-ONLY marshalling helper reads the SAME live combat instance `_hud_room_contents` already fetches for the `in_combat`/flee affordance (no `combat.py`/`parser` mutation — the established `_active_combats` read pattern), keyed by `combatant.id == char.id ==` the NPC's DB row id (`add_combatant` stores `combatants[char.id]`); `_hud_room_contents` stamps `wound_level` (0–6) + `wound_name` (the canonical HUD label map, reused from the player readout) onto each `room_contents.npcs` entry. NPCs not in a live fight default to `0`/`healthy` (NPC damage isn't persisted to the DB row). **Consumer** (`static/client.html`): the HERE-panel NPC render adds a `.here-wound` badge, gated on `wound_level > 0`, with stun/hurt/crit severity buckets escalating the colour — no phantom producer (both halves land together). Best-effort throughout: a combat-internals hiccup degrades to no badge and never crashes the HUD push. New `tests/test_g5_npc_wound_in_room_contents.py` (14): the pure helper (id-keying, non-NPC exclusion, Healthy→0, dead-in-teardown, char=None skip, broken-combat→{}, str-id coercion) against the real `WoundLevel` enum, the shared `_wound_name` labels, and static-parse consumer checks (client reads `wound_level`/`wound_name`, renders the badge + severity buckets gated on `>0`, CSS rule present). Marshalling + docs/test only: **no schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean, engine READ-ONLY**; `engine/combat.py` untouched (avoid-lane — read only). Targeted gate green (89: new + adjacent SPA/HUD sidebar panels + smart-buttons + npc-desc + scene-context + hygiene + smoke-foundation).

### 2026-07-01 — T3.24 generalized-questline lane reaches its skill-pool boundary — the 36th arc is a genuine design fork, escalated not guessed — *no code; design-call log only*
The T3.24 generalized-quest expansion lane has shipped **35** accessible `kind: questline` arcs (through "The Cleared Fault" / `blaster`+`bowcaster`, 88e3730) and has now **exhausted every clean attribute skill pool**. A HEAD skill sweep of `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (tallying every `skill:` field across all questline steps) confirms only four chain skills remain unused — `dodge`, `brawling parry`, `melee parry` (reactive defenses a proactive prove-the-racket `skill_check` gates awkwardly) and `lightsaber` (era-sensitive: Jedi/Sith weapon vs. civilian racket-bust arcs) — while `blaster`, `bowcaster`, and the three capital-ship skills are each now used exactly once. STRENGTH/KNOWLEDGE/PERCEPTION/TECHNICAL/MECHANICAL/DEXTERITY pools are all spent. Any 36th arc would therefore either repeat an existing skill spread (violating the lane's fresh-spread invariant) or force a reactive/lightsaber skill — a design decision, not a guess. Per the drop workflow (step 5: "do not guess — log it and ask"), logged the fork to `TODO.json` → `design_calls_pending_brian` as **`QUEST.t3_24_36th_arc_skill_pool_exhausted`** (options A–E; recommended default: wind down T3.24 as complete at 35 arcs, or add one civilian big-ship venue room to unlock the last structurally-fresh combat-arc shape). **No engine, world-YAML, chain, or content change** — TODO.json + this CHANGELOG note only; the lane is **paused pending Brian's decision** and the loop should pivot to the standing quality lane meanwhile. Premise verified at HEAD 88e3730.

### 2026-07-01 — "The Cleared Fault" — the thirty-fifth accessible questline (T3.24 generalized-quest expansion): a suppressed-defect / faked-recall product-safety cover-up on personal weapons in Coruscant's Invisible Sector, on a thirty-fifth distinct skill spread (the first personal-weapons range-master build — blaster + bowcaster) — *drop generalized-questline-cleared-fault*
T3.24 **generalized quest expansion**, the **thirty-fifth** accessible (non-t5, non-tutorial) `kind: questline` chain, after the prior thirty-four (… Papered Refit / Blank Ticket). It reuses the **live questline engine** (`engine/tutorial_chains.py` + `chain_events.py`: the `kind: questline` type + `active_questline` slot, the existing event types, the four reward funnels) with **NO new engine code**, no new slot/event type, no schema migration, **no new reward faucet**, and **NO map-coordinate or exit-graph change** — the sanctioned right-of-launch widening per `quest_expansion_postlaunch_path_v1.md`. **Firsts:** a **thirty-fifth distinct skill spread — `blaster` + `bowcaster` (both Dexterity)** — neither used by any prior arc as a chain skill — the **first accessible questline to reward the PERSONAL-WEAPONS RANGE-MASTER / GUNSMITH-PROOF build**, the **first to use `blaster`** as a chain skill, and the **first to use `bowcaster`** (the two remaining clean Dexterity personal-weapon skills; a full corpus sweep confirmed neither has ever gated a `skill_check_passed` step — `blaster` and `dodge` are the FOILS' combat stats across the corpus but never CHAIN skills). *Honest framing:* **NOT a cross-attribute spread** (both Dexterity), so it is the **fourth all-Dexterity-weapon-pool spread**, after The Empty Proof (firearms + thrown), The Paper Death (running + melee combat), and The Cold Charge (grenade + missile weapons), and **NOT the first weapon arc**; what is genuinely first is the two personal-firearm skills and the **prove-the-fault-is-live** pairing on personal weapons — run a "recall-cleared" blaster hot on the range to trip the chamber/actuator fault the recall swears was fixed, and span a "recall-cleared" bowcaster to full draw to show the riser/limb fault the recall swears was fixed still fractures. Unlike the exhausted big-ship Mechanical/Technical tail, a personal-weapon pair needs **no special venue** and **can take the standard combat step** (unlike the 34th's no-combat capital-ship arc). **Racket (new story shape):** a **suppressed-defect / faked-recall product-safety cover-up on personal weapons** — the first accessible arc whose racket is knowingly selling a lethal manufacturing defect, forging the recall-compliance record that swears the defect was re-worked out, and burying the buyers' injury reports. A civilian arms retailer (Riftline Arms & Import, proprietor Ostra Vell) sells personal blasters and bowcasters to the Invisible Sector's dockworkers, guards, caravan hands, and hunters; a whole production lot carries a known lethal defect (a blaster gas-chamber/actuating-block fault that over-pressures and bursts, a bowcaster riser/limb lamination fault that fractures at full draw), and when the injuries came back Riftline forged a recall-compliance certificate swearing every unit re-worked, kept selling the untouched defective stock as "recall-cleared," and buried the injury reports so the arms-standards board never saw a pattern — so a guard who paid for a safe sidearm draws on a raider and has it burst instead of fire. **Distinct** from The Empty Proof (an independent PROOF-HOUSE SKIPS test-firing third-party arms of unknown quality — an omission; here the SELLER KNOWS the exact defect, fakes the recall that swears it FIXED, and buries the victims), The Rigged Issue (substitutes counterfeits; here genuine stock with a real defect), The Cold Charge (fakes DESTRUCTION of ordnance; here nothing destroyed, sold openly as cleared), The Hollow Fit (guts a ship's defenses; here personal weapons), and The Fouled Sump (hides an environmental poison; here a consumer-product defect + faked recall). Busted by a **personal-weapons range-master** who takes a "recall-cleared" blaster off Riftline's own rack and runs it hot on the shop back-range until the chamber bursts (`blaster`, DC11), spans a "recall-cleared" bowcaster to full draw at the border scrap-lot against the sector wall until its riser fractures (`bowcaster`, DC13), stands off the shop's enforcer who corners the live proof (combat), and puts the two live-defective "recall-cleared" weapons beside the forged compliance certificate on the arms-standards board that voids the certification and pulls the arms license. **Fresh single-zone commercial_district cluster** (`outlander_club` giver/return / `invisec_main_street` blaster DC11 / `invisec_border` bowcaster DC13 / `mid_residential_block` combat) — another **Coruscant arc** and **3rd on the commercial_district face** (after The Sealed Ledger and The Blank Ticket), NOT a fresh face; the four-room CLUSTER is fresh to the entire corpus. Three rooms are **secured** (giver + the two firing-proof rooms host no fight — a skill check is never gated by room security); only the step-4 combat room (`mid_residential_block`, contested) must be combat-capable. **NPCs:** giver **Kesh Ordan** (Zabrak master range-safety armorer / ex-recall-bench hand, non-hostile, "Coruscant - The Outlander Club"); foil **Draven Kohl** (Weequay arms-shop enforcer, `chain_enemy_template cleared_fault_enforcer`, hostile, "Coruscant - Mid-Level Residential Block", carrying the proven `blaster_pistol` on the in-band beatable stat line — pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). Narrated-only props: proprietor Ostra Vell, the shop clerks, the manufacturer, the buyers who take the recall on faith, the maimed and dead, and the arms-standards board. **Reward band** (unchanged, self-calibrating): 300 cr graduation + 150 cr step-4 = **450 cr**, **17** Independent rep (grad 5; steps 2/2/3/3/2), a **cp_reward-3** achievement (`cleared_fault_cleared`). Files: `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (+chain block), `data/worlds/clone_wars/npcs_drop_generalized_questline_cleared_fault.yaml` (new, 2 NPCs), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 `content_refs.npcs` ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_cleared_fault.py` (new, 30-test slice). **Gate:** slice + `test_t5_questline_content` (all-questlines-walkable + reward-tier) + reachability-invariant + reward-tier + achievements + hygiene + smoke-foundation; foil-band held-out check confirms the identical 3 pre-existing `BAL.condemned_hull_melee_foil_band` (Bohrus Kang) failures, **zero new**. Era-clean, WEG D6, funnel-routed rewards, `force_sensitive` derived. Original characters, no canon figures.

### 2026-07-01 — "The Blank Ticket" — the thirty-fourth accessible questline (T3.24 generalized-quest expansion): a crew-certification / competency-ticket fraud in the Coruscant mid-city, on a thirty-fourth distinct skill spread (the first capital-ship bridge-crew / operations build — capital ship piloting + gunnery + shields), and only the second no-combat arc — *drop generalized-questline-blank-ticket*
T3.24 **generalized quest expansion**, the **thirty-fourth** accessible (non-t5, non-tutorial) `kind: questline` chain, after the prior thirty-three (… Hollow Fit / Papered Refit). It reuses the **live questline engine** (`engine/tutorial_chains.py` + `chain_events.py`: the `kind: questline` type + `active_questline` slot, the existing event types, the four reward funnels) with **NO new engine code**, no new slot/event type, no schema migration, **no new reward faucet**, and **NO map-coordinate or exit-graph change** — the sanctioned right-of-launch widening per `quest_expansion_postlaunch_path_v1.md`. **Firsts:** a **thirty-fourth distinct skill spread — `capital ship piloting` + `capital ship gunnery` + `capital ship shields` (all three Mechanical)** — none used by any prior arc — the **first accessible questline to reward the CAPITAL-SHIP BRIDGE-CREW / OPERATIONS build** (fly it, fight it, screen it), the **first to use** each of the three, the **big-ship OPERATIONS pool** sibling to the 33rd's big-ship REPAIR pool (The Papered Refit). *Honest framing:* **NOT a cross-attribute spread** (all three Mechanical) and **NOT the first ship arc** (Salted Lane / Condemned Hull / Rolled Log / Hollow Fit / Papered Refit worked ship classes first); what is genuinely first is the three **capital-ship OPERATIONS** skills, the bridge-crew / merchant-marine build, and the **run-the-three-boards** pairing — run the helm board, the gunnery board, and the shield board to master standard, on the fraud's own rigs, to prove the boards were never run on the crews it "certified." **Second structural first:** only the **SECOND accessible questline that resolves on SKILL not force** — three skill-check steps and **NO combat step**, after The Sealed Ledger (the ninth) proved the no-combat shape — and the **first no-combat arc whose three checks are all one attribute's pool** (the big-ship Mechanical operations trio). Because there is no combat step there is **no antagonist NPC and no `chain_enemy_template`**; the only placed NPC is the giver. **This sidesteps the "civilian-big-ship-ROOM problem"** entirely (the reason the big-ship pools stalled after the 33rd): a no-combat certification arc needs no contested combat room, so it runs in the **secured** civilian mid-city, and the boards are run on **leased simulator rigs** — which is how bridge crews are actually certified — not on a berth. **Racket (new story shape):** a **crew-certification / competency-ticket fraud** — the first accessible arc whose racket forges a **person's competency** rather than a ship's, a document's, or a good's. A licensed bridge-crew certification bureau (Coreward Crew Certification, board-master Sabin Orro) holds the freight lines' contract to run the mandatory competency boards a spacer must pass before a line may rate them to a capital-freighter bridge, and instead of running the boards **sells the tickets**: a cadet pays, gets a full master's rating stamped, never sits a rig, and the bureau logs an instant "pass" with **no simulator telemetry** behind it — so the lines put "certified" bridge officers who cannot run a bridge onto capital freighters and the ships founder (a bulk hauler that couldn't hold its traffic tier, a freighter whose rated gunners couldn't answer a raider pass and whose rated shield-hand folded the envelope on the first load). **Distinct** from The Sealed Ledger (its no-combat sibling — also Coruscant commercial district, also an owns-the-desk fraud beaten by skill not force — but it forges DEBT-RECORDS, busted by a thief's three hands; here it forges CREW COMPETENCY, busted by a master mariner's three boards), The Empty Proof (certifies third-party GOODS untested; here a PERSON's competency), The Bonded Crew (bonds a crew via debt; here credentials are forged, no bondage), The Papered Refit (bills for a ship's OVERHAUL never done — the REPAIR pool; here a crew's RATING never earned — the OPERATIONS pool), and The Hollow Fit (guts a ship's real defenses; here nothing is touched on any ship — the lie is entirely in the crew's paper). Busted by a **capital-ship bridge officer** who runs Coreward's master helm board on its leased loft-warehouse simulator (`capital ship piloting`, DC11), its master gunnery board at the market-arcade front it dresses as a credentials counter (`capital ship gunnery`, DC13), and its master shield board in the discreet Outlander back room — pulling its telemetry-less pass logs off the rig (`capital ship shields`, DC14) — then stands three real boards beside a book of blank ones so the freight lines and underwriters collapse every ticket and pull the unqualified crews off the capital bridges. **Fresh commercial_district cluster** (`coco_town_civic_block` giver/return / `coco_town_loft_district` DC11 / `coco_town_market_arcade` DC13 / `outlander_cantina` DC14) — the **4th Coruscant arc** and **2nd on the commercial_district face** (after The Sealed Ledger's dexters_diner / mid_transit_hub / commercial_district_main / commercial_district_atmospheric), NOT a fresh face; the four-room CLUSTER is fresh to the entire corpus. All four rooms are **secured**, which is fine — no combat step, no fight to gate. **NPC:** giver **Jenra Voll** (Duros retired capital-ship master / spacers'-board examiner, non-hostile, "Coruscant - Coco Town - Civic Block") — the **only** placed NPC. Narrated-only props: Coreward Crew Certification + board-master Sabin Orro, the cadets who buy blank tickets, the freight lines + underwriters, the "certified" crews and the freighters they founder, the lost haulers. **Reward band** (unchanged, self-calibrating): 300 cr graduation + 150 cr step-4 = **450 cr**, **17** Independent rep (grad 5; steps 2/2/3/3/2), a **cp_reward-3** achievement (`blank_ticket_cleared`). Files: `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (+chain block), `data/worlds/clone_wars/npcs_drop_generalized_questline_blank_ticket.yaml` (new, 1 NPC), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 `content_refs.npcs` ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_blank_ticket.py` (new, 27-test slice). **Gate:** slice + `test_t5_questline_content` (all-questlines-walkable + reward-tier) + reachability-invariant + reward-tier + skill-difficulty-winnability + hygiene + smoke-foundation; foil-band held-out check confirms the identical 3 pre-existing `BAL.condemned_hull_melee_foil_band` (Bohrus Kang) failures, **zero new** (this no-combat arc adds no foil). Era-clean, WEG D6, funnel-routed rewards, `force_sensitive` derived. Original character, no canon figures.

### 2026-07-01 — "The Papered Refit" — the thirty-third accessible questline (T3.24 generalized-quest expansion): a phantom-refit / survey-completion fraud at a Kuat civilian refit yard, on a thirty-third distinct skill spread (the first capital-ship / big-ship yard-tech build — capital ship repair + capital ship weapon repair) — *drop generalized-questline-papered-refit*
T3.24 **generalized quest expansion**, the **thirty-third** accessible (non-t5, non-tutorial) `kind: questline` chain, after the prior thirty-two (… Cold Charge / Hollow Fit). It reuses the **live questline engine** (`engine/tutorial_chains.py` + `chain_events.py`: the `kind: questline` type + `active_questline` slot, the existing event types, the four reward funnels) with **NO new engine code**, no new slot/event type, no schema migration, **no new reward faucet**, and **NO map-coordinate or exit-graph change** — the sanctioned right-of-launch widening per `quest_expansion_postlaunch_path_v1.md`: author MORE questlines on the proven pattern. **Firsts:** a **thirty-third distinct skill spread — `capital ship repair` (Technical) + `capital ship weapon repair` (Technical)** — neither used by any prior arc — the **first accessible questline to reward the CAPITAL-SHIP / BIG-SHIP YARD-TECH build** (open an "overhauled" capital hull to show its mandated structural rework was never done, then cycle a "serviced" point-defense to show its armament work was never done), the **first to use `capital ship repair`**, the **first to use `capital ship weapon repair`**, and the **first accessible arc set on the CAPITAL-SHIP (big-ship) class at all**. *Honest framing:* this is **NOT a cross-attribute spread** (both skills are Technical) — it is the **THIRD both-Technical spread**, after The Rigged Issue (blaster+armor repair) and The Cut Coil (repulsorlift+droid repair) — and **NOT the first ship arc** (The Salted Lane / The Condemned Hull / The Rolled Log / The Hollow Fit worked ship classes first); what is genuinely first is the two **capital-ship** skills, the big-ship class, and the prove-the-overhaul-never-happened pairing. **This resolves the standing "civilian-big-ship-ROOM problem"** flagged for the 33rd arc: Kuat's free drydocks are the *military* Venator/Acclamator ones and Nar Shaddaa's docks are used, but Kuat's Main Spaceport is explicitly the **civilian** port ("everything that isn't a warship"), so `kuat_arrivals` is a clean civilian big-ship berth — the war-coded rooms (`kdy_engineer_apartments` Republic-clearance, `kuat_ring_medical` clone-trooper, `house_andrim_office` Venator-contract) kept **OFF**. **Racket (new story shape):** a **phantom-refit / survey-completion fraud** — the first accessible arc whose racket is billing for a mandated capital-ship overhaul that was **never performed** and forging the survey that swears it was done. A civilian merchant-refit contractor (Trueline Refit & Survey, yard-master Palo Kessin) paid by bulk-freight combines to overhaul their capital freighters' hulls + point-defense books each ship in, does **no work**, forges the completed work-orders + survey certificate, bills the full fee, and returns the freighter unsound — so the haulers certified overhauled full go back to the deep lanes with hulls never renewed and point-defense never serviced. **Distinct** from The Hollow Fit (GUTS installed defenses + fences the cores — active theft; here nothing is touched, the work is simply never done), The Empty Proof (an independent proof-house certifies third-party arms untested; here the *contracted repairer* bills for the client's own ship's overhaul it never did), The Condemned Hull (seizes a *sound* ship as derelict; here the ship is genuinely *unsound* and sworn overhauled), The Rolled Log (rolls back a craft's *history*), and The Cut Coil (DAMAGES to bill a repair; here no damage, phantom labor billed). Busted by a **capital-ship yard tech** who opens the "overhauled" freighter's hull on the civilian Main Spaceport Arrivals berth and reads the mandated rework undone (`capital ship repair`, DC11) and cycles its "bench-serviced" point-defense on the yard's own test cradle in the corporate-ring survey office and reads it dead (`capital ship weapon repair`, DC13), stands off the yard's enforcer in the ring residential block (combat), and puts the phantom overhaul on the shippers' survey board that voids the certification and pulls the yard bond. **Cross-zone Kuat cluster** (`spaceport_concourse_hotel` giver/return / `kuat_arrivals` DC11 / `kdy_main_offices` DC13 / `kuat_ring_apartments` combat=contested) — the **5th Kuat arc**; the `kdy_orbital_ring` face is NOT fresh (Skimmed Line/Condemned Hull), the four-room CLUSTER is (precedented by The Empty Proof's / The Hollow Fit's cross-zone assemblies). **NPCs:** giver **Ormo Delth** (Mon Calamari master shipwright / capital-ship refit inspector, non-hostile, "Kuat - Spaceport Concourse Hotel"); foil **Vodran Sekk** (Quarren yard enforcer, `chain_enemy_template papered_refit_enforcer`, "Kuat Drive Yards - Ring Residential Block", `blaster_pistol` in the proven beatable band — pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). Narrated-only props: yard-master Palo Kessin, the survey clerk, the combines/shippers, the underwriters + survey board, the "overhauled" bulk freighter, the deep-lane raiders. **Reward band** (unchanged, self-calibrating): 300 cr graduation + 150 cr step-4 = **450 cr**, **17** Independent rep (grad 5; steps 2/2/3/3/2), a **cp_reward-3** achievement (`papered_refit_cleared`). ### 2026-07-01 — "The Hollow Fit" — the thirty-second accessible questline (T3.24 generalized-quest expansion): a hollow-fit / defensive-systems fraud at a Mos Eisley ship-outfitter, on a thirty-second distinct skill spread (the first ship defensive-systems technician build — starship weapon repair + starship shields) — *drop generalized-questline-hollow-fit*
T3.24 **generalized quest expansion**, the **thirty-second** accessible (non-t5, non-tutorial) `kind: questline` chain, after the prior thirty-one (… Rolled Log / Paper Death / Cold Charge). It reuses the **live questline engine** (`engine/tutorial_chains.py` + `chain_events.py`: the `kind: questline` type + `active_questline` slot, the existing event types, the four reward funnels) with **NO new engine code**, no new slot/event type, no schema migration, **no new reward faucet**, and **NO map-coordinate or exit-graph change** — the sanctioned right-of-launch widening per `quest_expansion_postlaunch_path_v1.md`: author MORE questlines on the proven pattern. **Firsts:** a **thirty-second distinct skill spread — `starship weapon repair` (Technical) + `starship shields` (Mechanical)** — neither used by any prior arc — the **first accessible questline to reward the SHIP DEFENSIVE-SYSTEMS TECHNICIAN build** (strip a "fitted" cannon mount to show its firing circuit is dead, then run a "full-rated" deflector fit up to show it folds), the **first to use `starship weapon repair`**, and the **first to use `starship shields`**. *Honest framing:* this **IS a cross-attribute spread** (Technical + Mechanical) but **NOT the first Mechanical+Technical cross** — The Condemned Hull, The Long Haul, and The Rolled Log crossed those before it; what is genuinely first is the two skills and the **prove-the-fit-is-hollow** pairing. *Memory-correction:* `starship shields` and `starship weapon repair` are **SMALL-SHIP skills** (the "starship" class, sibling to space transports / starfighter), **NOT the big-ship capital-ship pool** the room-wall memory feared — so the arc works a light freighter in a civilian docking pit and **sidesteps the civilian-big-ship-room problem** the standing note flagged for the 32nd arc; distinct from The Rolled Log's STARFIGHTER pilot/mechanic (airframe/drive) and The Salted Lane's `space transports` + `starship gunnery` (pilot/gunner). Set on **Tatooine**, spanning the **`tatooine_cantina`** and **`tatooine_spaceport`** zones on a **fresh four-room CROSS-ZONE cluster** (`chalmuans_cantina_entrance` / `chalmuans_cantina_back_hallway` / `docking_bay_94_entrance` / `spaceport_hotel`), every room fresh to the chain corpus; *honest discipline:* NEITHER face is fresh (The Crooked Wheel worked Chalmun's main bar; The Sabotaged Run / The Short Weight / The Rolled Log worked the spaceport), so this is the **eleventh Tatooine arc**, **NOT a fresh face** and **NOT a single-zone cluster** — it is a **cross-zone assembly** (precedented by The Empty Proof's `tatooine_outskirts` + `tatooine_jundland`); what is fresh is the four-room **cluster**. Only the step-4 combat room must be combat-capable (`spaceport_hotel` defaults to contested); the giver/return and two skill rooms host no fight. **New story shape:** breaking a **HOLLOW-FIT / DEFENSIVE-SYSTEMS FRAUD** — the **first accessible arc whose racket is selling a ship a hollow shell of the defenses it paid for and fencing its real teeth to the predators**. A ship-defense outfitter (Kestrel Defensive Outfitters, run by the proprietor Bevin Torr), paid by the independent traders, moisture-farm supply-haulers, and small caravan freighters to fit and certify their light freighters' deflector shields and hull-mount cannons against the raider-thick supply lanes, instead installs **gutted units** (generators with the capacitor banks pulled, cannon mounts with the emitter drawn and a dead slug fitted), certifies them **full-rated** on forged fit-sheets, pockets the gap, and fences the pulled real cores **and** the haulers' run schedules to the very raiders who prey on those lanes — so the freighters certified full are boarded and opened by defenses that fold on the first pass. Pointedly **distinct** from the prior arcs it might be mistaken for: The Empty Proof **skips** a proof on un-tested goods (nothing touched) — here the outfitter **guts** installed systems in place and forges the fit-sheet; The Rigged Issue **substitutes** counterfeits — here the real systems are gutted in place, the shell left; The Cut Coil **damages** a sound speeder to bill a repair — here the shop is paid to install defenses and delivers hollow ones **and** fences the real parts + schedules to the raiders; The Rolled Log rolls back a **craft's** flight-hours — here a ship's **defensive fit** is hollowed; The Cold Charge fakes the **destruction** of ordnance — here the **fit** of live defenses is faked. Busted not by a slicer, an investigator, or a medic but by a **ship defensive-systems technician** who strips a "fitted" hull-cannon mount off the shop's re-fit bench in the cantina back cellar and reads it dead (`starship weapon repair`, DC11 — a mount that can't throw a bolt was never fitted) and runs a "full-rated" freighter's deflector fit up in Docking Bay 94 until it folds far below its sheet (`starship shields`, DC13 — a shield that folds on the first load was never made), stands off the shop's enforcer at the Spaceport Hotel (combat), and puts the hollow fit on the traders' berth registry that voids the fit-contract and pulls the bonding. **NPCs:** giver **Nyla Sabo** (Sullustan freighter defensive-systems tech / ex-fit-bench hand, non-hostile, "Chalmun's Cantina - Entrance"); foil **Draxo Venn** (Aqualish ship-outfitter enforcer, `chain_enemy_template hollow_fit_enforcer`, "Spaceport Hotel", `blaster_pistol` in the proven beatable band — pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). Narrated-only props: the proprietor Bevin Torr, the fit-sheet clerk, the fringe raiders/fences, the trader and supply pools, the gutted generators and cannon mounts, the haulers boarded on the lanes, and the berth registry. **Reward band** (unchanged, self-calibrating): 300 cr graduation + 150 cr step-4 = **450 cr**, **17** Independent rep (grad 5; steps 2/2/3/3/2), a **cp_reward-3** achievement (`hollow_fit_cleared`). ### 2026-07-01 — "The Cold Charge" — the thirty-first accessible questline (T3.24 generalized-quest expansion): a demilitarization-diversion / falsified-destruction fraud at a Mos Eisley ordnance-disposal yard, on a thirty-first distinct skill spread (the first heavy-ordnance / EOD build — grenade + missile weapons) — *drop generalized-questline-cold-charge*
T3.24 **generalized quest expansion**, the **thirty-first** accessible (non-t5, non-tutorial) `kind: questline` chain, after the prior thirty (… Cut Lane / Rolled Log / Paper Death). It reuses the **live questline engine** (`engine/tutorial_chains.py` + `chain_events.py`: the `kind: questline` type + `active_questline` slot, the existing event types, the four reward funnels) with **NO new engine code**, no new slot/event type, no schema migration, **no new reward faucet**, and **NO map-coordinate or exit-graph change** — the sanctioned right-of-launch widening per `quest_expansion_postlaunch_path_v1.md`: author MORE questlines on the proven pattern. **Firsts:** a **thirty-first distinct skill spread — `grenade` (Dexterity) + `missile weapons` (Dexterity)** — neither used by any prior arc — the **first accessible questline to reward the HEAVY-ORDNANCE / EXPLOSIVE-ORDNANCE (EOD) build** (safe a "destroyed" grenade to show it never burned, then run a "demilitarized" launcher-round to show it still fires), the **first to use `grenade`**, and the **first to use `missile weapons`**. *Honest framing:* **both spread skills are Dexterity, so this is NOT a cross-attribute spread — it is the THIRD all-Dexterity-weapon-pool spread**, after The Empty Proof's `firearms` + `thrown weapons` and The Paper Death's `running` + `melee combat`; and it is **NOT the first weapon arc** (The Empty Proof's marksman came first). What is genuinely first is the two skills and the **prove-the-round-is-live** pairing — one hand arms-and-safes a "destroyed" round on the bench, the other puts a "demilitarized" one downrange. Set on **Tatooine** in the **`tatooine_mos_eisley`** zone on a **fresh four-room cluster** (`mos_eisley_inn` / `lucky_despot_staircase` / `dimu_monastery_gate` / `lucky_despot_star_chamber`), every room fresh to the chain corpus; *honest discipline:* the Mos Eisley street face is **heavily worked** (The Crooked Wheel / The Sabotaged Run / The Long Haul / The Short Weight / The Cut Coil all set arcs in it), so this is the **SIXTH arc on the Mos Eisley face** (the **tenth Tatooine arc**), **NOT a fresh face** — what is fresh is the four-room **cluster**. Only the step-4 combat room must be combat-capable (`lucky_despot_star_chamber` defaults to contested); the giver/return and two skill rooms host no fight. **New story shape:** breaking a **DEMILITARIZATION-DIVERSION / FALSIFIED-DESTRUCTION FRAUD** — the **first accessible arc whose racket is faking the DESTRUCTION of dangerous goods**. A licensed disposal outfit (Sarlacc Reclamation & Disposal, run by the yard-master Vorm Dessek), paid by the fringe moisture-farm collectives, mining claims, and caravan pools to safely demilitarize and destroy their time-expired and surplus ordnance, instead logs the live lots **"cold"** (destroyed) on forged manifests, keeps the munitions, re-crates them in the grounded-starship Lucky Despot hotel's disused holds, and sells them to the fringe raiders and underworld — collecting the disposal fee **and** the resale while the same seismic charges and defense grenades the settlers paid to have destroyed come back at their caravans in raider hands. Pointedly **distinct** from the prior arcs it might be mistaken for: The Fouled Sump illegally **dumps** hazardous waste to hide a poison (nothing of value taken) — here the "destroyed" goods are live munitions **diverted and sold**; The Empty Proof **skips** a proof it was paid to run — here the outfit fakes the **destruction** of goods it was paid to destroy; The Rigged Issue **substitutes** counterfeits — here the diverted munitions are genuine, only their destruction a lie; The Rolled Log rolls back a **craft's** history — here it is the **destruction record** of live ordnance that is fabricated; The Cut Coil **damages** a sound speeder — here nothing is damaged, live goods logged destroyed are kept and sold. Busted not by a slicer, an investigator, or a medic but by a **heavy-ordnance hand / disposal tech** who pulls a "destroyed" grenade off the crated demil lot and **safes it live** (`grenade`, DC11 — a round that arms never burned) and range-fires a "demilitarized" seismic round into the empty wastes past the old monastery (`missile weapons`, DC13 — a destroyed round is ash, not flight), stands off the yard's enforcer at the hotel-cafe front (combat), and puts a live round the manifest swore was destroyed on the settlers' supply board that voids the disposal contract. **NPCs:** giver **Dessa Molvar** (human caravan-defense armorer / ex-disposal-bench ordnance-hand, non-hostile, "Mos Eisley Inn"); foil **Hurss Kadag** (Weequay disposal-yard enforcer, `chain_enemy_template cold_charge_enforcer`, "Lucky Despot - Star Chamber Cafe", `blaster_pistol` in the proven beatable band — pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). Narrated-only props: the yard-master Vorm Dessek, the manifest-clerk, the fringe raiders/fences, the settler collectives and caravan pools, the crated demil lots, the settlers hit at the frontier, and the supply board. **Reward band** (unchanged, self-calibrating): 300 cr graduation + 150 cr step-4 = **450 cr**, **17** Independent rep (grad 5; steps 2/2/3/3/2), a **cp_reward-3** achievement (`cold_charge_cleared`). Files: `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (+chain block), `data/worlds/clone_wars/npcs_drop_generalized_questline_cold_charge.yaml` (new, 2 NPCs), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 `content_refs.npcs` ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_cold_charge.py` (new, 34-test slice). **Gate:** slice + `test_t5_questline_content` (all-questlines-walkable + reward-tier) + reachability-invariant + smoke-foundation; foil-band held-out check confirms the identical 3 pre-existing `BAL.condemned_hull_melee_foil_band` (Bohrus Kang) failures, **zero new**. Era-clean, WEG D6, funnel-routed rewards, `force_sensitive` derived. Original characters, no canon figures.

### 2026-06-30 — "The Paper Death" — the thirtieth accessible questline (T3.24 generalized-quest expansion): a false-death bond fraud in the Coruscant lower city, on a thirtieth distinct skill spread (the first manhunter / skip-tracer build — running + melee combat) — *drop generalized-questline-paper-death*
T3.24 **generalized quest expansion**, the **thirtieth** accessible (non-t5, non-tutorial) `kind: questline` chain, after the prior twenty-nine (… Empty Proof / Cut Lane / Rolled Log). It reuses the **live questline engine** (`engine/tutorial_chains.py` + `chain_events.py`: the `kind: questline` type + `active_questline` slot, the existing event types, the four reward funnels) with **NO new engine code**, no new slot/event type, no schema migration, **no new reward faucet**, and **NO map-coordinate or exit-graph change** — the sanctioned right-of-launch widening per `quest_expansion_postlaunch_path_v1.md`: author MORE questlines on the proven pattern. **Firsts:** a **thirtieth distinct skill spread — `running` (Dexterity) + `melee combat` (Dexterity)** — neither used by any prior arc — the **first accessible questline to reward the MANHUNTER / SKIP-TRACER build** (run a fleeing mark down through a warren, then take her close and quiet with a blade), the **first to use `running`**, and the **first to use `melee combat`**. *Honest framing:* **both spread skills are Dexterity, so this is NOT a cross-attribute spread — it is the SECOND all-Dexterity-weapon-pool spread**, after The Empty Proof's `firearms` + `thrown weapons`; and it is **NOT the first weapon arc** (The Empty Proof's marksman came first). What is genuinely first is the two skills and the **run-it-down-then-take-it-close pursuit pairing**, pointedly distinct from The Empty Proof's two-**ranged**-weapon marksman — this is a **pursuit-and-close** build (foot-speed to catch, a blade to take), not a shooting build. Set on **Coruscant** in the **southern_underground (undercity)** zone on a **fresh four-room cluster** (`crystal_jewel_alley` / `crystal_jewel_back_rooms` / `ice_crypts` / `lower_medical`), every room fresh to the chain corpus; *honest discipline:* The Buried Block already worked the undercity, so this is the **SECOND arc on the Coruscant undercity face** (the **eighth Coruscant arc**), **NOT a fresh face** — what is fresh is the four-room **cluster**. Only the step-4 combat room must be combat-capable (`lower_medical` is contested); the giver/return and two skill rooms host no fight. **New story shape:** breaking a **FALSE-DEATH BOND FRAUD** — the **first accessible arc whose racket is the fabrication of a living person's DEATH for profit**. A lower-city bond-broker (Dral Vante) who holds recovery bonds and death-clause policies on lower-city debtors files a debtor **confirmed dead** when she is worth more dead than found: a bought clinic orderly forges a death certificate, a salted stand-in body is logged into the freezing Ice Crypts where the cold defeats a forensic read, and the broker collects the death-clause payout **and** seizes the estate while the living woman is written out of existence and, if she surfaces, hunted to make the paper death real. Pointedly **distinct** from the prior arcs it might be mistaken for: The Bonded Crew **bonds** people into labor (they live, worked) — here a living person is declared **dead** to collect a death payout and seize an estate; The Buried Block declares an inhabited **building** derelict — here a living **person** is declared dead; The Wasting Ward / The Dust-Sick fake an **ailment** on the healthy — here the person is provably alive and the **death** is the lie; The Rolled Log rolls back a **craft's** history — here a **person's** death record is fabricated whole. Busted not by a slicer, an investigator, or a medic but by a **manhunter** who **runs** the living "corpse" down before the crew silences her (`running`, DC11) and takes the body-handler **close and quiet with a blade** at the crypt to seize the salted stand-in and the crypt-log before the fake corpse is sealed (`melee combat`, DC13), stands off the enforcer at the clinic (combat), and stands the living woman and the salted stand-in side by side for the lower-city bond registry that voids the death. **NPCs:** giver **Nima Tarr** (Twi'lek lower-city skip-tracer, non-hostile, "Coruscant - Crystal Jewel Alley"); foil **Tovek Grahl** (Klatooinian bond-broker's enforcer, `chain_enemy_template paper_death_enforcer`, "Coruscant - Lower City Medical Post", `blaster_pistol` in the proven beatable band — pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). Narrated-only props: the broker Dral Vante, the bought orderly, the lenders/insurers, the salted stand-in, the crypt-curators, the living debtor Oly Fenn, the real doctor, and the bond registry (the room-flavor figures Doss Vek / Pell Merdo / Wesseri are nodded to, never instantiated). **Reward band** (unchanged, self-calibrating): 300 cr graduation + 150 cr step-4 = **450 cr**, **17** Independent rep (grad 5; steps 2/2/3/3/2), a **cp_reward-3** achievement (`paper_death_cleared`). *Co-location note:* the southern_underground mob-grind drop already seeds ambient hostiles in three of the four contested undercity rooms — appropriate for a dangerous lower-city cluster, orthogonal to the chain-corpus freshness guard, no bar to the giver-offer/talk flow. Files: `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (+chain block), `data/worlds/clone_wars/npcs_drop_generalized_questline_paper_death.yaml` (new, 2 NPCs), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 `content_refs.npcs` ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_paper_death.py` (new, 34-test slice). **Gate:** slice + `test_t5_questline_content` (all-questlines-walkable + reward-tier) + reachability-invariant + smoke-foundation = **59 green**; foil-band held-out check confirmed the identical 3 pre-existing `BAL.condemned_hull_melee_foil_band` (Bohrus Kang) failures, **zero new**. Era-clean, WEG D6, funnel-routed rewards, `force_sensitive` derived. Original characters, no canon figures.

### 2026-06-30 — "The Cut Lane" — the twenty-eighth accessible questline (T3.24 generalized-quest expansion): a staged-raid freight-loss / courier-skim insurance fraud on the Coruscant Monumental District skylanes, on a twenty-eighth distinct skill spread (the first saddle-gunner / swoop-outrider build, the first Mechanical+Dexterity cross) — *drop generalized-questline-cut-lane*
T3.24 **generalized quest expansion**, the **twenty-eighth** accessible (non-t5, non-tutorial) `kind: questline` chain, after the prior twenty-seven (… Fouled Sump / Fixed Bout / Empty Proof). It reuses the **live questline engine** (`engine/tutorial_chains.py` + `chain_events.py`: the `kind: questline` type + `active_questline` slot, the existing event types, the four reward funnels) with **NO new engine code**, no new slot/event type, no schema migration, **no new reward faucet**, and **NO map-coordinate or exit-graph change** — the sanctioned right-of-launch widening per `quest_expansion_postlaunch_path_v1.md`: author MORE questlines on the proven pattern. **Two firsts:** (1) a **twenty-eighth distinct skill spread — `swoop operation` (Mechanical) + `vehicle blasters` (Dexterity)** — neither used by any prior arc — the **first accessible questline to reward the SADDLE-GUNNER / SWOOP-OUTRIDER build** (ride a hired swoop down the lane to catch a skim, gun a staged raider down off the saddle with the swoop's own mounted gun), the **first to use `swoop operation`**, the **first to use `vehicle blasters`**, only the **second cross-attribute spread of the corpus** (after The Fixed Bout's `brawling` + `willpower`, a Strength + Knowledge pair) and the **first to cross Mechanical + Dexterity**. *Honest note:* **not** the first vehicle arc (The Long Haul / The Sabotaged Run / The Fouled Sump rewarded ground/repulsorlift/walker/hover OPERATION first) and **not** the first weapon arc (The Empty Proof's marksman came first) — what is genuinely first is the swoop and vehicle-blasters skills, the **ride-and-shoot-from-a-mount pairing**, and the Mechanical+Dexterity cross. A wholly distinct build: not a tradesman, operator, investigator, clerk, medic, prizefighter, or the marksman who stood on a backstop on foot, but an **outrider who rides a mount down a skylane and fires a mounted gun off the saddle**; (2) the **seventh arc set on CORUSCANT**, on the Monumental District's freight skylanes (`column_commons` / `western_sea` / `skydome_gardens` / `kallarak_amphitheater`, the `monumental_district` zone), **every room fresh to the chain corpus** — reusing NONE of the prior Coruscant arcs' rooms (The Lost Courier, The False Provenance, The Sealed Ledger, The Twisted Word, The Buried Block, The Fouled Sump). **Honest discipline note:** The False Provenance already worked the Monumental District, so this is honestly the **second arc on the Monumental District face, not a fresh face** — what is fresh is the **four-room cluster**. Only the **step-4 combat room must be combat-capable** (`kallarak_amphitheater` is **contested** — no explicit security level, the default; the engine's all-questlines-walkable test proves the combat step walks there); the giver/return room and the two skill rooms host no fight — the swoop-riding shadow over the Western Sea and the saddle-gun shot over the gardens are riding and gunnery feats in open airspace. The Senate / Jedi / Republic war overhead never reach down to a freight-lane skim over the Monumental District; the district's foot traffic is ambient, and the lane is run afoul of **by the bled shippers themselves and the town's own skylane freight registry** (narrated) — the way every prior accessible arc keeps the larger powers offstage. **New story shape:** breaking a **staged-raid freight-loss / courier-skim insurance fraud**. **Pointedly distinct from the two prior staged-loss / salvage arcs:** The Wreckers' Light lures honest ships to *destruction* on a false beacon to salvage the wrecks, and The Salted Lane *plants contraband* to seize a clean hauler's run — here nothing is wrecked and nothing is planted; a licensed skycourier outfit (narrated: *Helevin Skycourier*, run by *Pell Helevin*) flies its **own** outriders, masked and unmarked, as the "skylane raiders" that supposedly plague its run, cuts marked high-value parcels off its own courier swoops in the quiet transit gaps, files them raider-lost to **pocket the shippers' declared-value indemnity AND keep the goods**, then sells the same bled shippers a costlier "armed escort" against a raider crew that is the company itself — busted not by a slicer, an investigator, or a relief pilot but by a neutral **outrider who rides the lane to catch the skim and guns the staged raider down intact off the saddle**. A Duros freight-broker and ex-lane-outrider, bled to ruin by losses month after month, can read a company outrider's flight from a real raider's in the company's own gun-camera but a broker's loss claim against the loss is the last word a registry takes, so she hires a neutral hand: take a hired swoop onto the open gap over the Western Sea and shadow a marked run at lane-speed to catch the skim coming off the company's own lane-house heading (`swoop operation`, DC 11), gun the staged raider down intact off the saddle over the Skydome Gardens — crippling its repulsor, not the rider — so its unmarked company swoop, flight-log, and the cut parcel still aboard come down where they can be read (`vehicle blasters`, DC 13), stand off the lane-boss who comes to the impound dock behind the amphitheater to recover the downed swoop before it is read (`combat_won`), and get the swoop, the flight-log routing the raid off the lane-house, and the cut parcel to the skylane freight registry. **Two placed NPCs** (giver + single foil; everything else — the lane-master *Pell Helevin*, the off-world fence, the masked company outriders, the cut parcel and the company swoop's flight-log/transponder, the bled shippers, the genteel district foot traffic, the skylane freight registry — narrated): **Resa Halix** (**Duros** freight-broker and ex-lane-outrider, GIVER + closer, `column_commons` / "Coruscant - Column Commons District", non-hostile, swoop-and-saddle-gun-skewed `swoop operation 4D+1` / `vehicle blasters 4D` to embody the two quest skills) and **Drovan Pell** (**Devaronian** courier-company lane-boss — the company's muscle, not a rider or a duelist, who settles things with a blaster off a service dock, `chain_enemy_template: cut_lane_enforcer`, `kallarak_amphitheater` / "Coruscant - Kallarak Amphitheater", hostile, the proven `blaster_pistol` of the ranged foils — squarely in the proven beatable band, no balance flag, the same in-band guarded stat line as The Salted Lane's … The Empty Proof's foils, and pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). Graduation pays the standard freelance band (300 credits + `independent` rep + the `cut_lane_cleared` achievement, `cp_reward 3`); total `independent` rep 17 + a step-4 150 cr = **450 cr / 17 rep**, identical to all prior accessible arcs, under the tuned ceiling. **Files:** the `coruscant_cut_lane` chain in `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (5 steps: talk → swoop operation → vehicle blasters → combat → talk; all four step rooms real loaded `monumental_district` slugs, combat room combat-capable), `npcs_drop_generalized_questline_cut_lane.yaml` (new, +2 NPCs, WEG-D6 stats matched to the shipped accessible-questline bands), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 npc ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_cut_lane.py` (new, 31).
