Only the **step-4 combat room must be combat-capable** (`jundland_tusken_overlook` is **lawless**); the giver/return room and the two proof rooms host no fight — the firearms proof is a backstop range, the thrown proof a witnessed test on the checkpoint's cleared ground. Hutt-fringe Tatooine has no Republic civic authority to keep offstage; the **clone troopers at the checkpoint are ambient**, here for the war and standing neutral witness to a sanctioned proof, not the racket's undoing, and the proof-house is run afoul of **by the town's own Mos Eisley arms-licensing prefect** (narrated) — the way every prior accessible arc keeps the larger powers offstage. **New story shape:** breaking an **independent weapons-proof-house certification fraud** — the **first accessible arc whose racket is a corrupt third-party CERTIFIER** rather than a crooked buyer, seller, or service. **Pointedly distinct from The Rigged Issue,** the only prior arms-fraud arc: there a crooked *seller* on a militia arms-supply contract *substitutes* the genuine issue with counterfeits, busted by an *armorer* who *strips the goods on the bench* (`blaster repair` + `armor repair`); here the corrupt actor is the **independent proof-house — the third-party certifier, not the seller** — selling a *certification* never earned on **real but un-proofed goods**, busted by a **marksman who re-runs the proof the bench skipped** (`firearms` + `thrown weapons`). Different actor, different fraud (false certification, not goods-substitution), different skills, different victims. A licensed Mos Eisley arms proof-house (narrated: *Tannor Proof & Assay*, run by *Veth Tannor*) — the independent bench paid to test-fire and range-check every defense lot before sale — takes a cut to skip the proof, stamping cheap frontier defense lots (slugthrower carbines + balanced throwing irons) "range-proofed to standard" **without ever firing or throwing a piece of them**, so un-tested out-of-spec arms reach the moisture-farm collectives and caravan outfits of the wastes road behind a clean independent mark; when a lot fails in the field and guards die, the bench calls it user abuse and bills the survivors. A caravan-guard captain who buried two guards behind a stamped lot that jammed in the heat offers her fee to a neutral hand who can shoot and throw: take a carbine from the failed lot to the canyon backstop and prove it cannot hold the group its stamp certifies (`firearms`, DC 11), run the throwing irons' proof on the checkpoint's cleared ground in plain view of the clone troopers as neutral witnesses so a "range-checked" stamp on a piece that flies untrue cannot be waved off (`thrown weapons`, DC 13), stand off the proof-house's enforcer who tracks the failed lot onto the lawless overlook ridge (`combat_won`), and get the matched failed lot and the bench's own log of lots stamped unfired to the Mos Eisley arms-licensing prefect. **Two placed NPCs** (giver + single foil; everything else — the proof-master *Veth Tannor*, his off-world broker, the failed lot and the skipped-proof log, the collectives and caravan outfits, the two dead guards, the ambient clone troopers, the rival proof-house, the arms-licensing prefect — narrated): **Sela Marrenn** (**Zabrak** wastes-road caravan-guard captain, GIVER + closer, `outskirts_trail_junction` / "City Outskirts - Desert Trail Junction", non-hostile, firearms-and-thrown-skewed `firearms 4D+1` / `thrown weapons 4D` to embody the two quest skills) and **Sesko Vahr** (**Trandoshan** proof-house enforcer — the bench's muscle, not a marksman, who settles things with a blaster off the road, `chain_enemy_template: empty_proof_enforcer`, `jundland_tusken_overlook` / "Jundland Wastes - Tusken Camp Overlook", hostile, the proven `blaster_pistol` of the ranged foils — squarely in the proven beatable band, no balance flag, the same in-band guarded stat line as The Salted Lane's … The Fixed Bout's foils, and pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). Graduation pays the standard freelance band (300 credits + `independent` rep + the `empty_proof_cleared` achievement, `cp_reward 3`); total `independent` rep 17 + a step-4 150 cr = **450 cr / 17 rep**, identical to all prior accessible arcs, under the tuned ceiling. **Files:** the `tatooine_empty_proof` chain in `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (5 steps: talk → firearms → thrown weapons → combat → talk; all four step rooms real loaded `tatooine_outskirts`/`tatooine_jundland` slugs, combat room lawless), `npcs_drop_generalized_questline_empty_proof.yaml` (new, +2 NPCs, WEG-D6 stats matched to the shipped accessible-questline bands), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 npc ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_empty_proof.py` (new, 31). ### 2026-06-30 — "The Fixed Bout" — the twenty-sixth accessible questline (T3.24 generalized-quest expansion): a fixed-prizefight / coerced-throw betting racket on the Nar Shaddaa promenade, on a twenty-sixth distinct skill spread (the first prizefighter / nerve-and-fists build) that closes out the Strength and Knowledge skill pools — *drop generalized-questline-fixed-bout*
T3.24 **generalized quest expansion**, the **twenty-sixth** accessible (non-t5, non-tutorial) `kind: questline` chain, after The Ghost Shipment / Crooked Wheel / Lost Courier / Skimmed Line / Dust-Sick / False Provenance / Forged Notice / Warrens Toll / Sealed Ledger / Sabotaged Run / Hollow Crew / Driven Herd / Condemned Hull / Long Haul / Twisted Word / Wasting Ward / Rigged Issue / Salted Lane / Short Weight / Wreckers' Light / Bonded Crew / Kept Watch / Buried Block / Cut Coil / Fouled Sump. It reuses the **live questline engine** (`engine/tutorial_chains.py` + `chain_events.py`: the `kind: questline` type + `active_questline` slot, the existing event types, the four reward funnels) with **NO new engine code**, no new slot/event type, no schema migration, **no new reward faucet**, and **NO map-coordinate or exit-graph change** — the sanctioned right-of-launch widening per `quest_expansion_postlaunch_path_v1.md`: author MORE questlines on the proven pattern. **Two firsts:** (1) a **twenty-sixth distinct skill spread — `brawling` (Strength) + `willpower` (Knowledge)** — neither used by any prior arc — the **first accessible questline to reward the PRIZEFIGHTER / NERVE-AND-FISTS build** (go one clean round in a fixed pit no favorite survives honest, then hold a nerve that will not be bought, dosed, or threatened into throwing the next bout); the **first to use `brawling`**, the **first to use `willpower`**, and — because `brawling` is the **last unused Strength skill** (climbing/jumping, stamina, lifting, swimming all spent) and `willpower` the **last unused Knowledge skill** (all thirteen others spent) — the **first arc to close out TWO whole attribute skill pools at once**: after this slice **Strength and Knowledge join Perception as exhausted attributes**, no unused skill remaining under any of the three. It is the **first cross-attribute Strength + Knowledge spread** (prior Strength arcs paired Strength+Strength — climbing/jumping + stamina — or Strength+Mechanical — powersuit operation + lifting; prior Knowledge arcs paired Knowledge or Mechanical), a wholly distinct build: not a tradesman, operator, investigator, clerk, or medic, but a fighter who wins clean and a will that cannot be turned; (2) the **fifth arc set on the Corellian Sector Promenade of NAR SHADDAA** (`bounty_hunters_quarter` / `burning_deck_back_room` / `hutt_emissary_tower_audience` / `weapons_cache`, all `nar_shaddaa_promenade` zone), **every room fresh to the chain corpus** — reusing NONE of the prior promenade arcs' rooms (The Ghost Shipment's `burning_deck_cantina` / `floating_market`, The Forged Notice's `enforcer_alley` / `smugglers_guild_hall` / `upper_dock_observation`, The Wasting Ward's `refugee_sector`, or The Wreckers' Light's `old_corellian_quarter` / `promenade_corporate_tower` / `the_grid` / `vertical_bazaar`). **Honest discipline note:** four prior arcs already set work on the Corellian Sector Promenade, so this is honestly the **fifth arc on that face, not a fresh face** — what is fresh is the **four-room cluster** (the giver room `burning_deck_back_room` is a *distinct room slug* from The Ghost Shipment's `burning_deck_cantina` — the same dockside venue's back room versus its cantina floor, two different loaded rooms; at 26 arcs and a finite set of combat-capable non-SECURED rooms, no untouched zone with four contested rooms remains). The lawless Smugglers' Moon has no Senate / Jedi / Republic war reach, and the circuit is run afoul of **not by any law (there is none here) but by the marks themselves and the Hutt backer protecting a book no one will bet** — the way every prior accessible arc keeps the larger powers offstage. **New story shape:** breaking a **fixed-prizefight / coerced-throw betting racket** — the **first accessible arc whose racket is a rigged BLOOD SPORT**, the harm carried in crippled fighters and a fleeced betting crowd rather than in a single mark's purse or a poisoned draw. **Pointedly distinct from the two prior rigged-contest arcs:** The Crooked Wheel rigs a *game of chance* the house controls (busted by reading the rigged game — `investigation` + `gambling`); The Sabotaged Run rigs a *race* by sabotaging a machine (busted by reading the tampered speeder — `sensors` + `repulsorlift operation`); this rigs a *contest of skill* by coercing the contestants and is broken by **fighting one honest round and holding a nerve the racket cannot buy**. An unsanctioned promenade prizefight circuit — feeder bouts in the Burning Deck's back-room pit, high-stakes title bouts in the Hutt emissary's tower, backed by a Hutt kajidic taking its cut of the betting book — has its matchmaker (narrated: *Tovan Dree*) fix the outcomes: favored fighters told to throw, the ones who refuse slow-dosed before the late rounds or leaned on through whatever they love, so the house book always lands where the kajidic wants and the crowd and the fighters both pay. A forty-year Nikto cornerman whose fighter was crippled (dosed, then thrown) for refusing to take a fall has a corner hand's word against a Hutt-backed book on a moon with no law. Step into the Burning Deck's pit and beat the house's untouchable favorite clean, the one win the matchmaker cannot fix (`brawling`, DC 11), take the audience the win earns up in the Hutt emissary's tower and refuse the purse, the "steadying" cup (the dose), and the flat threat the majordomo makes every fighter take to throw the next bout — holding nerve enough to walk out with the kajidic's own marker of which bouts were bought (`willpower`, DC 13), stand off the circuit's enforcer who comes for the marker at the weapons cache (`combat_won`), and get the marker of the fixed book back where the bettors and fighters and a rival promoter will read it. **Two placed NPCs** (giver + single foil; everything else — the matchmaker *Tovan Dree*, the Hutt emissary kajidic and its majordomo *Pazda the Hutt* (an original, narrated-only figure, never a canon Hutt), the crippled and dosed and coerced fighters, the betting crowd, the steadying cup and bought purses and the kajidic's marker of the fixed book, the rival promoter — narrated): **Garn Holvo** (**Nikto** forty-year fight-circuit cornerman, GIVER + closer, `bounty_hunters_quarter`, non-hostile, brawl-and-nerve-skewed `brawling 4D+1` / `willpower 4D` to embody the two quest skills) and **Neevo Karr** (**Rodian** circuit enforcer — the book's muscle, not a fighter, who settles things with a blaster off the canvas, `chain_enemy_template: fixed_bout_enforcer`, `weapons_cache`, hostile, the proven `blaster_pistol` of the ranged foils — squarely in the proven beatable band, no balance flag, the same in-band guarded stat line as The Salted Lane's, The Short Weight's, The Wreckers' Light's, The Bonded Crew's, The Kept Watch's, The Buried Block's, The Cut Coil's, and The Fouled Sump's foils, and pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). Graduation pays the standard freelance band (300 credits + `independent` rep + the `fixed_bout_cleared` achievement, `cp_reward 3`); total `independent` rep 17 + a step-4 150 cr = **450 cr / 17 rep**, identical to all prior accessible arcs, under the tuned ceiling. **Files:** the `nar_shaddaa_fixed_bout` chain in `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (5 steps: talk → brawling → willpower → combat → talk; all four step rooms real loaded `nar_shaddaa_promenade` slugs), `npcs_drop_generalized_questline_fixed_bout.yaml` (new, +2 NPCs, WEG-D6 stats matched to the shipped accessible-questline bands), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 npc ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_fixed_bout.py` (new, 29). (2) the **sixth arc set on CORUSCANT**, on the Commercial District / **"Coruscant Works" face** (`commercial_district_hab` / `commercial_district_power_grid` / `gartro_rookery` / `mid_market_street`, all `commercial_district` zone), **every room fresh to the chain corpus** — reusing NONE of the prior Coruscant arcs' rooms (The Lost Courier's southern-underground rooms, The False Provenance's Monumental-District rooms, The Sealed Ledger's *other* Commercial-District rooms, The Twisted Word's Entertainment-District rooms, or The Buried Block's undercity rooms). **Honest discipline note:** The Sealed Ledger already set an arc in the `commercial_district` zone, so this is honestly the **second arc on that face, not a fresh face** — what is fresh is the **four-room cluster** (at 25 arcs and a finite set of combat-capable non-SECURED rooms, no untouched zone with four contested rooms remains). The Senate, the Jedi, and the Republic war overhead have no reach down in a mid-level works district and never appear, and the outfit is run afoul of in the end by the district's own Coruscant Works environmental inspectorate, which will not have a licensed disposal contractor dump the hazardous waste it was paid to haul to reclamation into the level the hab block draws from — the way every prior accessible arc keeps the larger powers offstage. **New story shape:** breaking an **illegal-dumping / disposal-fraud cover-up** — the **first accessible arc in which the racket is an environmental / contamination cover-up**, the harm carried in the air and water of a hab block rather than in a single mark's purse. A Coruscant Works hazardous-waste disposal contractor (narrated: *Drell Reclamation*) holds the commercial district's licensed disposal contract — paid to haul the works' spent coolant and heavy-metal sludge and reactor-cell tailings out to the proper offworld reclamation yards — and instead walks its rigs down into a dead service void under the district (the abandoned coolant sump beneath the Central Power Grid Station and the gartro-choked shaft below it) and dumps the drums there for free, pocketing the haul-and-process fees and filing clean reclamation manifests for runs that never left the level; the void it dumps into is exactly the level the Engineering Corps hab block's air-and-water recyclers draw from, so a season of dumped sludge has surfaced in the block (the water gone metallic, the children sickening, the plumbing corroding), all written off as old infrastructure. **Distinct from The Wasting Ward's fake-plague extortion** (which fakes a plague that is *not* there to extort the well; this hides a poison that *is* there to protect a fee). Take the works' own cargo-walker down the flooded sump floor and bring up a dumped drum where no wheel or lift can go (`walker operation`, DC 11), fly a hover cargo-rig down into the gartro void to recover the outfit's abandoned hover-sled and its routing log — the record that ends every "reclamation" run in the void instead of at a yard (`hover vehicle operation`, DC 13), stand off the disposal crew's enforcer who comes for the drum and the log on the Mid-Level Market Street (`combat_won`), and get the contaminated drum and the routing log to the Coruscant Works environmental inspectorate. **Two placed NPCs** (giver + single foil; everything else — the disposal boss *Vass Drell*, the offworld broker, the dumped drums and abandoned hover-sled and falsified manifests, the rig crew, the sickening works families, the gartro flocks and void scavengers, the market-street vendors, the environmental inspectorate — narrated): **Burlo Vethic** (**Besalisk** thirty-year works heavy-rig operator turned hab-block maintenance steward, GIVER + closer, `commercial_district_hab`, non-hostile, Mechanical-skewed `walker operation 4D+1` / `hover vehicle operation 4D`, who traced the metallic draw down his rig routes to the sump but is a lone works hand whose word means nothing against a licensed contractor's clean manifests and whom the disposal crew would run off the grid station) and **Draz Kullo** (**Klatooinian** disposal-crew enforcer, `chain_enemy_template: fouled_sump_enforcer`, `mid_market_street`, hostile, the proven `blaster_pistol` of the ranged foils — squarely in the proven beatable band, no balance flag, the same in-band guarded stat line as The Salted Lane's, The Short Weight's, The Wreckers' Light's, The Bonded Crew's, The Kept Watch's, The Buried Block's, and The Cut Coil's foils, and pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). and where the only prior droid work re-*flashes* a droid's behavior (droid programming, software), this *repairs* a droid the racket physically *gutted* (hardware) to restore a wiped witness; (2) the **seventh arc set on TATOOINE**, on the Mos Eisley **street face** (`mos_eisley_south_end` / `dowager_queen_corner` / `mos_eisley_north_end` / `mos_eisley_spaceport_row`, all `tatooine_mos_eisley` zone), **every room fresh to the chain corpus** — reusing NONE of the prior Tatooine arcs' rooms (The Crooked Wheel's market district, The Dust-Sick's Jundland, The Sabotaged Run's speeder circuit, The Long Haul's haul-road and power station, The Rigged Issue's civic compound, The Short Weight's spaceport docking bays, or The Kept Watch's market shop row). **Honest discipline note:** at 24 arcs across 6 planets and a finite set of combat-capable (non-SECURED) rooms, no untouched zone with four contested rooms remains, so this slice keeps the discipline still available — a **fresh room cluster, not a fresh face** (the Mos Eisley street zone already carries three prior arcs' rooms on *different* rooms). Tatooine is Hutt-fringe with no Republic presence to keep offstage at all, and the garage is run afoul of in the end by the town's own Mos Eisley vehicle-licensing prefect, who will not have a licensed repair shop condemn the speeders it was paid to mend and impound them on a manufactured fault — the way every prior accessible arc keeps the larger powers offstage. **New story shape:** breaking a **sabotage-for-impound / product-tampering consumer-fraud racket** — the **first accessible arc in which the racket DAMAGES the very thing it is paid to fix**. A Mos Eisley repulsorlift repair garage (narrated: *Hax Vortassa's lift & tune*) underprices the honest benches, then on the owners it judges a tow and a fee away from going under it nicks a repulsor coil on the bench — a file-line cut into the winding too fine to see, set to hold a week and then drop the lift drive dead in the lanes — tows the stranded speeder in with its own recovery droid, books a ruinous repair against the fault it built, and strips the speeders the owners must sign over, gutting the recovery droid's tow log after every rigged job so nothing ties the breakdowns to the bench. **Distinct from The Rigged Issue's arms-substitution** (which swaps counterfeit arms for the genuine issue and fences the real ones; this leaves the customer's *own* speeder in the customer's hands and quietly rigs it to fail, so the breakdown looks like bad luck and the recovery tow looks like a mercy). Get into the wreckage-corner scrap lot and read a towed-in speeder's lift drive for the deliberate cut (`repulsorlift repair`, DC 11), rebuild the garage's gutted recovery droid in its North End yard until it gives up the tow-and-job log the racket erased (`droid repair`, DC 13), stand off the yard's enforcer who comes for the droid and the proof on Spaceport Row (`combat_won`), and get the cut coil and the droid's log to the Mos Eisley vehicle-licensing prefect. **Two placed NPCs** (giver + single foil; everything else — the garage boss *Hax Vortassa*, the offworld fence, the cut coils and gutted recovery droids and rigged invoices, the recovery crew, the working-folk owners signed out of their speeders, the Dowager Queen corner's Jawas and con men, the South End neighbors, the vehicle-licensing prefect — narrated): **Nilo Sabb** (**Sullustan** fifty-year speeder-and-droid mechanic, GIVER + closer, `mos_eisley_south_end`, non-hostile, Technical-skewed `repulsorlift repair 4D+1` / `droid repair 4D`, underpriced out of his custom and the one being on the row who read the cut coil for what it is, but a beaten rival whose word means nothing against a licensed garage's clean records and who the yard would walk off the lot) and **Vagh Sarko** (**Weequay** garage recovery-crew enforcer, `chain_enemy_template: cut_coil_enforcer`, `mos_eisley_spaceport_row`, hostile, the proven `blaster_pistol` of the ranged foils — squarely in the proven beatable band, no balance flag, the same in-band guarded stat line as The Salted Lane's, The Short Weight's, The Wreckers' Light's, The Bonded Crew's, The Kept Watch's, and The Buried Block's foils, and pointedly NOT the melee line of The Condemned Hull's out-of-band Houk foil). Graduation pays the standard freelance band (300 credits + `independent` rep + the `cut_coil_cleared` achievement, `cp_reward 3`); total `independent` rep 17 + a step-4 150 cr = **450 cr / 17 rep**, identical to all prior accessible arcs, under the tuned ceiling. **Files:** the `mos_eisley_cut_coil` chain in `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (5 steps: talk → repulsorlift repair → droid repair → combat → talk; all four step rooms real loaded Tatooine slugs), `npcs_drop_generalized_questline_cut_coil.yaml` (new, +2 NPCs, WEG-D6 stats matched to the shipped accessible-questline bands), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 npc ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_cut_coil.py` (new, 29). Go down the sealed access by the broken conduit shafts the crew thinks impassable (`climbing/jumping`, DC 11), endure the deep level's foul air and recycler heat to reach the Warren-Nine pocket and clear and hold a route to bring the trapped residents up (`stamina`, DC 13), stand off the outfit's enforcer posted at the lower-city black market who comes for the freed and the proof (`combat_won`), and get the freed residents and the proof of habitation to the Lower City Reclamation Board. **Two placed NPCs** (giver + single foil; everything else — the outfit boss *Vergo Sennat*, the offworld redeveloper, the falsified dereliction survey and the welded shaft seal, the strip crew, the forty sealed residents, the tenement-stack dwellers and the black-market traders, the Lower City Reclamation Board — narrated): **Tomar Hess** (**Human** forty-year lower-city conduit climber and squatter, GIVER + closer, `underground_tenement_block`, non-hostile, Strength-skewed `climbing/jumping 4D+1` / `stamina 3D+2`, an old climber who knows the dark shaft network the crew never mapped but gone too grey in the knees to make the descent, the rescue, and the fight all at once, and unheard against a filed survey) and **Brunt Kosh** (**Aqualish** salvage-outfit posted enforcer, `chain_enemy_template: buried_block_enforcer`, `lower_market`, hostile, the proven `blaster_pistol` of the ranged foils — squarely in the proven beatable band, no balance flag, the same in-band guarded stat line as The Salted Lane's, The Short Weight's, The Wreckers' Light's, The Bonded Crew's, and The Kept Watch's foils). The Hutt-front dock authority that watches the approach for Republic interdiction (named in the upper-dock and Grid room text) stays distant background — and is, in the end, the pragmatic local power a freelance wreck-luring outfit poaching the moon's own approach lanes runs afoul of — the way every prior accessible arc keeps the Republic/CIS conflict offstage. **New story shape:** breaking a **false-beacon / wreck-luring (salvage) racket** — a freelance salvage outfit (narrated: *Coreward Salvage & Reclamation*, leased behind discreet signage in the Promenade trade tower) plants *spoofed distress and navigation beacons* on the outer Corellian-sector approach — foundering-ship calls no decent spacer flies past, set on a bearing that drags whoever answers them off the charted corridor into the **Saljenn Drift**, a derelict field and gravity-shadow that kills a ship that strays in — then logs the dead ship as a fresh "derelict," files a salvage claim, and strips and fences the hull, while the dock authority writes each loss up as bad piloting and the approach earns a name as a killing lane that herds honest traffic onto the outfit's own escort service. **Distinct from The Condemned Hull's racket** (which *condemns a sound ship* via certification fraud to seize it cheap; this *destroys* ships outright with a faked beacon and harvests the wrecks), **from The Long Haul's** (which *strands* rigs on the road to tow them off under a salvage lien, crews and cargo intact; this lures ships to their *deaths* and strips the wrecks), **and from The Salted Lane's** (which bends a freighter out to a partnered hijack crew that *strips* the ship, crew and hull walking away; this leaves *no ship and no crew* to walk away). Work the information-broker's comm-feed grid to capture the spoofed distress beacon and triangulate the transmitter that fakes it, proving the call a plant and not a foundering ship (`communications`, DC 11), lay the outfit's filed claim coordinates over the system's true approach lanes to prove every claimed wreck sits off the corridor on the beacon's drag-bearing inside the Drift, engineered and not accident (`planetary systems`, DC 13), stand off the outfit's claims-agent who comes for the proof in the vertical bazaar (`combat_won`), and get the beacon trace and the claim overlay to the dock authority's claims board. **Two placed NPCs** (giver + single foil; everything else — the salvage outfit's boss, his offworld fence, the spoofed beacons and the transmitter that fakes them, the lost ships and dead crews, the Saljenn Drift, the information-broker and his Gamorrean sentinels, the Hutt-front dock authority and its claims board, the scared independent spacers — narrated): **Corwin Hale** (**Corellian Human** independent freighter captain, GIVER + closer, `old_corellian_quarter`, non-hostile, Mechanical/Knowledge-skewed `communications 4D` / `planetary systems 4D+1`, a navigator who answered one of those beacons and came out of the Drift with a wrecked ship and two dead crew, who can feel a beacon sit wrong on a lane but has no licensed standing against a stamped "derelict" claim) and **Hask Vorran** (**Weequay** salvage outfit's claims-agent, `chain_enemy_template: wreckers_light_enforcer`, `vertical_bazaar`, hostile, the proven `blaster_pistol` of the ranged foils — squarely in the proven beatable band, no balance flag, the same in-band guarded stat line as The Salted Lane's and The Short Weight's foils). Graduation pays the standard freelance band (300 credits + `independent` rep + the `wreckers_light_cleared` achievement, `cp_reward 3`); total `independent` rep 17 + a step-4 150 cr = **450 cr / 17 rep**, identical to all prior accessible arcs, under the tuned ceiling. **Files:** the `nar_shaddaa_wreckers_light` chain in `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (5 steps: talk → communications → planetary systems → combat → talk; all four step rooms real loaded Nar Shaddaa slugs), `npcs_drop_generalized_questline_wreckers_light.yaml` (new, +2 NPCs, WEG-D6 stats matched to the shipped accessible-questline bands), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 npc ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_wreckers_light.py` (new, 28). Targeted (28) + reachability-invariant + reward-tier-consistency + skill-difficulty-winnability + all-questlines-walkable + smoke-foundation green. Clear of all avoid-lanes (no engine/parser/director/combat/harvest edits — pure additive content via existing consumers). ⚠ **Pre-existing baseline red (NOT this drop):** `test_questline_foil_winnability_band` fails on origin/main (3 cases) due to The Condemned Hull's `Bohrus Kang` melee foil (`vibroaxe`, blaster `2D+2` — below the to-hit floor + outside the sidearm set); this drop adds zero new failures (verified by re-running the band test with this drop's NPC file held out — the identical 3 fail), and its own foil (blaster `4D` / `blaster_pistol`) is squarely in band. Tracked as `BAL.condemned_hull_melee_foil_band` for Brian. — *drop generalized-questline-wreckers-light*

and distinct from the only prior **Strength-skill** arc, The Driven Herd (`beast riding` + `swimming`, an open-water wrangler) — this is raw industrial *lifting*, a stevedore's back; (2) the **sixth arc set on TATOOINE** and the **first ever set on the iconic Mos Eisley docking bays** — the spaceport freight-terminal face (`notsub_shipping_lobby` / `docking_bay_87` / `docking_bay_86` / `dockside_cafe`, spanning the `tatooine_mos_eisley` + `tatooine_spaceport` zones), **every room fresh to the chain corpus** — reusing NONE of the five prior Tatooine arcs' rooms (The Crooked Wheel's market district, The Dust-Sick's Jundland, The Sabotaged Run's speeder circuit, The Long Haul's freight-haul margins, The Rigged Issue's civic compound). Run a powered loader through the contractor's calibration cycle and hold a true weight against the rigged sensor to prove the rig logs short on purpose (`powersuit operation`, DC 11), break a salted ballast pod by hand and restack the dead weight that hides the skim to prove the shortfall theft and not shrinkage (`lifting`, DC 13), stand off the contractor's collector who comes for the proof in the dockside cafe (`combat_won`), and get the loader trace and the salted pod's manifest to the spaceport port-authority desk. **Two placed NPCs** (giver + single foil; everything else — the loading contractor, his offworld fence, the honest co-op crew blamed for shrinkage, the rigged loaders and doctored sensors, the salted ballast pods and hidden skim compartments, the independent traders, the clone garrison/Republic liaison, the port-authority desk — narrated): **Grett Bashan** (**Aqualish** veteran loader, GIVER + closer, `notsub_shipping_lobby`, non-hostile, Strength/Mechanical-skewed `lifting 4D+1` / `powersuit operation 4D`, a being who can feel a rigged calibration in a loader's drift but has no licensed standing against a certified scale — the **first Aqualish questline giver**) and **Drozz Kael** (**Klatooinian** contractor's collector, `chain_enemy_template: short_weight_enforcer`, `dockside_cafe`, hostile, the proven `blaster_pistol` of the ranged foils — squarely in the proven beatable band, no balance flag, the same in-band guarded stat line as The Salted Lane's and The Rigged Issue's foils — the **first Klatooinian combat foil**). The Clone War the `refugee_sector` room description already names (the newest arrivals "from CIS-occupied worlds") stays distant background — the displaced are the racket's **prey**, not its cause — the way every prior accessible arc keeps the Republic/CIS conflict offstage. **New story shape:** breaking a **fake-plague / quarantine-extortion (medical-fraud) racket** — a Hutt-lieutenant's self-styled "sanitation board" (narrated) declares a contagious "wasting fever" loose in the refugee sector, seals the level under quarantine, sets a clearance toll on anyone who wants out to work, and sells the only "cure," while its backers stand ready to buy the emptied, condemned habs cheap as the families who can't pay sicken or flee. The "fever" is no contagion — it is a slow cytotoxic by-product dosed into the board's own tonic and clearance-ration water, so it tracks the **dose**, not the **contact**. Read a board-"treated" patient at the back-alley medcenter and prove the wasting a poisoning by how it travels (`medicine`, DC 11), trace the cut to the toxic industrial sludge scavenged out of the droid junkyard and name the agent in the board's own cure (`scholar`, DC 13), stand off the board's collector who comes for the proof at the undercity market (`combat_won`), and get the truth to the Moon's port-medical registry. **Two placed NPCs** (giver + single foil; everything else — the Hutt lieutenant, the board, its inspectors, the "cure" and clearance toll, the off-books buyers, the scrap-buyer, the treated patient, the dying refugees, and the medcenter's ambient back-alley doctor — narrated): **Ulil Maan** (**Ithorian** volunteer medic, GIVER + closer, `refugee_sector`, non-hostile, Technical/Knowledge-skewed `medicine 4D+1` / `scholar 4D` — the **first Ithorian questline giver**, a clinical healer with no standing against a Hutt-backed board) and **Greel Vodo** (**Rodian** board collector, `chain_enemy_template: wasting_ward_enforcer`, `undercity_market`, hostile, the proven `blaster_pistol` of the ranged foils — the **first Rodian combat foil**, squarely in the proven beatable band, no balance flag). Graduation pays the standard freelance band (300 credits + `independent` rep + the `wasting_ward_cleared` achievement, `cp_reward 3`); total `independent` rep 17 + a step-4 150 cr = **450 cr / 17 rep**, identical to all prior accessible arcs, under the tuned ceiling. **Files:** the `nar_shaddaa_wasting_ward` chain in `data/worlds/clone_wars/tutorials/chains.yaml` (5 steps: talk → medicine → scholar → combat → talk; all four step rooms real loaded Nar Shaddaa slugs), `npcs_drop_generalized_questline_wasting_ward.yaml` (new, +2 NPCs, WEG-D6 stats matched to the shipped accessible-questline bands), `data/achievements.yaml` (+1), `data/worlds/clone_wars/era.yaml` (+1 npc ref), `tests/test_generalized_questline_wasting_ward.py` (new, 28). Targeted + reward-tier + skill-difficulty + directory + reachability-invariant + all-questlines-walkable green (89 passed). Clear of all avoid-lanes (no engine/parser/director/combat/harvest edits — pure additive content via existing consumers). ⚠ **Pre-existing baseline red (NOT this drop):** `test_questline_foil_winnability_band` fails on origin/main (3 cases) due to The Condemned Hull's `Bohrus Kang` melee foil (`vibroaxe`, blaster `2D+2` — below the to-hit floor + outside the sidearm set); verified identical with this drop stashed. This drop's own foil is squarely in band. — *drop generalized-questline-wasting-ward*

### 2026-06-29 — Dark-Side bounty-hunter pursuit pacing externalized to `tunables.yaml` — the prestige-domain DSP-hunter tick now has DENSITY + DIFFICULTY TUNE levers (completes the Phase-2 safe-lane set) — *drop dsp-hunter-pacing-tunables*
T3.19 **config breadth**, the **eleventh** domain after the grind faucet, craft quality, CP progression, communal-objective, encounter-density, vanity-title, the bounty board, the mission board, the smuggling board, and the environmental hazards — and the **third and final Phase-2 safe-lane domain** named in the loop prompt's set (communal_objective / hazards / dsp_hunter / smuggling / bounty_board) to ship, after communal / smuggling / bounty / hazards. It is the **prestige-domain sibling** of the hazards pacing drop: where hazards pace the wilderness attrition tick (how often it bites, how hard) **with** a credit SINK, the DSP-hunter is the "soft consequence for the dark path" — once a Force-user's Dark Side Points cross the wanted threshold (the same DSP-4 band the BH board flags under Dark-Side Notoriety) a **named, non-canon bounty hunter** picks up the trail and closes in over ticks, escalating through stages (`tracking`→`closing`→`imminent`→`at_heels`, with a live-spawn fightable climax at `at_heels`), and the only escape is to **atone** back under the threshold. The pursuit is **deterministic and prestige-only** (no credits move — defeating or atoning past the hunter IS the resolution), so unlike hazards it has **no SINK lever**; the operator-tunable surface is purely the **DENSITY/DIFFICULTY pace**. Every governing constant in `engine/dsp_hunter.py` was a hardcoded module value: the **per-DSP-tier per-tick advance** (`_STEP_MARKED` 5 / `_STEP_HUNTED` 9 / `_STEP_DARKEST` 14 — the deeper the fall, the faster the hunter closes), the **stage boundaries** on the 0..100 progress scale (`_CLOSING_AT` 40 / `_IMMINENT_AT` 75 / `_AT_HEELS_AT` 100), and the **progress ceiling** (`PROGRESS_MAX` 100, the `at_heels` climax point). This drop externalizes the lot — **7 keys** — to `data/tunables.yaml` under `dsp_hunter.*`, read at the USE SITE via live accessors (`_step_marked/_step_hunted/_step_darkest`, `progress_max`, `closing_at`/`imminent_at`/`at_heels_at`) on the proven tolerant-coerce (`_safe_int`) + `get_tunable` fallback pattern (mirrored from `engine/hazards.py`), behaviour-identical when the YAML omits / mistypes / nulls a key. The constants stay defined as the in-code fallbacks (the existing `tests/test_drop4b_dsp_hunter.py` imports `H._STEP_*`/`H.PROGRESS_MAX`/`H._CLOSING_AT` etc. as module attributes, so they keep passing). The **DSP tier bands themselves** (the 4/6/9 Marked/Hunted/Darkest cutoffs in `step_for_dsp`/`_dsp_tier`) are **deliberately NOT externalized** — they are single-sourced with `engine/bounty_board`'s notoriety tiers and the board labels and must never drift from them; only the *pace* of the hunt is tunable (a test pins that an invented `dsp_hunter.tier_*` key is ignored). The one cross-module consumer in `server/tick_handlers_progression.py` — the escape-reconcile that resets a **slipped quarry**'s dread back to the imminent boundary — is rewired from the frozen `H._IMMINENT_AT` constant to `H.imminent_at()` so the reset honors the *tuned* boundary (else an operator who raised `stage_imminent` would see the reset drift). Safety guards: the steps and stage boundaries **clamp >= 0** (a fat-fingered negative can't stall or reverse the deterministic tick), and the ceiling **clamps >= 1** (a 0/negative `progress_max` can't collapse the whole 0..N scale to a point and trap every quarry at `at_heels`). Additive config ONLY — no gameplay/schema change; every value EQUALS the in-code default, so this section is behaviour-identical to omitting it; `engine/dsp_hunter.py` (the pure pacing helpers) + the one `tick_handlers_progression.py` line are the only files touched — clear of the avoid-lanes (`harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`npc_combat_ai.py`/`director.py`/`space_commands.py`; `dsp_hunter_runtime.py`, which owns the live-spawn IO, is untouched and references none of these constants), and era/Q1-clean (no new strings; the hunter roster is all invented names). New `tests/test_dsp_hunter_pacing_tunables.py` (33): the step + boundary + ceiling accessors default/override/negative-clamp (ceiling→1)/null-fallback/corrupt-value-fallback/float-truncate; the overrides flow end-to-end through the REAL deciders — `step_for_dsp` (tier routing unchanged, only magnitudes move), `advance_progress` (advances by the tuned step, clamps to the tuned ceiling not the frozen 100, a zeroed step can't advance or raise), and `pursuit_stage` (tuned boundaries move the stage thresholds) — plus a frozen behaviour-identity sweep; and drift pins that the shipped YAML carries all 7 keys at their in-code defaults, the deciders read via the accessors (a revert fails loudly), the tier bands stay single-sourced, and the tick driver uses `H.imminent_at()` (not the frozen `H._IMMINENT_AT`). Targeted gate green (60 across the 33 new + the original `test_drop4b_dsp_hunter` pursuit/board suite + hygiene + smoke-foundation). **NEXT config-breadth candidates** (the Phase-2 safe-lane set is now complete): the **skills**-board difficulty group (`bounty.track_dc_<tier>`, the smuggling patrol/scrutiny levers deferred from the smuggling drop, mission-skill DCs); the remaining `@balance progress` legs (faction-rep magnitudes in `engine/organizations.py`, territory influence); the entertainer/medical/housing/den/debt service-economy faucets named in their telemetry-emitter comments. AVOID: `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py`/`space_commands.py` knobs.

### 2026-06-29 — Environmental-hazard balance model externalized to `tunables.yaml` — the wilderness hazard tick now has DENSITY + DIFFICULTY + SINK TUNE levers (the encounter-density sibling) — *drop hazard-tunables*
T3.19 **config breadth**, the **tenth** domain after the grind faucet, craft quality, CP progression, communal-objective, encounter-density, vanity-title, the bounty board, the mission board, and the smuggling board — and the **second Phase-2 safe-lane domain** named in the loop prompt's set (communal_objective / hazards / dsp_hunter / smuggling / bounty_board) to ship, after communal / smuggling / bounty. It is the DENSITY/DIFFICULTY sibling of the encounter-density drop (where that paces how often the wilderness OFFERS a huntable mob, this paces the periodic environmental-hazard tick — how often a hazard re-checks a character and how hard the skill-vs-DC roll is) **and** a SINK sibling of the smuggling-bust / vanity-title sinks (the urban_danger pickpocket theft). Every governing constant in `engine/hazards.py` was a hardcoded module-level value: `HAZARD_CHECK_INTERVAL` (300s — the per-`(char, room)` re-check cadence), the per-type `base_difficulty` map (extreme_heat 10 / toxic_atmosphere 12 / urban_danger 10 / radiation 15), the shared `(severity-1)*3` DC step **duplicated verbatim at three call sites** (the `check_hazard_for_character` fallback, the `_check_wilderness_hazard` synthetic-room build, and `set_room_hazard`), the `_EXTREME_HEAT_TOD_MOD` clock-grading band (day +3 / dusk 0 / night −4) plus its `max(5, …)` DC floor, and the urban_danger pickpocket SINK's `0.05` credit fraction / `max(50, severity*100)` cap. This drop externalizes the lot — **13 keys** — to `data/tunables.yaml` under `hazards.*`, read at the USE SITE via tolerant accessors (`_safe_int`/`_safe_float` + `get_tunable` fallback, mirrored from `engine/bounty_board.py`), and **collapses the triplicated DC formula into one `_scaled_difficulty(type, severity)` helper** the three sites now share (so a per-type reprice or step change can't silently desync them). The keys: `hazards.check_interval_seconds`; `hazards.difficulty_<type>` (the four types); `hazards.severity_difficulty_step`; `hazards.heat_tod_{day,dusk,night}` + `hazards.heat_difficulty_floor`; and the SINK trio `hazards.theft_credit_fraction` / `hazards.theft_floor` / `hazards.theft_per_severity`. The pickpocket theft stays **funnel-routed** (`adjust_credits(…, "hazard_theft")`) so it already surfaces as a credit sink in `@balance flows` / `@economy velocity` — the observe→tune loop this closes. The **double-scale fix is preserved** (QA hazards 2026-06-23): `_scaled_difficulty` computes from the raw base, while `check_hazard_for_character` still uses a *stored* `difficulty` as-is (only the no-stored-difficulty fallback re-scales) — so a severity-3 hazard stays DC 16, not the old DC 22. Safety guards: the interval **clamps >= 0** (0 = a check every hazard tick; a negative can't underflow the `(now - last) >= window` gate), the DC **clamps >= 1** (a fat-fingered negative step can't hand the player an auto-pass DC), the heat TOD modifier is left **unclamped** (night relief is legitimately negative) but its floor keeps the post-modifier DC sane, and the theft fraction **clamps to [0,1]** with both magnitudes **>= 0** (the SINK can never PAY the victim). Additive config ONLY — no gameplay/schema change; every value EQUALS the in-code default (the constants stay defined as fallbacks, so the external importers — `test_guide_24` / `test_guide_19` / `test_lane_e2b_clock` / the craft mitigation suites — keep their pinned values); `engine/hazards.py` is the only engine file touched (NOT an avoid-lane; clear of `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py`/`space_commands.py` and the fun-drive surfaces); era-clean (no new strings). New `tests/test_hazard_tunables.py` (24): the accessors default/override/clamp (interval >= 0, DC >= 1, theft fraction → [0,1])/null-fallback/corrupt-value-fallback + the heat-band negative-allowed/unknown-band-no-op cases; the interval override flows end-to-end through the REAL `_should_check` cooldown gate, and the theft overrides flow through the REAL `check_hazard_for_character` to the metered `adjust_credits("hazard_theft")` debit (a forced-fail urban_danger room yields the exact `min(fraction*credits, max(floor, severity*per_sev))`, the funnel tag is asserted, and a debit is never a credit); plus drift pins that the shipped YAML carries all 13 keys at their in-code defaults with the per-type DCs matching the live `HAZARD_TYPES` map, that the three difficulty sites share the one helper (the inline formula is gone), and that the accessors read via `get_tunable` (a revert fails loudly). Targeted gate green (24 new + 192 across the hazard/guide/wilderness consumers — `test_guide_24`/`test_guide_19`/`test_lane_e2b_clock`/`test_qa_batch2_tail`/`test_craft_p0`/`test_craft_p2_gundark_drop_e`/`test_field_kit_drop_b`/`test_fork_env_recovery`/`test_t2_wenc`/`test_lane_d_geonosis` + hygiene + smoke-foundation). **NEXT config-breadth candidates:** the last named Phase-2 safe-lane domain **dsp_hunter** (prestige-only — its pursuit-progress / stage-threshold / spawn-cadence knobs, no credit lever); the **skills**-board difficulty group (`bounty.track_dc_<tier>`, the smuggling patrol/scrutiny levers, mission-skill DCs); the remaining `@balance progress` legs (faction-rep magnitudes in `engine/organizations.py`, territory influence); the entertainer/medical/housing service-economy faucets. AVOID: `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py`/`space_commands.py` knobs.

### 2026-06-28 — Smuggling reward model externalized to `tunables.yaml` — the `@balance objectives` board now has the matching smuggling-FAUCET TUNE levers (completes the reward triad) — *drop smuggling-reward-tunables*
T3.19 **config breadth**, the **ninth** domain after the grind faucet, craft quality, CP progression, communal-objective, encounter-density, vanity-title, the bounty board, and the mission board — and the **third and final leg of the `@balance objectives` board's reward triad** (the `objective` telemetry rolls up start→complete→abandon + the `reward` column for `mission` / `bounty` / `smuggling` tier pay; mission + bounty shipped the prior two fires). The `@balance objectives` board lets an operator OBSERVE the smuggling faucet (the `smuggling`-tagged rows from `engine/smuggling`'s `emit_objective` sites), but the reward model it informs was a wall of hardcoded constants. This drop externalizes the whole **PAY model** to `data/tunables.yaml` under `smuggling.*` (28 keys). Smuggling has **two live pay paths** — a direct per-tier job and a multi-planet ROUTE job (the live board carries both: a guaranteed grey-market tier-0 job plus weighted-random routes) — so externalizing only one would leave a **phantom lever** that barely moves the faucet; this drop covers both: the **4 per-tier bands** (`smuggling.pay_<tier>_min`/`_max` from `TIER_PAY_RANGE`, grey 200–500 → spice 5000–15000), the **5 route bands** (`smuggling.route_<key>_pay_min`/`_max` from the `ROUTE_TIERS` pay overrides — local/blackmkt mirror their tier bands while interplan/spicerun/corerun pay their own route rates), the **5 route spawn-rarity weights** (`smuggling.route_weight_<key>` from the new named `ROUTE_SPAWN_WEIGHTS` constant, replacing the inline `[40,25,20,10,5]` — local common → core run rare), and the **bust SINK** (`smuggling.fine_fraction_<tier>` + `_default` from `FINE_FRACTION_BY_TIER`/`FINE_FRACTION`, apex tiers keep more so patrol risk is the variance). Read at the USE SITE via four live accessors — `_tier_pay_range(tier)` / `_route_pay_range(key, lo, hi)` / `_route_weights()` / the rewritten `_fine_fraction(tier)` — on the proven tolerant-coerce (`_safe_int`/`_safe_float`) + `get_tunable` fallback pattern (mirrored from `engine/bounty_board.py`), behaviour-identical when the YAML omits / mistypes / nulls a key. A new `_TIER_KEY = {t: t.name.lower() for t in CargoTier}` map ties the keys to the enum names so a tier rename can't silently desync. Safety guards: pay magnitudes **clamp >= 0** with **hi clamps >= lo** so `random.randint` can never get an empty range from an inverted band; a **zeroed route-weight set falls back to the in-code weights** so `random.choices` can't raise "Total of weights must be greater than zero"; the fine fraction **clamps to [0, 1]** (a bad value can neither PAY the smuggler on a bust nor confiscate more than the reward). The **patrol-chance / scrutiny difficulty levers are deliberately left hardcoded** — `PATROL_DIFFICULTY` / `TIER_PATROL_CHANCE` / the route patrol overrides / `CHECKPOINT_*` boosts / lockdown +0.30/+5 / `PLANET_PATROL_FREQUENCY` form a cohesive **encounters/scrutiny** group (the in-code `CHECKPOINT_*` comment already flags them for "a tunables file alongside the lockdown values"), a distinct future config-breadth drop, kept out here to preserve this drop's "matching board" framing (the `objective` board observes reward, not patrol difficulty) — exactly as the bounty fire left `TRACK_DIFFICULTIES`. Additive config ONLY — no gameplay/schema change; values remain the documented defaults; `engine/smuggling.py` is the only engine file touched (NOT an avoid-lane; clear of `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py`/`space_commands.py` and the fun-drive surfaces); era-clean (no new strings; the in-code constants stay defined as fallbacks, so the external consumers that import them — the smoke `smuggling_loop` scenario, `test_guide_06_economy_authoritative`, `test_tier2_tuning_batch` — keep their values). New `tests/test_smuggling_reward_tunables.py` (29): the four accessors default/override/negative-clamp/hi≥lo-guard/null-fallback/corrupt-value-fallback/float-truncate + the fine-fraction [0,1] clamp + the apex-tier-keeps-more invariant + the zeroed-weight fallback; the overrides flow end-to-end through the REAL `generate_job` (a collapsed tier band yields the exact reward; a route pay override flows in while the tier is unchanged; a fine override changes the fine; a zeroed-weight board still generates without raising) and a frozen behaviour-identity sweep (40 grey-market jobs stay in [200,500]·rounded-to-50 with a 50% fine); plus drift pins that the shipped YAML carries every one of the 28 keys at its in-code default with no stray/missing keys, and the deciders read via the accessors (a revert fails loudly). Targeted gate green (110 across the 29 new + the smuggling smoke loop + contraband-scan-checkpoint + a5-spice-demand + economy-validation + guide-06-economy + tier2-tuning + hygiene + smoke-foundation). **NEXT config-breadth candidates:** the **skills**-board difficulty group (`bounty.track_dc_<tier>`, the smuggling patrol/scrutiny levers just deferred, mission-skill DCs); the remaining `@balance progress` legs (faction-rep magnitudes in `engine/organizations.py`, territory influence); the entertainer/medical/housing service-economy faucets. AVOID: `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py`/`space_commands.py` knobs.

### 2026-06-28 — Mission-board reward model externalized to `tunables.yaml` — the `@balance objectives` board now has the matching mission-FAUCET TUNE levers — *drop mission-reward-tunables*
T3.19 **config breadth**, the **eighth** domain after the grind faucet, craft quality, CP progression, communal-objective, encounter-density, vanity-title, and the bounty board — and the mission-faucet complement to that just-shipped bounty model (the two faucets the `@balance objectives` board's `reward` column tracks side by side). The `@balance objectives` board renders the per-kind start→complete funnel **with a `reward` column** (the `mission`-tagged rows), letting an operator OBSERVE the mission faucet — but the reward model it informs was hardcoded. T3.19 Phase 1 had externalized only the **smuggling-tier ceiling** (`mission.reward_smuggling_max`); the rest of the curve was frozen `PAY_RANGES` / `SPAWN_WEIGHTS` / the inline `DISTRESS_REWARD_BONUS` constant. This drop completes the externalization: the full **14-type reward band** (`mission.reward_<type>_min`/`_max`, delivery 100–300 → space-intercept 2000–3000), the **spawn-rarity weights** (`mission.spawn_weight_<type>`, ground types only — delivery always-on → social/slicing rare), and the **distress emergency premium** (`mission.distress_reward_bonus_pct`, +30% while a `DISTRESS_SIGNAL` world-event is live) all join `tunables.yaml` under `mission.*`. Read at the USE SITE via three live accessors — `_pay_range(type)` / `_spawn_weights()` / `_distress_bonus_pct()` — on the proven tolerant-coerce (`_safe_int`/`_safe_float`) + `get_tunable` fallback pattern (mirrored from `engine/bounty_board.py`), behaviour-identical when the YAML omits / mistypes / nulls a key. The **Phase-1 `mission.reward_smuggling_max` key is preserved** — under the consistent `mission.reward_<type>_max` naming it *is* the smuggling-type max (`SMUGGLING.value == "smuggling"`), so the legacy lever and operator config keep working (the special-case branch in `_scale_reward` folds into `_pay_range`). Note these `mission.*` keys are a **distinct namespace** from the `bounty.*` bounty-BOARD keys — the mission `BOUNTY` *type* is a separate faucet (a contract picked off the mission board), so `mission.reward_bounty_*` and `bounty.reward_*` coexist without collision. Safety guards: magnitudes **clamp >= 0** (a negative can't pay a debit), **hi clamps >= lo** so `random.randint` can never get an empty range from an inverted band (the smuggling tier already had its max lever; every type now has the symmetric guard), the guaranteed-delivery slot's `randint(lo, hi//2)` gains a `max(lo, …)` so a collapsed delivery band can't make it raise, a **zeroed weight-set falls back to the in-code weights** so `random.choices` can't raise "Total of weights must be greater than zero", and the distress premium clamps >= 0 (the worst a bad value does is no premium). Rewires `_scale_reward` (band), `_pick_type` (weights), `distress_mission_bonus` (premium), and `generate_board`'s delivery line; removes a dead `lo, hi = PAY_RANGES[COMBAT]` assignment immediately overwritten by `_scale_reward`. Additive config ONLY — no gameplay/schema change; the values remain the documented `PAY_RANGES`/`SPAWN_WEIGHTS`/`DISTRESS_REWARD_BONUS` defaults; `engine/missions.py` is the only engine file touched (NOT an avoid-lane; clear of `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py`/`space_commands.py` and the fun-drive surfaces); era-clean (no new strings). New `tests/test_mission_reward_tunables.py` (37): the three accessors default/override/negative-clamp/hi≥lo-guard/all-zero-fallback/null-fallback/corrupt-value-fallback/float-truncate; the overrides flow end-to-end through the REAL `_scale_reward` (a pinned band is deterministic; the Phase-1 smuggling-max key still collapses the band; a negative/inverted band never debits or raises), `_pick_type` (a single non-zero weight on a never-biased type forces it — the Director singleton is nulled so the alert-bias path can't perturb the draw; all-zero still returns a valid type), `distress_mission_bonus` (a +100% premium doubles pay; a negative yields no premium), and `generate_board` (a collapsed delivery band yields reward 100 without raising); plus drift pins that the shipped YAML carries every key at the in-code default, the Phase-1 `mission.reward_smuggling_max` is still 5000, all keys are documented, and the deciders read via the accessors (a revert fails loudly). Targeted gate green (97 across the 37 new + the T3.19-Phase1-econ-knobs + worldevent-flag-consumers + guide-06-economy suites; plus 233 across the full mission consumer set — era-aware/chain-missions/faction-missions; plus hygiene + smoke-foundation). **NEXT config-breadth candidates:** the **skills**-board track DCs (`bounty.track_dc_<tier>` / mission-skill DCs); the remaining `@balance progress` legs (faction-rep magnitudes, territory influence); the entertainer/medical/housing service-economy faucets named in their telemetry-emitter comments. AVOID: `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py`/`space_commands.py` knobs.

### 2026-06-28 — Vanity-title cost curve externalized to `tunables.yaml` — the `@balance progress` board now has the matching prestige-SINK TUNE levers — *drop vanity-title-cost-tunables*
T3.19 **config breadth**, the **sixth** domain after the grind faucet, craft quality, CP progression, communal-objective, and encounter-density — and the **first SINK-side** lever (the prior five were faucet/density knobs). The `@balance progress` board's "Vanity titles … cr soaked" line lets an operator OBSERVE the prestige sink (which tiers sell, how much veteran credit it soaks against take-up), but the 8-tier cost curve it informs was a hardcoded `VANITY_TITLES` constant in `engine/titles.py` — the very lever the producer's own `_emit_title_telemetry` comment names verbatim ("the signal for tuning the 8-tier `VANITY_TITLES` cost curve (2k→400k): a dead top tier means it is priced past reach; everyone parked on the cheap tiers means the sink is too shallow"). This drop externalizes the **8** tier costs to `data/tunables.yaml` under `title.cost_<key>` (wayfarer 2k → luminary 400k), read at the USE SITE via one accessor `title_cost(key, default)` on the proven tolerant-coerce + `get_tunable` fallback pattern (behaviour-identical when the YAML omits / mistypes / nulls a key). The accessor **clamps to >= 0** (a negative would PAY the buyer to take a prestige sink; 0 = a deliberately-free tier) and **truncates a float to int** (credits are whole). It rewires BOTH consumers: `catalog_lines` (so the `+title` catalog renders the repriced figure AND its owned/buy/locked affordability mark uses it) and `purchase_title` (so the actual `vanity_title` ledger debit is the resolved amount, with the insufficient-funds `short` computed against it). The `tier` price-index in the purchase telemetry is a structural `VANITY_TITLES` lookup by key (not a magnitude) and is left untouched; `EARNED_TITLES` are deed grants with no credit movement and have no cost lever. Additive config ONLY — no gameplay/faucet/schema change (it's a pre-existing pure SINK; externalizing its cost adds no new faucet); the costs remain the documented defaults; `engine/titles.py` is the only engine file touched (NOT an avoid-lane; clear of `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py` and the fun-drive's combat/parser/client surfaces); era-clean (faction-neutral, no GCW-era / canon strings — the catalog already enforces B3). New `tests/test_vanity_title_cost_tunables.py` (16): the accessor default/override/negative-clamp-to-zero/float-truncate/null-fallback/corrupt-value-fallback; the override flows end-to-end through the REAL `catalog_lines` (a reprice changes the displayed cost AND flips affordability both ways) and the REAL `purchase_title` debit (default debits the in-code cost, a reprice debits the tunable amount, a price-out blocks the buy with the resolved `short` and moves no credits); and drift pins that the shipped YAML carries all 8 keys at the in-code `VANITY_TITLES` costs, every tier key is documented, and both consumers read via `get_tunable` (a revert fails loudly). Targeted gate green (44 across the 16 new + the vanity-titles branch suite + the title-telemetry suite + the tunables foundation + hygiene + smoke-foundation). **NEXT config-breadth candidates:** the remaining `@balance progress` legs (faction-rep `REP_GAINS` magnitudes in `engine/organizations.py`; spacer-quest per-step pay; territory influence) then hazards / dsp_hunter / smuggling / bounty_board knobs. AVOID: `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py` knobs.

### 2026-06-28 — Wilderness encounter-DENSITY levers externalized to `tunables.yaml` — the `@balance encounters` board now has the matching TUNE levers (the mob-DENSITY complement to the grind faucet) — *drop encounter-density-tunables*
T3.19 **config breadth**, the **fifth** domain after the grind faucet, craft quality, CP progression, and communal-objective. Where `grind.*` externalized what a huntable kill PAYS (`engine/hunting_rewards.py`), this externalizes how often the wilderness even OFFERS one to kill — the mob-DENSITY leg of the same grind funnel. The `@balance encounters` board (the `wild_encounter` events: fire-rate, `reason` mix — chance_miss / on_cooldown / no_eligible_entries / averted_by_excluder / ok — and threat-band) is the read-side signal; its own producer comment in `engine/wilderness_encounters.py` names the tuning targets verbatim ("the signal for tuning `base_chance_per_move`, the 60-second cooldown, threat-band pool authoring … does the wilderness the grind loop was just redirected into actually spawn enough to grind?", the 2026-06-24 realignment that stripped 158 civic/SECURED placements into the wilderness). Of the three named targets, `base_chance_per_move` is already a **per-pool DATA field** (each region's encounter YAML — operator-tunable per region) and threat-band eligibility is **per-entry DATA** (`min_band`/`max_band`), so neither needs a global lever; the two genuinely-hardcoded GLOBAL module constants were the gap: `ENCOUNTER_COOLDOWN_SECONDS` (60 — the per-character cooldown the board comment names, THE grind-density pacing lever) and `ANIMAL_EXCLUDER_AVERT_CHANCE` (0.5 — the Merr-Sonn deterrent's flat turn-away chance). This drop externalizes the **two** to `data/tunables.yaml` under `encounter.*`, read at the USE SITE via two live accessors (`cooldown_seconds()` / `animal_excluder_avert_chance()`) on the proven `_safe_int`/`_safe_float` tolerant-coerce + `get_tunable` fallback pattern (behaviour-identical when the YAML omits / mistypes / nulls the key). `cooldown_seconds()` rewires the `_is_on_cooldown` window check and **clamps to >= 0** (a negative would underflow the `(now - last) < window` comparison; 0 = a roll every move). `animal_excluder_avert_chance()` rewires the avert roll and **clamps to the [0,1] probability range** (it is compared against `random()` in [0,1) — a value >= 1 always averts, < 0 never; clamping keeps it a probability). The module-level `DEFAULT_BASE_CHANCE_PER_MOVE` constant has **no runtime consumer** (verified by tree-wide grep — `base_chance_per_move` is read only off the per-pool field) so it is left **untouched** with a note: externalizing a dead constant would be a phantom. Additive config ONLY — no gameplay/faucet/sink/schema change; the constants remain the documented defaults; `engine/wilderness_encounters.py` is the only engine file touched (NOT an avoid-lane; clear of `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py` and the fun-drive's combat/parser/client surfaces); era-clean (wilderness fauna/thugs, no GCW-era strings). New `tests/test_encounter_density_tunables.py` (17): the two accessors default/override/negative-cooldown-clamp/avert-[0,1]-clamp/null-fallback/corrupt-value-fallback/int-float-coerce; the cooldown override flows end-to-end through the REAL `_is_on_cooldown` gate (tighten releases sooner, widen holds longer, 0 never gates) and the avert override flows through the REAL `_roll_encounter_impl` animal-excluder path (avert=1.0 turns the creature away, avert=0.0 lets it fire, the unset 0.5 default is behaviour-identical to legacy); and drift pins that the shipped YAML carries the two keys at the in-code defaults and the use sites read via `get_tunable` (a revert fails loudly). Targeted gate green (113 across the 17 new + the wilderness-encounter / band-eligibility / wild_encounter-telemetry / flood-encounter suites + the tunables foundation + the grind config-breadth sibling + hygiene + smoke-foundation). **NEXT config-breadth candidates:** hazards / dsp_hunter / smuggling / bounty_board reward/difficulty knobs (grep each for hardcoded constants its `@balance` / `@economy` board observes). AVOID: `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py` knobs.

### 2026-06-28 — Communal-objective (dark-side cult) difficulty + reward knobs externalized to `tunables.yaml` — the `@balance communal` board now has the matching TUNE levers — *drop communal-objective-tunables*
T3.19 **config breadth** (the WRITE-side complement to the telemetry sweep), the **fourth** domain — and the **first Phase-2 safe-lane domain** the loop prompt named (communal_objective / hazards / dsp_hunter / smuggling / bounty_board) — after the grind faucet, craft quality, and CP progression. The `@balance communal` board gives the read-side signal for the dark-side cult uprising (design III.3): menace-tick + tier-escalation count (how fast the cult gathers strength) and strike count + success rate (how the community pushes back). And the producer's own emit-site comments in `engine/communal_objective_runtime.py` name their tuning target verbatim — "direct input to tuning strike_difficulty/DEADLINE_HOURS post-launch" and "for MENACE_PER_MINUTE / DEADLINE_HOURS / strike balance: does the cult outrun the community". But the seven levers that signal exists to inform were **hardcoded module constants** in `engine/communal_objective.py`: an operator could OBSERVE the cult win/loss economy via `@balance communal` but could not TUNE it without a code edit + redeploy — the observe→tune loop half-open exactly as grind's / craft's / cp's were. This drop externalizes the **seven** — the four **difficulty** levers `MENACE_START` (35) / `MENACE_PER_MINUTE` (0.35) / `DEADLINE_HOURS` (48) / `STRIKE_COOLDOWN_S` (600) and the three **prestige-reward** levers `REP_FLOOR` (3) / `REP_MAX` (15) / `TITLE_SHARE_THRESHOLD` (0.10) — to `data/tunables.yaml` under `communal.*`, read at the USE SITE via seven live accessors (`menace_start()`/`menace_per_minute()`/`deadline_hours()`/`strike_cooldown_s()`/`rep_floor()`/`rep_max()`/`title_share_threshold()`) on the proven `_safe_int`/`_safe_float` tolerant-coerce + `get_tunable` fallback pattern (behaviour-identical when the YAML omits / mistypes / nulls the key). Routed at **every** use site across BOTH the pure module (the `advance_menace` escalation, `reward_rep_for_share`, `earns_title`, and the `viewer_contribution_line` strike-cooldown rally-board view) **and** the DB-touching runtime (the two objective-POST sites + the per-character strike-cooldown check in `engine/communal_objective_runtime.py`). Magnitudes **clamp to >= 0** so a fat-fingered negative can never invert a lever: a negative `menace_per_minute` would otherwise make the cult **self-route** over time (turning the objective into a guaranteed community win), and a negative rep would **DEBIT** a winning contributor (violating the design's opportunities-never-penalties contract). **`deadline_hours()` clamps to >= 1** (not 0) because a 0/negative window posts every objective already past its deadline — instantly **LOST = unwinnable** — so it floors at the minimum hour that keeps the win path reachable (the win-window analog of `cp.ticks_per_cp`'s divisor floor). `reward_rep_for_share` snapshots `rep_floor()` + `rep_max()` once each into locals so both endpoints of the linear rep scale come from one consistent config read. The pure **0..`MENACE_MAX`** menace axis is mechanism, not a balance magnitude, and stays a hardcoded constant. Additive config ONLY — no gameplay/faucet/sink/schema change; the constants remain the documented defaults; `engine/communal_objective.py` + `engine/communal_objective_runtime.py` are the only files touched (NOT an avoid-lane; clear of `engine/director.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py` and the fun-drive's combat/parser/client surfaces); era-clean (the cult is an invented non-canon dark-side faction, no GCW-era strings). New `tests/test_communal_objective_tunables.py` (23): the seven accessors default/override/negative-clamp (with the explicit `deadline_hours` floors-at-1-not-0 win-window case)/null-fallback/bad-value-fallback/int-float-coerce; the overrides flow end-to-end through the REAL deciders (escalation pace, the rep curve, title eligibility, the cooldown rally-board view) and **negative-safety** is proven behaviourally (a negative `menace_per_minute` can't self-route; a negative rep never debits a winner; a loss pays nothing regardless of config); behaviour-identical to the frozen legacy numbers when unset (pinned against literals, not the live constants); and drift pins that the shipped YAML carries the seven keys at the in-code defaults, the accessors read via `get_tunable` (no import-time freeze), and **both** the engine and runtime use sites call the accessors not the raw `CO.MENACE_START`/`CO.DEADLINE_HOURS`/`CO.STRIKE_COOLDOWN_S` constants (a revert fails loudly). Targeted gate green (132 across the 23 new + the communal telemetry/menace/cult suites + the grind/craft/cp config-breadth siblings + hygiene + smoke-foundation). **NEXT config-breadth candidates:** hazards / dsp_hunter / smuggling / bounty_board reward/difficulty knobs (grep each for hardcoded constants its `@balance` board observes). AVOID: `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py` knobs.

### 2026-06-28 — CP progression faucet knobs externalized to `tunables.yaml` — the `@balance progression` board now has the matching TUNE levers — *drop cp-progression-tunables*
T3.19 **config breadth** (the WRITE-side complement to the telemetry sweep), the **third** domain after the grind faucet and craft quality. The `@balance progression` board + the `cp_income`/`cp_spend` telemetry give the read-side signal for the Character-Point tick economy — income by source (passive / scene / kudos / ai_eval / milestone), the tick→CP conversion rate, and weekly-cap pressure — and the telemetry producer docstring names its tuning target verbatim: "the signal Brian wants to tune the CP levers (`TICKS_PER_CP`, `WEEKLY_CAP_TICKS`, `KUDOS_TICKS`, `SCENE_TICKS_PER_POSE`, …)". But the CP faucet's reward/cap levers were **hardcoded module constants** in `engine/cp_engine.py`: an operator could OBSERVE the CP economy via `@balance progression` but could not TUNE it without a code edit + redeploy — the observe→tune loop half-open exactly as grind's and craft's were. This drop externalizes the **nine** reward/cap magnitudes — `TICKS_PER_CP` (200), `WEEKLY_CAP_TICKS` (400), `PASSIVE_TICKS_PER_DAY` (10), `SCENE_MIN_POSES` (3), `SCENE_TICKS_PER_POSE` (2), `SCENE_MAX_TICKS` (60), `KUDOS_TICKS` (35), `KUDOS_PER_WEEK` (3), `AI_MAX_TICKS_PER_EVAL` (15) — to `data/tunables.yaml` under `cp.*`, read at the USE SITE via nine live accessors (`ticks_per_cp()`/`weekly_cap_ticks()`/`passive_ticks_per_day()`/`scene_min_poses()`/`scene_ticks_per_pose()`/`scene_max_ticks()`/`kudos_ticks()`/`kudos_per_week()`/`ai_max_ticks_per_eval()`) on a shared `_cp_tunable(key, default, floor)` helper that falls back to the in-code constant when the YAML omits (or mis-types/nulls) the key (purely additive — behaviour-identical to today). Magnitudes **clamp to >= 0** (a fat-fingered negative can never invert an income source into a tick DEBIT); **`ticks_per_cp()` clamps to >= 1** because it is the tick→CP **DIVISOR** and a 0 would raise `ZeroDivisionError` on the next award (a real defense-in-depth concern unique to the divisor). Where a lever is read more than once in a single operation it is snapshotted into a local first — the conversion in `_award_ticks` reads `per_cp` once so both sides of the CP boundary use one consistent divisor, and each award method reads `cap`/`min_poses`/`kudos_award` once. The **structural/timing** constants (`WEEK_SECONDS`, the scene-cooldown/kudos-lockout windows, `PASSIVE_CHECK_INTERVAL`, `ADMIN_CAP_FLAG_WEEKS`, `AI_EVAL_FLOOR`) are deliberately left hardcoded — they are mechanism, not the balance magnitudes the board informs. **Display consumers routed for consistency:** the `cpstatus` text (`parser/cp_commands.py`) and the web HUD CP sidebar (`server/session.py`) now read the live value via the accessors instead of the frozen constant import, so an operator tune never makes the displayed conversion-rate/cap lie. Additive config ONLY — no gameplay/faucet/sink/schema change; `engine/cp_engine.py` is the only engine file touched (NOT an avoid-lane); era-clean; `engine/director.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py` + the fun-drive's combat/parser/client surfaces untouched. New `tests/test_cp_progression_tunables.py` (21): the nine accessors default/override/negative-clamp (with the explicit `ticks_per_cp` clamps-to-1-not-0 divisor case)/null-fallback/bad-value-fallback/float-coerce; the overrides flow end-to-end through the REAL award paths (scene ticks-per-pose / scene-max cap / min-poses gate, kudos payout / weekly-kudos cap / weekly-tick cap, the tick→CP conversion, passive trickle) on in-memory stub DBs, plus a `ticks_per_cp=0` award proves no `ZeroDivisionError`; behaviour-identical to the legacy numbers when unset; and drift pins that the shipped YAML carries the nine keys at the in-code defaults, the helper reads via `get_tunable`, and every decision site (engine + cpstatus + HUD) calls the accessors not the frozen constants. **invariant-auditor fan-out clean.** Targeted gate green (21 new + cp-income telemetry + cp-spend telemetry + progression rollup + cp-audit + smoke progression + cp-trickle fork + command-syntax + hygiene + smoke-foundation). **NEXT config-breadth candidates:** communal_objective / hazards / dsp_hunter / smuggling / bounty_board reward knobs (the loop-prompt's named Phase-2 safe-lane domains) — grep each for hardcoded reward/difficulty constants its `@balance` board observes. AVOID: `harvest.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py`/`director.py` knobs.

### 2026-06-28 — Craft-quality knobs externalized to `tunables.yaml` — the `@balance craft` board now has the matching TUNE levers — *drop craft-quality-tunables*
T3.19 **config breadth** (the WRITE-side complement to the telemetry sweep), the second domain after the grind faucet. But those five produced-item quality levers — `QUALITY_MULT_BASE` (1.0) / `QUALITY_MULT_MAX` (1.3) / `QUALITY_MULT_CRIT` (1.5) / `QUALITY_MULT_EXP_CRIT` (2.0) / `QUALITY_PARTIAL` (0.5) in `engine/crafting.py` — were **hardcoded module constants**: an operator could OBSERVE the craft outcome distribution via `@balance craft` but could not TUNE it without a code edit + redeploy. This is the proven Phase-1 pattern (read-at-use-site + null/bad-value/negative-safe), extended to the first **non-economy** balance-telemetry domain — crafting quality is item-stat, not credits, so there is no faucet/sink to pair. Additive config ONLY — no gameplay/faucet/sink/schema change; the constants remain the documented defaults; `engine/crafting.py` is the only engine file touched (NOT an avoid-lane), and only to route the existing quality decision through the accessors (the produced quality for any given roll is byte-identical when the YAML carries the shipped defaults); era-clean; `engine/director.py`/`combat.py`/`death.py` + the fun-drive's combat/parser/client surfaces untouched. ### 2026-06-28 — `@balance craft`: the crafting-outcome calibration telemetry funnel finally has a consumer — *drop telemetry-craft-rollup*
T3.19 telemetry READ-SIDE quality — the **last leg of the dedicated-producer producer→consumer sweep** the previous six fires each peeled a layer of (`ff2032d` chain, `375d1e9` session, `1c46d9a` skill_check, `cbfb765` economy/cp_spend, `923e3dd` progression, `3605042` command), each resolved best/most-complete by **adding the consumer, not dropping the producer**. The command fire's own follow-up note named **`craft`** as the one genuinely-distinct producer still read-side-dropped; this drop closes it, and with it the dedicated-producer telemetry-coverage sweep is **genuinely complete**. `engine/crafting.py::resolve_craft` (the unified craft-completion chokepoint that wraps `_resolve_craft_impl` then emits) has long fired one fail-open `craft` event per assembly — `schematic`/`output_key`, the gated `skill`/`difficulty`, the outcome distribution `success`/`partial`/`fumble` (a partial is a success that produced a lower-quality item, so it carries `success=True` AND `partial=True`), `quality` (the produced item's quality multiplier, 0 when nothing was produced), and `critical`/`margin`/`experiment`. The emit-site docstring states its whole purpose: "the per-schematic success/partial/fumble + quality distribution (the direct tuning signal for the `QUALITY_MULT_*` knobs and per-schematic difficulty) was unobserved." Added a single `craft` rollup to `summarize()` (crafts + distinct crafters; the success/partial/fumble/crit/experiment outcome distribution; **mean produced-item quality** over crafts that produced something — a fumble's quality-0 is excluded so it doesn't drag the QUALITY_MULT signal down spuriously; success rate **by WEG difficulty band** — reusing the `_difficulty_band`/`_DIFFICULTY_BANDS` helper the skill board introduced, since craft is skill-check-shaped; and **by schematic**, top-12 by craft volume, each row carrying that recipe's success rate + mean quality) + a `@balance craft` sub-board (`_render_craft`; aliases `craft`/`crafting`/`crafts`; also in the overview). The two **self-maintaining** sub-board guards (`@balance.md` help + Guide_27 §3, both derive the canonical sub-board list from the live `usage` string) forced the doc reconciles to land in the same drop — both gain the `craft` entry (+ the two hand-maintained parametrized lists in `test_guide_27_administration.py` / `test_help_balance_reconcile.py`), no drift. The `@balance` telemetry dashboard now surfaces **every dedicated producer** with a rollup + board (grind/cp/objective/wild_encounter/communal/chain/session/skill_check/economy/progression/command/craft); the only un-rolled-up event types are credit-leg txn events whose money flow is already captured by `flows`/`@economy`.
