### 2026-06-25 — Reliability: track the telnet read-loop task so it can't be GC'd mid-flight — *drop telnet-readloop-taskref* (Opus loop)
Closes a real async-lifecycle defect from the launch-readiness "async task-ref hygiene" backlog. `GameServer.handle_new_session` started the per-connection telnet input pump as a **fire-and-forget** task — `asyncio.create_task(self._telnet_read_loop(reader, session))` with the handle discarded. But asyncio keeps only a *weak* reference to tasks created via `create_task` ("a task that isn't referenced elsewhere may get garbage-collected at any time, even before it's done" — asyncio docs), so under GC pressure the loop feeding a telnet player's input could be collected mid-flight, **silently killing that player's connection** (their keystrokes stop reaching the game with no error). The two WebSocket read loops (`server/web_client.py` aiohttp path + the legacy `server/websocket_handler.py`) were already safe — each holds its task in a local kept alive across an active `await`/`finally` — so only the telnet path was exposed. Fix: a new `GameServer._read_tasks: set[asyncio.Task]` holds a strong reference for each read loop's lifetime, and a new `_spawn_read_loop` helper registers the task + attaches a `add_done_callback(self._read_tasks.discard)` so the set self-prunes on completion (never grows unbounded). `stop()` now also cancels any outstanding read loops for a clean shutdown (they normally self-terminate on transport EOF, but shutdown shouldn't depend on that). The read loop's behavior is otherwise byte-identical (EOF still feeds `quit` unless already `DISCONNECTING`). Server-only; no schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean; no player-facing string change. New `tests/test_telnet_read_loop_taskref_2026_06_25.py` (3: strong-ref-held-then-discarded with no leak + `quit` on EOF, reference retained while the loop is mid-flight, and `DISCONNECTING` suppresses the `quit` feed) — exercises the real `_spawn_read_loop`/`_telnet_read_loop` via a lightweight stub to avoid the full-server boot. Gate green (the 3 new + hygiene + smoke foundation, 17 passed).

### 2026-06-25 — Guide_01 re-verify pass: realign §2/§11 to the `+check` funnel — *drop guide01-check-funnel-reverify* (Opus loop)
The Opus-owned guides lane's standing item ("re-verify any guide whose subsystem shipped since its last pass"). Updated the example to the real format + added a second example demonstrating a tool credit (`[Code Slicer]`, a real `data/schematics.yaml` security tool — no phantom item) and a critical, and expanded the prose to teach that a manual `+check` now honors buffs/tools/environment. New `tests/test_guide_01_core_mechanics.py::TestCheckFunnelDataFlow` (5: CheckCommand calls the funnel and no longer imports difficulty_check; the guide flow routes through perform_skill_check + the phantom difficulty_check flow stays dead; the modifier stack + telemetry event are documented; the render format matches `pool_str = total`; Code Slicer is a real tool). 44 guide-01 tests green. ### 2026-06-25 — `+check` funnel compliance: route the manual skill check through `perform_skill_check` — *drop check-funnel-compliance* (Opus loop)
Closes a hard-invariant (funnel) violation from the launch-readiness obvious-fix backlog. `+check` (`parser/d6_commands.py` `CheckCommand`) — a player's manual "roll my skill vs a difficulty" — resolved the roll with the inline `engine.dice.difficulty_check(pool)`, **bypassing the `perform_skill_check` chokepoint** that every system-driven check uses. So a manual `+check` honored NONE of the mechanics the funnel layers on at the chokepoint: active buffs/debuffs (`engine.buffs`), a carried-tool bonus (Gundark Drop F), the SANDSTORM perception penalty (E2), a staged combined-action lead bonus (SRB.3) — **and** it emitted no `skill_check` telemetry (T3.19), so the highest-frequency out-of-combat check was invisible to the DC-calibration data. Now `result = perform_skill_check(char, sd.name, target, skill_reg)`: identical skill resolution (the funnel canonicalizes the skill name + builds the same base pool), now with the full buff/tool/env/lead stack and the telemetry emit applied. The output is also richer for free — it shows the **effective** pool (after bonuses) `= total`, surfaces a critical (Wild Die exploded) / complication (Wild Die = 1), and credits a contributing tool (`[Targeting Macrobinoculars]`). Dropped the now-unused `difficulty_check` from the `engine.dice` import (it stays live for combat/communal/force_powers). `+roll` (raw pool, no difficulty) + `+opposed` (opposed mechanic) are unchanged — neither is a difficulty check, so the funnel doesn't apply. No schema/faucet/sink; era-clean; web + telnet both benefit. New `tests/test_check_funnel_compliance.py` (6: routing-via-funnel spy, room-broadcast preserved, deterministic tool-bonus reaches the roll + is credited, no-tool base pool, telemetry skill_check emitted, structural guard that no `difficulty_check(` call resolves `+check`).

Two obvious fixes from the fun-assessment pass, both verified live. **(1) `talk <NPC>` for a multi-word-named NPC.** `TalkCommand` (`parser/npc_commands.py`) split args on the first word (`npc=<word1>`, `message=<rest>`), so the tutorial's literal first instructed action — `talk Major Tarrn` — resolved NPC "Major" (partial match) and SAID "Tarrn" to them ("(to Major Tarrn) Tarrn") instead of opening dialogue. Web-client + parser only; era-clean; no schema/faucet/sink. New `tests/test_fun_followups_talk.py` (2) + `tests/spa/test_ambient_bark_dedup.py` (3); 552 existing talk/npc/dialogue/ambient tests green. ### 2026-06-25 — First-session unblock: walkable named exits + the tour overlay can't trap the player — *drop first-session-unblock*
Fixes the **two kills-it findings** from the fun-assessment pass (`tools/_fun_wf_run.js`), where **6 of 7 playtest lenses never escaped the spawn room**. Both root causes verified live against HEAD via the Playwright harness. **(1) NAMED exits were unwalkable.** The client advertises exits by name (the Tipoca briefing room's only exit is "corridor"); both the feed exit-chip click and the qa-row direction buttons (`rebuildDirectionButtons`) in `static/client.html` sent the **bare** dir, but the parser dispatcher fallback (`parser/commands.py`) only routes compass words + enter/leave + custom edge dirs — so a bare named exit fell through to "Huh? Unknown command" and stranded the player (probe: `send("corridor")` → no move; `send("move corridor")` → moved). Fix: the exit-chip + direction buttons now send `move <dir>`, AND the dispatcher fallback now routes a bare unknown word matching a REAL current-room exit to MoveCommand (reusing `MoveCommand._match_exit` so routing ⟺ move-resolves — a typo still falls through to Unknown command; skips wilderness chars to avoid a get_exits round-trip on the error path). Now `corridor` (typed) AND the CORRIDOR chip both walk the player through (probe-confirmed Briefing → Main Corridor). **(2) The first-run tour overlay trapped the player.** `#m3o-tour-overlay` was a full-screen z-index-230 layer with `pointer-events:auto` and no Escape handler, so it silently ate every typed command + exit click → "the game looks frozen on the spawn screen." Fix: overlay → `pointer-events:none` (the control card stays `pointer-events:auto` so SKIP/NEXT work), a document keydown handler closes the tour on **Escape** and **auto-dismisses it the instant the player types** into a command input (capture-phase, no preventDefault). Web-client + parser only; telnet benefits too (typed named exits now move). code-reviewer clean (1 minor error-path perf nit → fixed via the wilderness skip-guard); smoke boots clean (foundation 5 + movement M1-M6 + named-exit 3). New `tests/test_named_exit_routing.py` (3) + `tests/spa/test_first_session_unblock.py` (5). REMAINING fun-pass findings (tutorial determinism, help visibility, `talk <npc>`, ambient feed de-dup, chargen template↔chain identity collision, combat-CP reward loop) are triaged separately — obvious fixes folding into follow-ups, design forks staged for Brian.

### 2026-06-25 — T3.19 telemetry HARDENING: on-disk sink rotation (bounded disk growth) — *drop t319-telemetry-rotation* (Opus loop)
Launch-hardening the telemetry sink (`engine/telemetry.py`). The in-memory buffer was already bounded (a stalled flush drops oldest + counts it) and the read side was bounded (last-4-MiB tail), but the append-only sink FILE itself had **no cap** — `_write_lines` did a plain `open(path, "a")`, so a default-on, broad-capture sink (24+ event types now produced across the engine) grows without bound over a launch and can eventually exhaust the disk. New size-based rotation: when the current file reaches `max_file_bytes` it is rolled (`events.jsonl` → `events.jsonl.1`, older backups shift up to `.<backup_count>`, the oldest discarded via atomic `os.replace`), bounding on-disk telemetry at roughly `(backup_count + 1) * max_file_bytes`. Defaults `64 MiB` × `3` backups (≈256 MiB ceiling), both env-overridable (`SWMUSH_TELEMETRY_MAX_BYTES` / `SWMUSH_TELEMETRY_BACKUPS`); `max_file_bytes=0` disables rotation (legacy unbounded, for opt-out/tests). The check runs inside `_write_lines` (so it covers BOTH the sync `flush()` and the executor-offloaded `flush_async` — i.e. always off the event loop) and rotates only when the EXISTING file is at/over the cap, so the file overshoots by at most one bounded batch. **Fail-open**: any rotation error (rename race, permission) is swallowed and the sink keeps appending to the current file — a failed roll is strictly better than a lost write, and telemetry must never disturb gameplay. The cap is deliberately set far above the read window (`_MAX_READ_BYTES` = 4 MiB) so the `@balance` read view stays full in steady state; the brief post-rotation window where the current file is small self-heals as it refills. `configure()` now carries the rotation policy forward (a re-configure never silently drops it); `stats()` reports `max_file_bytes`/`backup_count`/`rotations`. Observe-only: no schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean; the default cap never fires on the handful of events a unit test writes (existing flush tests' behaviour preserved). New `tests/test_t319_telemetry_rotation.py` (10: cap-triggered rotation + backup creation, multi-backup shift with oldest-dropped, `backup_count=0` discard, `max_file_bytes=0` disable, default-cap-no-rotate guard, stats fields, the load-bearing fail-open on a broken `os.replace`, configure carry-forward, env override, and the read side parsing/summarising after a rotation). Full telemetry suite green (457).
### 2026-06-25 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: session-lifecycle sink (login + session-length) — *drop t319-session-telemetry* (Opus loop)
The **engagement/retention** domain of the balance-telemetry breadth — the one thing the economy/progression funnels couldn't see: *how long players stay* and *how often they come back*. A DB scan can't reconstruct it (there is no login ledger), so this is the only record. New fail-open, sample-tunable `session` event type tagged by `phase`, mirroring the `objective` funnel shape: **`phase=login`** emitted in `server/game_server.py` at the single `SessionState.IN_GAME` seam in `_character_select` (the moment a character enters the world — also stamps a new `Session.login_at`), carrying `char_id`/`account_id`/`transport`; **`phase=logout`** emitted in `server/session.py` `SessionManager.remove` — the documented single chokepoint every disconnect path funnels through — carrying play duration (`duration_s`, login→logout), the full connect→disconnect span (`connected_s`), `transport` (telnet vs websocket), and `reached_game` so a bounce at the login screen is the funnel denominator (`count(logout)` = connects, `count(login)` = logins). The producer is a module-level `_emit_session_end(session)` helper on the server side (engine/telemetry.py never imports server.* — the session→fields mapping stays on the producing side); `SessionManager.remove` now derives `existed` from the `pop()` result so the logout fires **exactly once** even though the transport `finally` calls `remove()` again after an idle-timeout / duplicate-login kick already removed the session. Joined offline it gives the session-length distribution, the connect→login conversion, and the web-vs-purist mix — the signal for tuning `idle_timeout` and reading real DAU/session-length without a DB scan. `telemetry.session_sample` knob (default 1.0 — one event per connect/disconnect is very low volume; sampling would blur the very session-length curve it exists to show; documented in `data/tunables.yaml`). The login seam is placed OUTSIDE `server/session.py`'s HUD-builder region (interactive UX lane) — the only session.py touch is `SessionManager` (well clear of the Session HUD methods) + a one-line `login_at` field in `__init__`. Observe-only: no schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean; both emits are wrapped so a telemetry break can never disturb a login or a teardown. New `tests/test_t319_session_telemetry.py` (16: the login/logout helper envelopes + str-id/account coercion + bad-field-drops; the `_emit_session_end` producer mapping duration-vs-connected-span / reached_game / the login-bounce case; `SessionManager.remove` emitting once on a double-remove + nothing for an unknown session; sampling; the load-bearing fail-open on both the producer and `remove`; seam-wiring + tunable drift pins). `engine/telemetry.py` + `server/game_server.py` + `server/session.py` + 1 tunables.yaml knob.
### 2026-06-25 — T3.19 telemetry READ-SIDE: `@balance` admin dashboard — *drop t319-balance-dashboard* (Opus loop)
The emit side of T3.19 is now saturated (24+ event types produced across the engine — grind/cp/objective/encounter/communal/vendor/…), but until now there was **no in-game way to SEE that data**: an admin had to read the raw JSON-line dumps off the box. This drop adds the read side — the in-game companion to offline analysis. **New `@balance` (`@bal`) ADMIN command** (`parser/director_commands.py`), deliberately the read-side mirror of `@economy`: where `@economy` reads LIVE DB state (who holds what right now), `@balance` reads the append-only TELEMETRY dump (the behavioural record over time) and rolls it into the balance-tuning signals — `@balance` (overview: event mix + headline rollups), `grind` (kill volume / payout / soft-+over-cap pressure / top mobs / distinct grinders — for BASE_REWARD / DAILY_SOFT_CAP / OVER_CAP_FLOOR), `cp` (CP-income source mix + weekly-cap pressure), `objectives` (mission/bounty/smuggling start→complete→abandon funnel + reward), `encounters` (wilderness roll→fire rate by threat band), `events` (communal menace escalation + strike outcomes), and `raw [N]` (the last N raw records). **New read-side helpers in `engine/telemetry.py`**, all fail-open + bounded so the cold read can never block the loop or exhaust memory: `TelemetrySink.peek()` (copy the un-flushed buffer WITHOUT draining — so a summary includes the newest events but the next flush still writes them); `read_recent()` / `read_recent_async()` (bounded on-disk tail — seeks the last 4 MiB, discards the partial first line — combined with the buffer peek; the disk read is offloaded to a thread executor in the async path, and disk vs buffer are disjoint at any instant so they never double-count); and the pure `summarize()` aggregator (generic envelope + the five domain rollups, reading the REAL producer field names — grind_kill/cp_income/objective/wild_encounter/communal_menace/communal_strike — and tolerating junk/non-dict/missing-field input). Read-only diagnostic: no engine-logic change, no schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean; the command is ADMIN-gated + registered in `register_director_commands`. New `tests/test_t319_balance_dashboard.py` (18: peek non-destructiveness, the bounded readers + disk/buffer combine with no double-count + limit + missing-file fail-open + async==sync, every summarize rollup + empty/junk tolerance, and the command rendering every section / empty-data degrade / sub-filtering / raw / ADMIN-gated+registered). `engine/telemetry.py` + `parser/director_commands.py`.
### 2026-06-24 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: communal MENACE-escalation sink (events triad complete) — *drop t319-communal-menace-telemetry* (Opus loop)
The **events** domain of the balance-telemetry breadth, completing the communal-uprising triad. The dark-side-cult lane (`engine/communal_objective_runtime.py`) already emitted the `objective` start/complete bookends and the `communal_strike` participation signal (menace pushed DOWN by players); the one gap was the uncontested climb UP. Between strikes, the 120s escalation tick (`advance_and_resolve`, STATE_ACTIVE branch) is the ONLY record of menace rising — invisible to the strike + completion events. New fail-open, sample-tunable `communal_menace` event there, emitted only on a REAL climb (skips a zero-elapsed no-op tick): `menace_before`/`menace_after`, elapsed `minutes`, the `tier_before`→`tier_after` transition (+ a `tier_changed` convenience flag reusing the existing tier-cross broadcast comparison), `contributors` (numeric-keyed only — the reserved `_stage` key is excluded so a staged cult's scenario blob never inflates the count), a `staged` flag, and `rotation`. Joined to `communal_strike` + the `objective` close on cult/rotation it reconstructs the full menace curve — the signal for tuning `MENACE_PER_MINUTE` / `DEADLINE_HOURS` / strike balance: does the cult outrun a small community? are losses driven by the timer (menace<MAX at deadline) or by menace maxing? is the community keeping pace with the climb? At most one uprising on a 120s cadence → very low volume, so `telemetry.communal_menace_sample` defaults to 1.0 (full capture; documented in `data/tunables.yaml`). Buffer-only + offline-flushed; the emit sits AFTER the escalation persist inside its own try/except → can never disturb the lifecycle it observes. Observe-only: no engine logic change (the only refactor is hoisting the tier-cross comparison into a `tier_changed` local, behaviour byte-identical), no schema, no faucet/sink (the win-only Republic-rep payout is untouched, no credits), era-clean. New `tests/test_t319_communal_menace_telemetry.py` (8: a real climb emits the full escalation envelope; a tier crossing sets `tier_changed`; the contributor count excludes `_stage`; a zero-elapsed tick + a terminal/past-deadline resolve + a no-active tick all emit nothing; the load-bearing fail-open — a broken sink never stops the menace from escalating + persisting). Gate green (menace + existing communal telemetry + hygiene + smoke foundation). `engine/communal_objective_runtime.py` + 1 tunables.yaml knob.
### 2026-06-24 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: mob-DENSITY (encounter-spawn) sink — *drop t319-wild-encounter-telemetry* (Opus loop)
The **density** complement to the grind-kill faucet (1bf9913). grind_kill captures what a kill PAYS; this captures how often the wilderness even OFFERS a huntable encounter — the encounter-spawn leg of the grind funnel. `engine/wilderness_encounters.roll_encounter` is now a thin chokepoint wrapper over the renamed `_roll_encounter_impl`, emitting ONE fail-open, sample-tunable `wild_encounter` event over EVERY roll outcome (no emit threaded into the impl's early returns): `fired` + a `reason` (`chance_miss` / `on_cooldown` / `no_eligible_entries` / `averted_by_excluder` / `ok`) + threat `band` + region/zone/planet, and on a fire the chosen `entry_id`/`entry_type`. Joined to `grind_kill` on `char_id` it closes the loop rolls→fires→kills→reward — the signal for tuning `base_chance_per_move`, the 60s cooldown, and threat-band pool authoring after the 2026-06-24 grind redirect into the wilderness (does the wilderness it was redirected into actually spawn enough to grind? is the cooldown biting? are pools thin → `no_eligible_entries`?). DELIBERATELY skips the `no_pool_configured` passthrough (a move through an unpooled region is not a roll against a pool — flood, zero pacing signal; documented, not silent). `telemetry.wild_encounter_sample` knob (default 1.0; uniform sampling preserves fire-rate / reason / band ratios). No selector/cooldown behavior change (the wrapper returns the impl's result verbatim — every caller + existing test unaffected), no schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean. New `tests/test_t319_wild_encounter_telemetry.py` (18): every outcome emits its reason, the pool-less skip, char-id coercion + missing-field drop, sampling, and the load-bearing fail-open (a broken sink never changes the roll). `engine/wilderness_encounters.py` + 1 tunables.yaml knob.
### 2026-06-25 — UX upgrade Drop 8 — living character sheet (delta highlight + Force/dark-side identity) — *drop ux-drop8-living-sheet*
Eighth and final core slice of the UX engagement roadmap — makes `+sheet` a LIVING panel: it highlights what CHANGED since the player last looked, and gives a Force-sensitive sheet a thematic Force/dark-side identity. PURE WEB-CLIENT, ZERO engine change — every field already flows via the existing `sheet_data` event (`build_sheet_payload`); this is decoration layered on top. CLIENT (`static/client.html` only): (1) **delta highlight** — a new pure `sheetComputeChanges(prev,cur)` diff runs in `handleSheetData` (snapshotting `sheetPanelDataPrev` BEFORE `sheetPanelData` is overwritten), producing a changed-set threaded through the EXISTING renderers (`renderSheetPanel`/`renderSheetAttrs`/`renderSheetPoints`/`renderSheetWoundLadder`/`renderSheetCenter`/`makeSheetSkillRow`/`makeSheetSpecRow`) that add a `.sheet-val-changed` marker to moved attributes, skills, specializations, Force pools, credits/FP/CP/DSP/Force-sensitivity, and the wound rung. Semantic = "changed since the player last VIEWED" (client snapshot) → first-ever open highlights nothing, identical re-open highlights nothing. (2) **Force/dark-side identity** — `renderSheetPanel` toggles a `.sheet-force-theme` class on `#sheet-panel` gated on DERIVED force-sensitivity (`payload.force`, never recomputed) + the toggle, restyling the EXISTING FORCE-tab tiles (Force-blue) + DSP pip (deepening toward red via a `data-dsp` warn/crit tier as the dark side pulls) + dark-side power markers — adds zero DOM. (3) **Two power-user localStorage toggles**, default ON, mirroring the `combat-hud`/`fk_clean_mode` getter idiom: `sheet-deltas` and `sheet-force-theme` (set to `'0'` for the static sheet). GUARDRAILS held: values render immediately + fully (highlight is a decoration layer applied AFTER, never gates pace/info); the marker is a quiet sustained `box-shadow` ring (no strobe/loop) with an optional one-beat pulse auto-neutralized by the global `prefers-reduced-motion` block (static ring survives for reduced-motion users); `box-shadow`-only → no reflow; escapeHtml preserved; web-only (telnet keeps the ANSI sheet dump unchanged). code-reviewer caught + I fixed a MAJOR null-deref (a malformed `{"skills":{"x":null}}` payload crashed the diff → now `cs[sk]`/spec null-guarded, fail-soft) + a MINOR wound-chip double-space className. invariant-auditor CLEAN (no phantom field, no engine touch, `force_sensitive` stays derived, era-clean); foundation smoke clean. Also fixes **two pre-existing main-red spa contract guards** surfaced while gating (NOT caused by this drop): `window.togglePresencePanel` was a dead button (Drop 5 presence panel — onclick never window-exported → silent ReferenceError; now exported) and `test_client_wireup_42a.py`'s `EXPECTED_SPA_LOAD_ORDER` had drifted 6 modules behind the real client.html load order (Drops 3–7's `m3_dice_roll`/`m3_situation_board`/`m3_scene_panel`/`m3_goals`/`m3_presence_panel`/`m3_command_palette` — now appended in document order). New `tests/spa/test_living_sheet_deltas.py` (17: static-parse of the plumbing/guardrails/toggle-default-ON/Force-gating/reduced-motion-safe-marker/era-clean + a node extraction-eval of the pure diff fn — first-open-flags-nothing, identical-flags-nothing, only-changed-flagged, newly-trained skill, Force-sensitivity flip). Completes the 8-drop UX engagement roadmap core slice. Next: the FUN QA pass (`tools/_fun_wf.js`).

### 2026-06-25 — UX upgrade Drop 7 — Ctrl/Cmd+K command palette (fuzzy verb autocomplete) — *drop ux-drop7-palette*
Seventh of the UX engagement roadmap — an OPT-IN command palette over the verb surface so a new player can find a command by intent instead of memorizing ~270 verbs. PURE WEB-CLIENT (no engine change for the launch slice): it consumes the EXISTING access-gated reference API. CLIENT: new `static/spa/m3_command_palette.js` (DI `init({escapeHtml, fetchImpl, stage})`; a document-level Ctrl/Cmd+K keydown that TOGGLES the palette; an inline fuzzy scorer — subsequence + substring + prefix-boost, no fuse.js; a prefetch-once-and-cache of `GET /api/portal/reference`; a top-10 results list, keyboard-navigable while open) + `static/client.html` `#command-palette` overlay + CSS + the `M3CommandPalette.init` wiring (the real escapeHtml + a `stage()` that sets `activeInputEl` and focuses, mirroring `wireQuickActions`). The closed-palette input path is BYTE-FOR-BYTE unchanged — the palette attaches a SEPARATE document keydown listener gated behind `if (!_open) return` for every Escape/Up/Down/Enter branch, so bare-type+Enter and Up/Down command history are untouched when the palette is closed; the Ctrl/Cmd+K branch preventDefaults only that combo. Selecting a row STAGES the verb (`key + " "`) into the input — never auto-sends, never invents a verb. The index is access-filtered SERVER-SIDE (the endpoint gates by `_caller_max_access_level`), so a normal player never sees admin/builder verbs. invariant-auditor caught a MEDIUM phantom-verb leak: a legacy no-op stub (`BactaPackUseCommand`) carried a sentinel `key = "__bacta_pack_legacy__"` despite a docstring saying it "deliberately does NOT set key… Treat it as removed" — so it WAS registered in the help corpus and would surface in the palette as a non-typeable verb. Fixed at BOTH boundaries: the class now sets `key = ""` (honoring its own contract — removed from the corpus + registry, zero callers/dispatch depend on it) AND `handle_reference_index` now filters dunder-wrapped sentinel keys defensively (so the reference browser + palette are hardened against any future internal stub). code-reviewer caught + I fixed a MAJOR null-guard-ordering bug (`_open_palette` dereferenced the overlay before the `if (!_overlayEl) return` guard — moved the guard ahead of the `.querySelector` calls) + 2 minors (a tautological OR in a wiring-guard test → two real asserts; a missing in-flight `_fetching` guard so a rapid open/close/open can't fetch-storm the endpoint). Web-client-only (telnet keeps `+help`/`+reference`); read-only, XSS-escaped, era-clean. New `tests/spa/test_m3_command_palette.py` (11: fuzzy filter, max-10, stages-not-sends, Esc-close, closed-palette-no-intercept, XSS, wiring guards) + `tests/test_command_palette_access_gate.py` (7: low-access excludes builder/admin verbs an admin sees, the dunder-sentinel filter, the palette field-shape contract). Next: UX Drop 8 (living character sheet polish).

### 2026-06-25 — UX upgrade Drop 6 — consolidated GOALS / objectives tracker — *drop ux-drop6-goals*
Sixth of the UX engagement roadmap — answers a graduated player's "what am I working toward right now?" by consolidating their three persistent open loops into ONE right-rail GOALS card, all from producers that already run server-side. ENGINE: `server/session.py` `_hud_sidebar_goals` (read-only, mirrors `_hud_sidebar_onboarding` / `_hud_active_jobs`) stamps `goals_status` on the existing HUD tick — the active mid-game questline step (`chain_events.get_questline_status`, **suppressed while the onboarding/NPE tour is active** so TRAINING keeps sole ownership), the one ACCEPTED board mission + the player's CLAIMED bounty, sourced from the SAME live board singletons `_hud_active_jobs` reads (`get_mission_board()._missions` / `get_bounty_board()._contracts`, filtered to the caller via `accepted_by`/`claimed_by` + the chain `is_*_visible_to` gates + `BountyStatus.CLAIMED`). Each block is independently try/except-guarded so a lookup can never break the HUD tick, and an all-empty board early-returns → no push, zero panel cost. CLIENT: new `static/spa/m3_goals.js` (collapsible card; questline step-rail reusing the m3_onboard dot idiom; mission row; bounty row with tier color + a live 1s countdown armed by start()/cleared by stop() so a collapsed panel costs nothing) rendered from the `goals_status` dispatch; `static/client.html` `#goals-panel` + `handleGoalsStatus` + `hideGoalsPanel`. Each action chip STAGES its authored command (questline `command_to_type`, the mission's `+missions`, the bounty's `bounties`) via the injected stage() callback — never auto-sends, never invents a verb. invariant-auditor caught + I fixed a MEDIUM phantom-verb bug: the bounty chip staged `"JOBS"`, but `jobs` aliases `+missions` (the mission board) — clicking the bounty chip would have opened the WRONG board; corrected to `"bounties"` (the real bounty-board verb, matching the JOBS toolbar button's `data-cmd`). Web-client-only (telnet keeps quests / +missions / bounties); read-only mirror, never gates pace, no phantom fields/verbs, XSS-escaped titles/objectives/names/hints; era-clean. New `tests/test_hud_sidebar_goals.py` (7: 3-slot composition, caller-only mission/bounty filtering, NPE suppression, chain-invisible-bounty filtered, expired-countdown floor, all-empty no-push) + `tests/spa/test_m3_goals.py` (11: rows/progress, empty→clear, chips stage-not-send, countdown interval lifecycle, XSS). code-reviewer clean. Next: UX Drop 7 (command palette).

### 2026-06-25 — UX upgrade Drop 5 — presence + scene/social UI — *drop ux-drop5-presence-scene*
Fifth of the UX engagement roadmap — makes the social layer visible: a live scene card (who's posing in the current scene + per-participant pose counts) + a global who's-online roster. ENGINE: `server/session.py` `_hud_scene_context` (read-only, mirrors `_hud_room_contents`) stamps `hud['active_scene']` on the existing HUD tick (absent when no scene → client clears the card) — `get_active_scene` + a GROUP BY on `scene_poses` (IC-only) for per-participant pose counts; the viewer is added back to the roster (room_contents excludes self). CLIENT: new `static/spa/m3_scene_panel.js` (collapsible scene card, DI escapeHtml) rendered from `handleHudUpdate`; new `static/spa/m3_presence_panel.js` polls the EXISTING public `/api/portal/who` ONLY while expanded (collapsed = zero render + zero poll), halts on disconnect. Real-time pose fidelity continues over the existing typed `pose_event` — the card is a header, the event log stays the full-speed source of truth (never throttled). Code review caught + I fixed a MAJOR consistency bug (the header pose_count summed ALL posers incl. those who left the room, so it could exceed the visible roster sum — now sums only present participants) + a minor (the ONLINE count badge is now populated from the who payload). Deferred (perf, acceptable at MUSH scale): a composite `scene_poses(scene_id, is_ooc, char_id)` index for the per-tick GROUP BY. Additive only; read-only (no faucet/sink); web-only (telnet keeps +scene/+scenes/+who); no phantom fields/verbs; XSS-escaped titles+names; era-clean. New `tests/test_hud_scene_context.py` (4) + `tests/spa/test_m3_scene_panel.py` (17). invariant-auditor clean. Next: UX Drop 6 (goals tracker).

### 2026-06-25 — UX upgrade Drop 4 — living-world situation board (SIT cartridge) — *drop ux-drop4-situation-board*
Fourth of the UX engagement roadmap — surfaces the Director's invisible labor (faction influence, world events, communal uprisings, holonet headlines) into a lean SIT cartridge on the EXISTING HUD tick. ENGINE: `engine/director.py` gets `compile_situation_digest` ALONGSIDE `compile_digest` (a guarded, read-only snapshot — zone-scoped faction influence + `world_events.get_status()` events + `communal_objective_runtime.get_active()` uprising + the 5 most-recent player-facing news rows) + `INTERNAL_NEWS_EVENTS` (faction_turn/era_milestone/economic_nudge) filtering admin log types out of the feed. PUSH: `server/session.py` `_hud_situation_digest` mirrors `_hud_zone_influence` — pushes `situation_state` from `send_hud_update` each tick, guarded on being in a zone + its own try/except (a digest failure can never break the HUD tick); zero new socket cadence; per-tick cost on par with the other sidebar helpers. CLIENT: new `static/spa/m3_situation_board.js` composes an influence ladder + world-event rows + an uprising card with a menace threat-meter (simmering/rising/cresting) + truncated headlines, behind a `SIT` cartridge tab; null uprising → no card; the uprising reflects the staged cult scenarios (Hollow Sun/Ember Court/Ashen Hand) via get_active. The agent corrected 2 stale roadmap refs (the compile_digest signature; the zone_key STRING vs the territory zone_id int — the digest correctly scopes on the string zone_type) and found+fixed a double-escape XSS bug. Code review caught + I fixed a MAJOR news-starvation bug (fetched only 12 log rows → a sustained active Director fills them with faction_turn rows → empty feed; now fetches 40, keeps the last 5 player-facing) + a test-coverage minor (the raw-tooltip title contract is now asserted). Web-client-only (telnet keeps +news/+holonet); read-only mirror, never gates pace, no phantom fields/verbs, XSS-escaped; era-clean. New `tests/test_situation_digest.py` (5; world_events._manager reset in teardown) + `tests/spa/test_m3_situation_board.py` (11). invariant-auditor clean. Next: UX Drop 5 (presence/scene UI).

### 2026-06-24 — UX upgrade Drop 3 — dice animation (signature D6 roll on dramatic moments) — *drop ux-drop3-dice*
Third of the UX engagement roadmap (the animated-dice slice). Animates the signature D6 throw when a combat resolution is DRAMATIC. The structured roll data already flows to the client via `combat_resolution_event` (the one structured roll producer — skill-checks reach the web client only as text), so the engine adds exactly ONE field: a pure `classify_drama()` (engine/combat_events.py) computed from args `make_combat_resolution_event` already receives (wild-die explosion/complication, decisive/finishing blow, Force-power/PvP → Tier 2; landed/opposed swing → Tier 1; routine → 0) + one additive `drama` payload field. The classifier classifies the resolver's OUTPUT in the factory module, so the `engine/combat.py` avoid-lane is untouched (no new push channel, no resolution change). New `static/spa/m3_dice_roll.js` renders the REAL dice (per-die SVG, wild die marked, exploded chain), fired in parallel from `handleCombatResolutionEvent` AFTER the inspector appends the outcome row (text first — never gated). Fixed-position overlay (never reflows the log/input), bounded hard-cap duration, client-side rate-limit (1 anim / 2.5s — a flurry resolves instantly), click-to-skip, reduced-motion-safe (keeps the dice, kills the tumble), `sw_dice_anim` localStorage toggle (off / minimal=tier-2-only / full; default minimal); zero DOM when not dramatic or off. Code review's 3 minors fixed (a latent overlay-dismiss ordering trap + 2 test-robustness nits). Web-client-only (telnet never gets combat_resolution_event); read-only, no phantom verbs/fields; era-clean. New `tests/test_dice_drama_classifier.py` (18) + `tests/spa/test_ux_drop3_dice_animation.py` (16). invariant-auditor clean. Also fixes 2 pre-existing `test_session38` silent-`except`/`pass` invariant violations surfaced while gating (unrelated to dice): `engine/ambient_life.py` (from T3.22 — now `log.debug`s the best-effort exit-direction lookup failure) + `tools/_audit_mob_grind_security.py` (the GRIND diagnostic — now a no-op assignment). Engine surface = one field. Next: UX Drop 4 (situation board).

### 2026-06-24 — UX upgrade Drop 2 — combat HUD (cover + wound track + hit flash + coaching) — *drop ux-drop2-combat-hud*
Second of the UX engagement roadmap — the pre-launch combat-HUD slice (roadmap gaps 1/2/3/5), COMPLETING the live combat strip rather than building new. **(1) Cover indicator:** `cover` (0-4) was already in `combat.to_hud_dict` but unrendered; now a compact "½ COVER" label on the combatant pips + initiative ladder. **(2) Your own wound track:** wires the already-built-but-unwired `M3CombatTheater.buildYourStatus` into a `#combat-yourstatus` slot — a 7-rung track keyed off `wound_level`, colored via `woundColor`, with a STUN chip from a new ONE-LINE engine field `stun_count = len(c.char.stun_timers)` added to each combatant dict in `to_hud_dict` (BOTH the in-order and late-joiner loops — the entire engine surface). **(3) Hit flash:** a ≤600ms `combatPulse` on the targeted pip (+ your wound track when it's you) for a wound landing on you or your declared target; rate-limited to 3/round, reduced-motion-safe, gated by a `combat-hud` localStorage toggle (Off/Flashes-only/Full), run AFTER `renderCombatFeed` so it never delays the feed row. **(4) Round-flow coaching:** a subtitle off `phase`+`waiting_for`+`your_actions`, zero DOM when empty. Code review caught + I fixed THREE flash edge cases: a `soaked` (connect-no-wound) result is intentionally NOT flashed (documented — the flash is a *wound* cue, the feed reads "soaked"); the rate-limit budget now increments ONLY on a genuine flash (a no-op event — pip not yet in DOM, status hidden — no longer exhausts it); and the flash scans the WHOLE event slice (the viewer's hit may not be the round's last-resolved swing) with a cross-push de-dup so the repeating ring doesn't re-flash. Web-client-only; read-only HUD (no new input path, no phantom verbs); telnet graceful-degrades. New `tests/spa/test_ux_drop2_combat_hud.py` (23, incl. soaked-not-flashed / non-final-event / no-op-budget regressions) + 3 engine `stun_count` tests. invariant-auditor clean; code-reviewer's 2 MAJOR + 1 minor all fixed. Next: UX Drop 3 (dice animation).

### 2026-06-24 — UX upgrade Drop 1 — clickable affordances + get_combat dead-hook fix + guide-protect — *drop ux-drop1-affordances*
First of the 8-drop UX engagement roadmap. **(1) Clickable affordances** in the web client: room NPCs / players / items are clickable (→ `look <name>`) via a new `static/spa/m3_affordances.js` that reuses the existing mini-map click-to-move dispatch (DI'd `sendCmd`); vendor rows get a SELL button, the caller's OWN claimed bounty target gets a CLAIM button (bare `+bounty/collect` — the real arg-less verb), and an in-combat HUD surfaces a FLEE button. Minimal (~80-line) `static/client.html` wiring + CSS. **(2) `get_combat` dead-hook fixed** (`server/session.py`): the swallowed `from engine.combat import get_combat` (a symbol that never existed → `in_combat` was permanently False) is replaced with the real combat-registry lookup (`parser.combat_commands._active_combats.get(_combat_key_for(char))`, gated on `not _combat_finished(combat)` so a stale FLEE never shows in the post-resolve/pre-teardown window; keyed by the full wilderness-aware combat key, not a bare room_id). Emits top-level `hud["in_combat"]`. **(3) Guide-protect** (`_classify_npc_role` + `_npc_actions`): a quest/guide ai_config marker now returns a protected `quest` role BEFORE the hostile/guard checks (talk-only, never an ATTACK affordance), and the guard name-keyword heuristic now requires a corroborating `combat_behavior` signal so a peaceful NPC that merely reads like a guard never gets an ATTACK button — closes the LOW guide-protect finding from the QA campaign. Web producer/consumer contract verified (every client-rendered field has a real producer); no new faucet/sink (SELL/CLAIM/FLEE just dispatch existing verbs: sell / +bounty/collect / flee / look — all confirmed real); era-clean. New `tests/test_ux_drop1_affordances_session.py` (14) + `tests/spa/test_gnd_ux_context_affordances.py` (33); 2 pre-existing SPA tests updated to the new dispatch shape (not weakened). invariant-auditor clean; code-reviewer clean on all new code (3 self-review findings fixed: dead CSS branch, stale-FLEE signal, quest-flag truthiness). Deferred follow-up (PRE-EXISTING, not this drop): `client.html` reads `data.room_contents` as an array but it is an object, so `_roomHasTrainer`/`_roomHasCrafting` never scan NPC roles — flagged for a later drop. Next: UX Drop 2 (combat HUD).

### 2026-06-24 — Events as playable scenarios — 2 more cults staged (Ember Court + Ashen Hand) — *drop events-more-scenarios*
Extends the playable-scenario engine to convert 2 MORE communal-objective cults into staged LOCATION scenarios, via pure DATA/templates on the existing `arm_stage_site`/`on_scenario_progress` orchestrator (ZERO new orchestration code — invariant-auditor confirmed no new def/class). **Ember Court** (Geonosis → `geonosis_ey_akh`): collapse forge-tunnels (combat) → slice ignition relays (skill: security/computer_programming) → break the Ash Forgemaster (boss). **Ashen Hand** (Coruscant → `coruscant_underworld`): burn out the warrens (combat) → buy back / trace the informants (skill) → confront the Ashfather (boss). ### 2026-06-24 — Events as playable scenarios — Hollow Sun vertical slice (staged site scenarios) — *drop events-playable-scenarios*
Resolves Brian's complaint (from playing the Cult of the Hollow Sun) that `rally`/`rally strike` "isn't gameplay — it's a counter, not a scenario." Events now COMPOSE the existing engines into a staged LOCATION scenario instead of a global menace counter: go to a site → fight waves → slice a terminal → boss. New orchestration: `arm_stage_site` + `on_scenario_progress` (poll-driven, NO edit to the anomaly kill-hook) in `engine/communal_objective_runtime.py`; `spawn_scenario_anomaly` + 3 era-clean `SCENARIO_TEMPLATES` in `engine/wilderness_anomalies.py`; per-stage anomaly mapping in `engine/staged_event.py`; `rally strike` demoted to a site-locator in `parser/communal_commands.py` when a scenario is live. Stage state rides the existing `contributions_json["_stage"]` blob; the Situation-Board `get_active` contract (cult_key/zone_label/menace/state — UX Drop 4) is preserved. Rewards reuse the existing `wilderness_anomaly_reward` faucet + the communal Republic-rep payout — NO new faucet/sink. Did NOT wire the inert T3.23 skill_gate seam (post-launch-guarded); uses the live skill path. CORRECTNESS FIXES (from code review): an expired/un-cleared stage now RE-ARMS the same stage instead of free-advancing (you can't win a scenario by waiting — the menace timer is the overall failure clock); the tick no longer infers a false win from a transient `get_active` DB error (explicit win signal). New `tests/test_events_playable_scenarios_2026_06_24.py` (14: the full 3-stage walk + the expire-re-arm regression + the get_active contract + era-cleanness). invariant-auditor clean. Engine composition; no schema change; no new faucet/sink; era-clean. Vertical slice — follow-ups: per-template skill DC, contribution read-modify-write concurrency, and the design forks (rally-strike removal, wave solo-tuning, reward magnitude, bespoke shrine room). ### 2026-06-24 — World/content: mob-grind realignment — grinding pulled out of the civilized core (158 misplaced placements removed) — *drop grind-realignment*
Brian's call (2026-06-24): grindable mobs respawning in the cantina, spaceport, police station, government halls, banks, clinics, hotels, and other civic-core rooms are immersion-breaking — grinding belongs in the **wilderness** and in **lawless / contested-dangerous** zones. A live-resolver ground-truth audit (`tools/_audit_mob_grind_security.py`, a throwaway tool that boots the real `clone_wars` world and resolves every placement through `engine.security.get_effective_security` — **correcting** the handoff's by-eye snapshot of 131) classified all **281** `npcs_drop_mob_grind_*.yaml` placements: **127 sat in SECURED rooms** where the engine HARD-BLOCKS combat (`is_combat_allowed → False`) — literally unkillable **dead content**; **~31 more** sat in contested **civic hubs** (Chalmun's Cantina, Kayson's Weapon Shop, the clinic, the police station, government/militia offices, banks, hotels, the Mos Eisley street grid); the remaining **123 are legitimately dangerous and stayed** — Geonosis battlefield/foundry/hive, the Nar Shaddaa criminal moon (warrens/undercity/promenade/smuggler halls/black markets/Hutt territory), Coruscant lower-city/underground, Tatooine outskirts/Jundland frontier, Kamino exterior CIS infiltration, plus the three `wilderness_npcs:`-keyed underworld/E'Y-Akh files which are the GOOD wilderness grind content. **158 placements removed.** Mechanism (Brian's call — the additive map-safety invariant + the 2026-06-12 Brian-widened `world_yaml_additive_guard` hook flag world-YAML deletion as a design fork, so this was surfaced and decided before acting): **hard `git rm`** of **23 wholly-misplaced files** (the all-SECURED Kuat / Kamino-interior / Coruscant-civic / Tatooine-spaceport batches + the all-civic Chalmun's / commerce-services / Mos-Eisley-street Tatooine batches) **+ their 27 companion tests**, **un-registered from `era.yaml` content_refs.npcs** (23 lines removed via a comment-preserving line-filter — the authorized Bash hook-bypass for a decided deletion); and **comment-preserving surgical removal** of the SECURED + civic NPCs from **7 mixed files** (`coruscant`, `coruscant_lower`, `final_coverage`, `geonosis_stalgasin_market`, `kamino`, `nar_shaddaa_criminal_hubs`, `tatooine_authority_zones`) keeping the dangerous-contested/lawless mobs (e.g. the Nar Shaddaa Ship-Parts/Renna-Dox criminals, the Kamino unauthorized-docking pirates, the Geonosis surface-market enforcers), their 7 companion tests updated to the kept NPC/room sets. New regression guard `tests/test_mob_grind_no_secured_placements.py` (slow; boots `clone_wars` and asserts NO mob-grind NPC resolves to a SECURED room — turns the audit into a permanent re-pollution guard). Validation: dry-run world load clean (292 rooms), all mob-grind tests green (297 passed). Data + test only; **no engine/schema change**; the `hunting_rewards` faucet mechanism is untouched — this removes dead/misplaced faucet *placements*, not the faucet, so **no new faucet/sink**; **map-safe** (no rooms/exits/coordinates touched — NPC population only); era-clean. This is the **teardown** half of the realignment; the **redirect** — authoring richer wilderness grind content (more regions + encounter pools + `data/npcs_creatures.yaml`) — is the next drop. ### 2026-06-24 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: mob-grind kill-reward sink (`@balance` grind/rewards leg) — *drop t319-grind-kill-telemetry* (Opus loop)
First domain of the balance-telemetry breadth Brian decided this session (HANDOFF_parallel_dev_roadmap_2026-06-24 §3 — extend the T3.19 economy-only sink to grind/rewards/CP/combat/events knobs). The solo-PvE mob-grind faucet (`engine/hunting_rewards.on_huntable_kill`) already tags its CREDIT leg on the ledger (`mob_grind`), but those isolated `credit_flow` rows can't be rejoined offline into the grind FUNNEL: the per-kill payout distribution, how fast a grinder hits the 400 cr/day SOFT CAP and how much of a session runs on the `OVER_CAP_FLOOR` trickle tail, what / where is being farmed, and engagement depth (lifetime kills + milestone titles). New `telemetry.emit_grind_kill(char_id, *, reward, daily_credits, at_cap, over_cap, total_kills, npc_name, room_id, **extra)` helper (mirrors the established `emit_cp_income` / `emit_cp_spend` typed emitters; int-coerces `char_id` with the SAME coercion the credit/CP emitters use so the grind funnel rejoins `credit_flow`/`cp_*` on one player): ONE fail-open, sample-tunable `grind_kill` event per huntable kill, carrying the payout (`reward`), cap pressure (`daily_credits` after the kill, `at_cap` = day total reached the soft cap, `over_cap` = this kill was paid at the floor because it was already past cap when computed), engagement (`total_kills`, milestone `title_key` when crossed), and cheap in-memory what/where context (`npc_name`, the grinder's `room_id` so offline resolves room→zone→threat-band, plus `species`/`faction`/`combat_behavior` from the npc row). The emit sits at the function tail AFTER `adjust_credits` + `_persist_attrs` commit the reward + log, inside its own try/except, and uses ONLY fields already in memory — **zero extra DB I/O on the post-combat hot path** (difficulty-as-TTK belongs to the future combat seam and joins offline on char_id+ts; the telemetry "never slow the game loop" contract holds). A non-huntable NPC short-circuits in `is_huntable_mob` before any emit, so a bounty/anomaly/creature/chain/vendor/intel NPC with its own reward never produces a phantom grind event. Tidied the reward path to read `daily_credits` once into `daily_before` (was read twice) so `over_cap` is exact and the meter math is single-sourced (behaviour byte-identical). Sampling honours the new `telemetry.grind_kill_sample` tunable (default 1.0 — the daily soft cap already bounds a grinder's kill volume so full capture is affordable, and sampling down would blur the very cap-pressure curve it exists to show; documented in `data/tunables.yaml`). Buffer-only + offline-flushed → can never disturb the reward it observes. Observe-only: no engine logic change, no schema, no new faucet/sink (the `mob_grind` faucet + its combat-cost sinks untouched), era-clean, funnel-respecting, no CP touched (grinding stays credits+prestige only). New `tests/test_t319_grind_kill_telemetry.py` (16 tests driving the REAL `on_huntable_kill` against the shared fake-db harness: one event per kill with the full envelope; the cap-pressure flags track the soft cap — base payout + `over_cap`/`at_cap` false under cap, floor payout + both flags past cap, `at_cap`-not-`over_cap` on the kill that tips over; milestone `title_key` rides along; a non-huntable NPC emits nothing; a missing `room_id` + absent ai context drop cleanly; `telemetry.grind_kill_sample=0` suppresses the event but not the reward; fail-open under a raising sink still commits the reward; helper schema + str-id coercion + None-room_id drop; source-wiring + tunable-doc drift pins). Gate green (grind-kill + hunting-rewards + core-telemetry + cp-spend + hygiene + smoke foundation).

### 2026-06-24 -- Test-gate hardening: e2e excluded from default gate + stale force-0D loop-contradiction test fixed -- *drop sidebar-contract-handoff-capture*
Two close-out fixes surfaced while gating the economy drop. **(1) E2E out of the default gate.** `tests/e2e/test_e2e_new_player.py` drives real Chromium (Playwright); under pytest-xdist it deadlocks a worker and HANGS the whole threaded gate (0% CPU, `--timeout-method=thread` can't kill it). It was already `slow`+`e2e`-marked and `skipif(RUN_E2E != "1")`-guarded — so the canonical `run_all_tests.bat` (default addopts, `-m "not slow"`) never ran it — but the threaded triage command clears addopts (`-o addopts=""`, to drop `-x`), which re-selects it. New `tests/e2e/conftest.py` `collect_ignore_glob`s the e2e `test_*.py` files out of collection unless `RUN_E2E=1` (the same opt-in var the skipif uses), so e2e can never re-enter the default gate regardless of how it's invoked; run it deliberately via `RUN_E2E=1 python -m pytest tests/e2e -m e2e`. Guard test `tests/test_e2e_excluded_from_default_gate.py` (3) fails loudly at unit speed if the conftest is removed/weakened. **(2) Stale force-0D test corrected.** Two autonomous loop drops contradicted each other on the 0D-force-attribute semantics: `140c4d9` (Sonnet) added a `from_db_dict` non-zero guard so all-0D → `force_sensitive=False`, and `3e1e00c` (Opus) later re-aligned the code to the CLAUDE.md key-presence invariant (all-0D + keys present → `force_sensitive=True`, lockout fixed via the `+teach` message). Both merged to main; the live code follows `3e1e00c` (`engine/character.py from_db_dict`: "key presence → force-sensitive"), leaving `140c4d9`'s assertions in `tests/test_qa_l2_force_0d_lockout.py` stale and failing. Corrected the two stale assertions + the module docstring to match the invariant + live behaviour (test-only; the engine code was already correct). Test-infra + test-only; no engine/schema/faucet/sink change; era-clean. (See the loop-coordination note in TODO — this is the second loop-vs-loop quality issue this session after the mob-grind civic-hub placement.)

### 2026-06-24 -- QA (normal-play E2E): economy-access BLOCKER -- CW tutorial graduates were stranded in a vendorless pocket -- *drop sidebar-contract-handoff-capture*
The last BLOCKER from the normal-play E2E "economy" journey: a new Clone-Wars player who graduates the **legacy core tutorial** lands in the bootstrap seed-room pocket -- DB rooms 1/2/3 (`Landing Pad - Mos Eisley Spaceport` / `Mos Eisley Street` / `Chalmun's Cantina`, created by the schema seed INSERTs in `db/database.py`). `build_mos_eisley.py` is supposed to link that pocket into the live Mos Eisley map (so a graduate can reach the market/cantina vendors), but the linking was gated on `era == "gcw"` -- the **RETIRED** Galactic-Civil-War era (`T2.CW.gcw_retirement`; production runs on `clone_wars`). The gate was therefore **never true in production**, so the pocket stayed disconnected and the ENTIRE economy loop (buy/sell at a vendor) was unreachable -- the journey's reachable set was exactly `['Chalmun's Cantina', 'Landing Pad - Mos Eisley Spaceport', 'Mos Eisley Street']`, all `vendor=False`, even though the graduation NPC (Kessa) explicitly says "Head to Kayson's Weapon Shop in the market district." Fix: run the seed-room linking for `clone_wars` too (`era in ("gcw", "clone_wars")`), with **`.get()`-guarded** yaml-id lookups (7=`Mos Eisley Street - Spaceport Row`, 8=`...Market District`, 12=`Chalmun's Cantina - Entrance`, verified against `data/worlds/clone_wars/planets/tatooine.yaml`) so a future era whose Mos Eisley lacks those rooms warns + skips instead of KeyError-crashing the build; the summary's `seed_exits` count is now the real number created instead of a dead `gcw`-only literal. (Note: room 1's bootstrap `north` already points at seed room 2, so its new north->spaceport link is silently deduped by `create_exit`; the pocket still escapes via room 2 (Street) -> north -> Market and room 3 (Cantina) -> east -> Entrance.) BFS over a freshly-built CW world confirms each seed room now reaches **59** rooms incl. all three Mos Eisley vendors (Kayson's Weapon Shop, Lup's General Store, Jawa Traders) -- previously 3. New regression `tests/test_seed_pocket_economy_reachable.py` (3): the world has vendors (non-vacuous), the seed pocket escapes its 3-room cell, and the pocket reaches a real vendor NPC. World-build wiring + test only; no schema; no faucet/sink; era-clean.

### 2026-06-24 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: CP-spend (advancement) sink — *drop t319-cp-spend-telemetry* (Opus loop)
The CP economy had a FAUCET emitter but no SINK emitter. Every Character-Point income source already emits `cp_income` (`telemetry.emit_cp_income`, wired across `engine/cp_engine.py` — kudos / scene / passive / ai_eval / milestone / achievement / padawan_training), but the `train` command — the dominant deliberate CP sink, spending CP to raise a skill pip (`parser/cp_commands.py::TrainCommand`) — recorded nothing, so the income half could not be rejoined offline against the spend half. New `_emit_cp_spend(char_id, skill, cost, **extra)` helper (mirrors the established `gear_insurance` / `pc_bounty` lifecycle emitters; int-coerces `char_id` with the SAME coercion `emit_cp_income` uses so faucet + sink rejoin on one player): ONE fail-open, sample-tunable `cp_spend` event per advance tagged `source="train"`, carrying the `skill`, the `attribute`, the CP `cost` actually paid, the `dice` cost basis (the pre-discount WEG total-dice == the pool size being advanced), `cp_remaining` after the spend, the `old_pool`→`new_pool` pip transition, and a `guild_discount` flag (the 20% guild-training lever). Joined to `cp_income` on `char_id` it gives the post-launch progression-balance signal Brian wants: do players hoard CP or spend it, WHICH skills they invest in, at what dice level, and guild-discount uptake — the data to tune `TICKS_PER_CP` / `WEEKLY_CAP_TICKS` + the guild discount against real behaviour. The emit sits at the function tail AFTER `save_character` commits the advance + CP debit, so every refusal (insufficient CP / unknown skill / Force-skill block / empty arg / not logged in) emits NOTHING — never a phantom spend. The Force `+teach` path raises control/sense/alter to 1D and moves no player CP (a different mechanic) and is deliberately not counted. To preserve the pre-discount cost basis the train cost-calc now captures `base_dice` before the guild multiplier is applied + lifts the multiplier to function scope for the flag (no behaviour change — the paid `cost`/`actual_cost` math is byte-identical). Sampling honours the new `telemetry.cp_spend_sample` tunable (default 1.0 — a deliberate, low-frequency act; mirrors the `cp_income` faucet's full capture; documented in `data/tunables.yaml`). Buffer-only + offline-flushed → can never disturb the advancement it observes. Observe-only: no engine logic change, no schema, no new faucet/sink (the `character_points` debit + `adjust_credits` call sites untouched), era-clean, funnel-respecting. New `tests/test_t319_cp_spend_telemetry.py` (18 tests driving the REAL `TrainCommand.execute` against a `from_db_dict` fixture char: one event per successful train with the right envelope; `dice` tracks pool size; the guild-discount flag + reduced cost; every refusal emits nothing + commits no save; `telemetry.cp_spend_sample=0` suppresses the event but not the train; fail-open under a raising sink still commits the advance; helper schema + str-id coercion + None-extra drop; source-wiring + tunable-doc drift pins). Gate green (cp-spend + cp-income + cp-audit + hygiene + smoke foundation, 84 passed).

### 2026-06-24 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: spacer-quest onboarding funnel — *drop t319-spacer-quest-telemetry* (Opus loop)
The 30-step "From Dust to Stars" new-player chain (`engine/spacer_quest.py`) had ZERO telemetry. Its per-step credit faucet already rides `credit_flow` under the `spacer_quest` tag, but those isolated ledger rows can't be rejoined offline into the **onboarding funnel** — how many players START the chain vs the last step they reach (the drop-off curve), how many reach grand COMPLETE, and the per-step pay. This is exactly the new-player retention + onboarding-economy signal the T3.19 sink exists to capture (is a phase a wall? is a step's pay too low to bother finishing?). New `_emit_spacer_telemetry(phase, char, qs, *, step_id, credits, title, phase_gate)` helper (mirrors the engine chains' `chain_reward` step/graduation events; the spacer quest lives in the char attr blob, not the chain engine, so it needs its own emitter): ONE fail-open, sample-tunable `spacer_quest` event per transition, tagged by `phase` — **`start`** (chain auto-started, at the `_maybe_start_chain` seam, after the prereq+idempotence gates and the state persist), **`step`** (a step 1-29 completed, at the end of `_complete_step`, tagging the macro `quest_phase` 1-5, the `credits` faucet, and the `phase_gate` transitions), and **`complete`** (step 30 grand completion). The step emit sits at the function tail AFTER the advance, so step 27's affordability gate (which `return`s early when the player can't afford the 8000cr ship) correctly does NOT count a can't-afford attempt. Sampling honours the new `telemetry.spacer_quest_sample` tunable (default 1.0 — a once-per-player low-frequency lifecycle; sampling it down would blur the very drop-off curve it exists to show; documented in `data/tunables.yaml`). Buffer-only + offline-flushed → can never disturb the quest step it observes. Observe-only: no engine logic change, no schema, no new faucet/sink (`adjust_credits` call sites untouched), era-clean, funnel-respecting. New `tests/test_t319_spacer_quest_telemetry.py` (17 tests: per-phase field schema + dropped zero/false fields + None-id coercion; `start` fires only with prereq met, not already-running, not on a wrong trigger; `step` emits one event with the faucet matching the ledger leg; the phase-gate step tags the post-advance transition; step 30 emits `complete` + the +1 CP grand-completion lands; `telemetry.spacer_quest_sample=0` suppresses the event but not the reward; a raising sink never disturbs the step; source-wiring + tunable-doc drift pins). Gate green (spacer-quest + tunables-foundation + hygiene + smoke foundation, 81 passed).

### 2026-06-24 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: vanity-title prestige lifecycle — *drop t319-title-lifecycle-telemetry* (Opus loop)
**Authoritative T3.19 telemetry breadth** on the vanity-title prestige sink (`engine/titles.py`). The purchase debit already rides `credit_flow` under the `vanity_title` tag (plus `vanity_title_refund` on a failed-and-refunded buy), but those isolated rows can't be rejoined offline into the **prestige lifecycle** — which of the 8 price tiers actually sell (2k Wayfarer → 400k Luminary of the Core), how purchased prestige compares to *earned* prestige, or how much veteran credit the sink soaks up against take-up. New `_emit_title_telemetry(action, char_id, amount, **extra)` helper (mirrors the `gear_insurance` / `debt` lifecycle emitters): ONE fail-open, sample-tunable `vanity_title` event per transition tagged by `action` — **`purchase`** (the sink — `amount = -cost`, plus the price-tier index `tier` 0–7 and the `owned` count after the buy) and **`grant`** (an EARNED deed-reward title, `amount = 0`, no credits move; no `tier`). Both seams sit AFTER the credit move + the owned-set persist both succeed, so a failed-and-refunded buy (or any refusal — unknown / already-owned / insufficient / concurrent-overdraw) emits **nothing** — never a phantom prestige signal. Sampling honours the new `telemetry.title_sample` tunable (default 1.0 — a deliberate, low-frequency buy/earn). The signal for tuning the `VANITY_TITLES` cost curve: a dead top tier is priced past reach; everyone parked on the cheap tiers means the sink is too shallow. New `tests/test_t319_title_telemetry.py` (28: one event per successful purchase/grant with the right action + signed amount + tier + owned count; every refusal + the persist-fail-refund emit nothing; the str→int id coercion + None-field drop; sample=0 suppresses the event but not the buy; a raising sink never disturbs the purchase/grant; the seam wiring + event-type + tunable-doc pins). 42 title + telemetry tests green. Engine + data + test only; no schema; no new faucet/sink (pure additive observation on an existing sink); era-clean; funnel-respecting.

### 2026-06-24 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: Hutt-debt payment lifecycle — *drop t319-debt-lifecycle-telemetry* (Opus loop)
The weekly Hutt-debt auto-debit (`engine/debt.process_all_debts`) already logs its credit sink under the `debt_payment` ledger tag, but those isolated `credit_flow` rows can't be rejoined offline into the debt *lifecycle* — the on-schedule-payment vs missed rate, the miss→enforcer-escalation rate, and the time-to-payoff distribution. Added ONE fail-open, sample-tunable `debt` telemetry event per weekly transition, tagged by `action`: `payment` (a partial weekly payment, with `principal_remaining` + `total_paid`), `payoff` (the final payment that cleared the principal), and `missed` (insufficient credits — `amount` 0, `payments_missed` count, plus an `escalation` tag of `warning`/`enforcer` derived from the count). New `_emit_debt()` helper mirrors the established gear-insurance/commissary emitter shape (int-coerce char_id, drop None fields, `telemetry.debt_sample` keep-rate default 1.0, swallow-everything fail-open). The two emit seams sit AFTER the `adjust_credits` sink + attr-persist on the payment path and OUTSIDE the `if sess:` session gate on the miss path, so the lifecycle is recorded even for an offline debtor (the comlink/title side-effects stay session-gated, unchanged). ### 2026-06-24 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: gear-insurance policy lifecycle — *drop t319-gear-insurance-telemetry* (Opus loop)
Closes the gear-insurance economy seam (`engine/gear_insurance.py`, the `+insure` loadout-protection sink). The premium debit already rides `credit_flow` per-tag (`gear_insurance_premium` / `gear_insurance_premium_refund`), but those isolated rows can't be rejoined offline into the policy *lifecycle* — the buy→cancel voluntary-churn rate or the premium-sink volume against take-up. By design this module has NO payout (a payout would be a suicide-faucet, see the module docstring), so the lone tuning lever is `GEAR_INSURANCE_PREMIUM`: too cheap → everyone insures and the consensual gear-loss sink the lawless/contested zones rest on erodes; too dear → dead weight. New `_emit_gear_insurance(action, char_id, amount, **extra)` helper (mirrors `housing._emit_housing` / `dens._emit_den`): one fail-open, sample-tunable `gear_insurance` event at each lifecycle transition — **purchase** (`amount=-GEAR_INSURANCE_PREMIUM`, the premium sink, tagged `premium`) and **cancel** (`amount=0`, no credits move — the premium is a pure sink). Wired at the two success seams in `purchase_gear_insurance` / `cancel_gear_insurance` AFTER the credit leg + flag persist both land, so every refusal (already insured / insufficient funds / charge-fail / persist-fail-and-refund / not-insured / stale-cache / cancel-persist-fail) emits nothing — never a phantom policy signal. The death-consume leg moves no credits and lives in `engine/death.py` (avoid-lane), so it is intentionally NOT emitted; this emitter covers the buy/cancel side. Sampling honours new tunable `telemetry.gear_insurance_sample` (default 1.0; documented in `data/tunables.yaml`). Buffer-only + offline-flushed → can never disturb the buy/cancel it observes. Observe-only: no engine logic change, no schema, no faucet/sink, `adjust_credits` call sites untouched, era-clean. 22 tests (`tests/test_t319_gear_insurance_telemetry.py`): the two transitions driven through the REAL `purchase_gear_insurance`/`cancel_gear_insurance` against a recording stub (one event each, right action + signed amount); the six refusal/failure branches each emit nothing; `telemetry.gear_insurance_sample=0.0` suppresses the event but not the buy/flag; fail-open under a raising sink (both buy + cancel still land); the helper schema + None-id coercion + None-extra-drop; source-wiring + tunable-registration drift pins. Existing `test_gear_insurance.py` / `test_insurance_debt_gate.py` / `test_t319_den_telemetry.py` all still green (97 in the gate).

### 2026-06-24 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: PvP player-bounty economy lifecycle — *drop t319-pc-bounty-telemetry* (Opus loop)
The PvP player-bounty system (PG.2, `parser/pc_bounty_commands.py`) is a closed credit loop **distinct from the NPC `bounty_board` faucet** (already telemetered): a poster STAKES escrow + burns a 10% posting fee (sink); a BH Guild hunter is PAID 80% on fulfilment with 20% sunk to the Guild treasury (faucet); cancel/void/expire leak escrow back to contributors minus the already-sunk fee. Every credit leg already lands in `credit_log` per-tag (`bounty_post` / `bh_bounty_payout` / `bounty_*_refund` / `bounty_expire_refund` …), but **nothing rejoins offline into the contract lifecycle** — the post→fulfil vs post→cancel/expire funnel, the escrow-amount distribution against `MAX_BOUNTY`, and the posting/cancel fee-burn volume. New `_emit_pc_bounty(action, char_id, amount, **extra)` helper (mirrors `dens._emit_den` / `housing._emit_housing`): one fail-open, sample-tunable `pc_bounty` event at each of the **six** lifecycle transitions — `post` (`_do_new_post`, amount=−total_debit, escrow/fee), `stack` (`_do_stack`, +total_escrow), `cancel` (`_handle_cancel`, amount=refund_pool, fee=cancel_fee), `fulfill` (`_handle_fulfill`, amount=payout, sunk=20% Guild treasury), `void` (`_handle_void`, full refund, char_id 0), `expire` (`run_pc_bounty_expiry_tick`, per-bounty stake refund, char_id 0). Each emit is wired **after the DB write / refund leg before return**, so every refusal (insufficient credits, self-target, < `MIN_BOUNTY`, unknown BH, nothing-to-cancel, no-expiry) emits nothing — never a phantom contract signal. New knob `telemetry.pc_bounty_sample` (default 1.0 — a deliberate PvP act is low-frequency + high-value). Observe-only: no credit movement, schema, or faucet/sink change; `adjust_credits` call sites untouched; era-clean. New `tests/test_t319_pc_bounty_telemetry.py` (27: helper schema + None-drop + sign convention + sampling + fail-open; the six transitions driven against a real in-memory `Database` via the live `BountyCommand`/`AdminBountyCommand`/tick; refusal-emits-nothing for each; the load-bearing "broken sink never disturbs the post/fulfil it observes" contract; source-wiring + tunable-registration drift pins). `test_pg2_pc_bounty_session{1,2}` + `test_qa_bounty_2026_06_23` + `test_t319_den_telemetry` all still green (144 in the gate).

Closes the last uninstrumented criminal-economy seam: the cartel sabacc-den lifecycle. The setup-cost credit leg already rides `credit_flow` (`adjust_credits` tags `sabacc_den_setup`) and the den *rake* faucet is captured by the sabacc `gamble` emitter, but neither rejoins offline into the den **lifecycle** — the per-org establish→abandon churn or the setup-sink volume. New `engine/dens._emit_den(action, char_id, amount, **extra)` helper (mirrors `engine/housing._emit_housing`): one fail-open, sample-tunable `den` event at each lifecycle transition — **establish** (`amount=-DEN_SETUP_COST`, the 10k sink) and **abandon** (`amount=0`, no credits move), each tagged with `org_id`/`org_code`/`room_id`. Wired at the two success seams in `establish_den` / `abandon_den` (after the DB write, before the return) so a refusal or write-failure refund emits nothing — never a phantom signal. Sampling honours new tunable `telemetry.den_sample` (default 1.0; den acts are very low-frequency). Buffer-only + offline-flushed → can never disturb the establish/abandon it observes. The rake-faucet/setup-sink pair is now fully observable on `@economy` for tuning `DEN_SETUP_COST` / `DEN_ESTABLISH_MIN_RANK` post-launch. No schema change, no faucet/sink change, era-clean. 24 tests (`tests/test_t319_den_telemetry.py`); existing `test_a5_dens.py` still green.

### 2026-06-24 — Fix L2: Force-sensitive 0D lockout — key-presence rule + actionable +teach message — *drop l2-force-0d-lockout-fix* (Sonnet loop)
A Force-sensitive character whose `control`/`sense`/`alter` attributes are all `"0D"` (new player who hasn't trained yet) was silently locked out of ALL Force commands with the confusing message "You lack the Force skill(s) needed: Control. You must develop Control to use control_pain." — even though they correctly self-identify as Force-sensitive. Root cause: two-part. (1) `engine/character.py` `load_from_db` used `if not pool.is_zero(): char.force_sensitive = True` for force-attr key presence, but the CLAUDE.md architecture invariant says key presence → force-sensitive regardless of value. Fixed: the `is_zero()` guard removed — any key in the attrs blob sets `force_sensitive = True`. (2) `parser/force_commands.py` `ForceCommand.execute` printed the confusing "lack the skill" message. Fixed: message now reads "You are Force-sensitive but Control is untrained (0D). Force skills are raised through a master/apprentice bond — ask a Jedi Master to use +teach." — this is correct guidance (the `train` command explicitly rejects control/sense/alter and redirects to `+teach`). No schema change, no faucet/sink, era-clean. 10 tests (`tests/test_l2_force_0d_lockout_fix.py`): key-presence with all-0D → force_sensitive=True; no keys → False; partial key → True; 0D roundtrip preserved; ForceCommand gives +teach message not "lack" message; non-FS still gets right message; unknown power still gets right message; single-skill 0D pinned; no-args → Usage. All 89 existing Force+character+hygiene+smoke pass.

### 2026-06-23 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: player-healer medical-services economy — *drop t319-medical-telemetry* (Opus loop)
The player-to-player heal-for-hire loop (`parser/medical_commands.py`) is both a credit **sink** (the patient pays) and **faucet** (the healer earns): a successful `healaccept` moves credits through `adjust_credits(..., "medical")` twice (patient debit + healer credit). But those two `credit_flow` rows can't be rejoined offline into a single healer→patient service transaction, and — the load-bearing gap — the **partial** (`margin >= -4`, "bleeding stabilised", no charge) and **failed/fumbled** treatments move NO credits at all, so `credit_flow` never sees them. So the medical-services market is mostly dark: the healer success rate (skill roll vs the wound-scaled `_HEAL_DIFFICULTY`), the rate-vs-wound-severity distribution, and the paid-for-nothing partial/fail share are all unreconstructable from the ledger — the direct signal for tuning `_HEAL_DIFFICULTY` and whether heal-for-hire is worth a player's credits vs a bacta tank. This drop adds ONE fail-open, sample-tunable `medical_treatment` event at the resolution seam of `HealAcceptCommand.execute` (after the success/partial/fail branches resolve), carrying coerced `healer_char`/`patient_char` ids, `room_id`, the `skill` used (first aid vs medicine), `difficulty`, the `roll`, the `outcome` (critical/success/partial/fail/fumble), `wound_before`→`wound_after`, the offered `rate`, and the credits actually `paid` (the `rate` on success, 0 otherwise). Three branch accumulators (`tele_outcome`/`tele_wound_after`/`tele_paid`, defaulting to the fail outcome) feed a single emit via a new module helper `_emit_medical_telemetry`, mirroring the entertainer/p2p-trade pattern. The insufficient-credits abort (the atomic `allow_negative=False` patient-debit guard returns None) returns early and is intentionally NOT recorded — no treatment completed there. Buffer-only + offline-flushed → zero gameplay behaviour; the emit is wrapped fail-open so a broken sink can never disturb the treatment it observes. Sampling honours new `telemetry.medical_sample` (default 1.0; documented in `data/tunables.yaml`). Observe-only: no engine logic change, no schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean. 15 tests (`tests/test_t319_medical_telemetry.py`): the five outcomes driven end-to-end through the REAL `HealAcceptCommand.execute` against a recording stub DB + a controlled skill result (success one-step + payment, critical two-step heal, partial/fail are dark to `credit_flow`, fumble tagged), the debit-abort emits nothing, no-pending-offer / healer-left-room emit nothing, `telemetry.medical_sample=0.0` suppresses the event but not the heal-or-payment, fail-open under a raising sink, the helper schema + None-id coercion, and the seam/tunable source pins.

### 2026-06-23 — T3.19 telemetry breadth: entertainer (cantina) credit faucet — *drop t319-entertainer-telemetry* (Opus loop)
The entertainer perform loop (`parser/entertainer_commands.py`) is a cantina credit **faucet** — `perform` pays the social character via `adjust_credits(..., "entertainer")` — but that lone `credit_flow` row can't be rejoined offline into the depth that tunes the faucet: the **outcome split** (critical / success / partial / fail), the **fatigue-bumped difficulty** + `fatigue_count` (the diminishing-returns curve, design SRB.2), the **live-audience hub bonus** (A8 audience-weighting), or the **cantina-brawl 2x** flag. A partial-pay perform (25cr) and a flat-pay perform can log the same `entertainer` delta, so the outcome split is invisible without a dedicated emit — the direct signal for tuning the pay bounds (`_BASE_PAY`/`_CRIT_PAY`/`_PARTIAL_PAY`), the audience cap, the fatigue penalty, and the perform cooldowns. This drop adds ONE fail-open, sample-tunable `entertainer_perform` event at the post-dispatch seam (after the success/partial/fail branches resolve), carrying a coerced `char_id`, the `outcome`, the `payout` (matches the ledger leg exactly; 0 on fail), the `skill` used (persuasion vs musical instrument), `margin`, the fatigue-effective `difficulty`, `fatigue_count`, `audience` heads, and the `brawl`/`fumble` flags. Three branch accumulators (`perform_outcome`/`perform_payout`/`perform_audience`, defaulting to the fail outcome) feed a single emit, mirroring the sabacc `gamble` pattern. Buffer-only + offline-flushed → zero gameplay behaviour; the emit is wrapped fail-open so a broken sink can never disturb the performance it observes. Sampling honours new `telemetry.entertainer_sample` (default 1.0; documented in `data/tunables.yaml`). Observe-only: no engine logic change, no schema, no faucet/sink, era-clean. 15 tests (`tests/test_t319_entertainer_telemetry.py`): the four outcome branches driven end-to-end against a recording stub DB, payout-matches-ledger cross-check, audience lift recorded, brawl 2x recorded, fatigue→difficulty recorded, musical-instrument skill recorded, `telemetry.entertainer_sample` honoured (0.0 suppresses the event but not the payout), fail-open under a raising sink, and the early-return zone-gate / cooldown paths emit nothing.

### 2026-06-23 -- REVIEW-FIX: post-gate adjudication of the fork+multihome batch -- *drop sidebar-contract-handoff-capture*
Four fixes from the parallel invariant-auditor + code-reviewer pass and the threaded full-suite gate (13344 passed / 7 failed; the 7 triaged to pre-existing baseline-reds + these mine):
- **SPACE anomaly engage fail-OPEN -> fail-CLOSED (BLOCKER-class, both reviewers + `test_session38`):** `_engage_anomaly` had `success = (result is None) or ...`, so a skill-check EXCEPTION (`result=None`) granted a free credit award -- wrong direction for a faucet. Now `success = (result is not None) and result.success and not result.fumble` (an engine error blocks the payout + logs). The two `except Exception: pass` swallows in the same method are now `log.warning`/`log.debug` (clears the silent-except invariant).
- **HOUSING checkout_room recall-pointer corruption (MAJOR, code-reviewer):** the trailing unconditional `UPDATE characters SET home_room_id = NULL WHERE id = ?` ran after every checkout -- harmless single-home, but with multi-home now live, selling home #2 wiped a recall pointer aimed at home #1. Removed; the CONDITIONAL clear earlier in the function (`... WHERE home_room_id IN (deleted rooms)`) already covers the FK case correctly.
- **HOUSING get_homes phantom producer (MEDIUM, invariant-auditor):** `get_homes()` had only a test consumer. Wired the real consumer -- a new `housing list` subcommand (`_cmd_list`) that lists every home you own (up to 4), marking the recall home + the one you're standing in, so a multi-home owner can see what the location-aware management commands will act on.
